Result: TraceViT在ARC-AGI-1上达到67.8% pass@2，在ARC-AGI-2上达到24.3% pass@2。受控消融实验表明，轨迹监督只有在与grounding机制配对时才体现出增益。

Conclusion: 通过逐步分解变换并配合接地机制，能够显著提升循环视觉推理器在抽象推理任务上的表现，为后续工作在中间阶段监督设计上提供了可行方向。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) tests whether a model can infer an unseen transformation from a few input-output examples and apply it to a new grid. Looped visual reasoners refine predictions over multiple iterations, but conventional training constrains only the final output, leaving intermediate refinements unconstrained. We propose that these refinements should instead follow the transformation step by step. We introduce TraceViT, a looped visual reasoner trained with semantically monotonic transformation chains. We obtain these chains by rewriting and verifying programmatic task implementations, decomposing each solution into intermediate grid states. Each iteration is grounded by a task reference derived from the few-shot demonstrations and an object workspace representing the current grid state. Because these chains may differ in length from the loop, soft trace alignment enforces only their ordering, letting the model allocate iterations freely. TraceViT achieves 67.8% pass@2 on ARC-AGI-1 and 24.3% on ARC-AGI-2. Controlled ablations on ARC-AGI-1 show that trace supervision becomes beneficial only when paired with grounding. Code and data will be available at https://github.com/LiuBinnan/TraceViT.

</details>


### [68] [TOOD: Task-Aware Out-of-Distribution Score Calibration for Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29592)
*Mostafa ElAraby,Samer B. Nashed,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习(CL)系统中的分布外(OOD)检测问题，发现OOD检测性能随时间退化的现象（称为OOD遗忘，OODF），并提出了TOOD这一无需训练的后处理方法来缓解该退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统不仅要学习新任务并保持旧任务性能，还需要能够区分分布外输入。然而，OOD检测在CL系统中的退化机制尚不明确，论文旨在揭示OOD遗忘的成因并提出缓解策略。

Method: 首先通过实验揭示两个关键机制：能量型检测器面临"置信度间隙"(Confidence Gap)——随任务增加logit尺度下降；特征型检测器遭受"流形拥挤"(Manifold Crowding)。基于这些观察，提出TOOD，一种无需训练的post-hoc方法，将logits分解为逐任务能量分数，并利用回放缓冲区(replay-buffer)的统计信息进行重新校准。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和100任务ImageNet-1K流上，TOOD在大多数设置下优于未校准的能量方法，在十个CIFAR配置中排名第一或第二，且在置信度间隙最严重时提升最大。

Conclusion: 持续学习中OOD性能的退化很大程度上源于分数校准偏差而非判别结构信息的完全丢失，OOD遗忘与分类遗忘仅弱相关，表明二者由不同的潜在机制驱动。

Abstract: The primary challenge of continual learning (CL) systems is to learn new tasks while remaining performant on previously learned tasks. A similarly important though less well-studied aspect of CL systems is their ability to distinguish inputs that are unlikely to come from within the set of tasks the system has already encountered, often called out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This paper presents several findings related to the dynamics of OOD detection in CL systems, causes of performance degradation over time which we call OOD forgetting (OODF), and proposed mitigation strategies for this degradation. Chiefly, we find the unintuitive result that OODF is only weakly anti-correlated with classification performance on previous tasks, suggesting that the underlying mechanisms producing OODF are distinct. Moreover, this effect is observed for both energy-based and feature-based OOD detection methods. Energy-based detectors suffer a drop in logit scale as additional tasks are learned, which we term the Confidence Gap, while feature-based detectors also degrade under a complementary effect we call Manifold Crowding. Motivated by these observations, we propose TOOD, a training-free post-hoc method that decomposes logits into per-task energy scores and re-calibrates them using replay-buffer statistics. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a 100-task ImageNet-1K stream show that TOOD improves OOD detection performance over uncalibrated energy in most settings and ranks first or second in nine of ten CIFAR configurations, with the largest gains when the confidence gap is most severe. These results suggest that a substantial portion of OOD deterioration in continual learning arises from score miscalibration rather than from a complete loss of discriminative structure.

</details>


### [69] [CoDe-SSM: Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model for Efficient UHD Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29595)
*Jiaxu Su,Zhijian Wu,Jun Li,Bo Zhang,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDe-SSM（上下文-细节解耦状态空间模型），通过将UHD图像恢复中的全局上下文聚合与局部细节保留分离到两条独立路径，实现高效且高质量的UHD图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高清（UHD）图像恢复需要平衡空间重复退化线索的聚合与局部图像结构的保留。现有方法通过降采样、窗口划分或基于聚类的令牌减少来管理UHD恢复成本，但大多未显式保留共享聚合难以表达的信息，导致边缘、纹理等细粒度结构信息受损。

Method: 提出CoDe-SSM模型，包含两条解耦路径：(1) 上下文建模路径——Global Cluster Scan Module (GCSM) 将特征聚合成K个输入相关的簇中心，并在固定顺序序列上应用选择性SSM推理，实现跨区域上下文共享并解耦计算成本与空间分辨率；(2) 细节恢复路径——Local High-Frequency Module (LHFM) 使用输入导出的高频掩码和稀疏混合卷积专家处理聚类残差，保留局部高频细节。

Result: 在五个UHD基准和五种退化类型上进行了大量实验，显式的上下文-细节解耦策略在保持理想效率的同时，显著提升了恢复质量。

Conclusion: 上下文-细节显式解耦是提升UHD图像恢复质量同时保持效率的有效策略，CoDe-SSM在多项基准上验证了该策略的优越性。

Abstract: Ultra-high-definition (UHD) image restoration must balance the aggregation of spatially recurring degradation cues with the preservation of localized image structures. Compact aggregation can reduce redundant processing but may attenuate edges, textures, and other fine structures. Existing approaches manage UHD restoration cost through downsampling, window partitioning, or cluster-based token reduction; yet many of them do not explicitly retain information that is poorly represented by shared aggregation. In this study, we propose a Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model (CoDe-SSM) for UHD restoration, which processes aggregated context and clustering residuals in separate pathways. The context modeling pathway, implemented by the Global Cluster Scan Module (GCSM), aggregates features into $K$ input-dependent cluster centers and applies selective SSM reasoning over the resulting fixed-order sequence, enabling cross-region context sharing while decoupling computational cost from spatial resolution. The detail recovery pathway, implemented by the Local High-Frequency Module (LHFM), processes the clustering residual with an input-derived high-frequency mask and a sparse mixture of convolutional experts. Extensive experiments on five UHD benchmarks and five degradation types demonstrate that our explicit context-detail decoupling strategy yields substantial gains in restoration quality while maintaining desirable efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [FlexComposer: Unified Video Compositing from Images to Dynamic Footage with Flexible Trajectory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29627)
*Songchun Zhang,Sitong Guo,Xianghao Kong,Pengwei Liu,Yuwei Guo,Lvmin Zhang,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexComposer是一个统一的视频合成框架，将视频合成标准化为轨迹引导的条件生成任务，通过规范前景表示、空间感知潜在注入和混合数据集训练等设计，实现静态图像和动态视频的灵活合成，在视觉质量、时间一致性和轨迹贴合上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式视频合成方法存在控制保真度权衡：要么从静态图像中臆造运动，无法保留预动画资产的动态；要么缺乏细粒度的空间控制来实现沿用户定义轨迹的精确资产放置。

Method: FlexComposer提出三个关键设计：(1)统一规范前景表示，将物体的内在运动与全局位移解耦，将异构输入标准化到稳定居中的潜空间；(2)空间感知潜在注入策略，利用VAE潜空间的平移等变性，通过无参数机制将规范特征迁移到目标轨迹上；(3)混合数据集与合成到真实课程训练，结合过程模拟、真实电影素材和生成数据，隐式学习物理合理的照明与阴影协调。

Result: 大量实验表明FlexComposer在视觉质量、时间一致性和轨迹贴合度上均优于现有最先进方法，能处理从产品照片到动态主体的多样化输入，实现高保真运动控制和环境融合。

Conclusion: FlexComposer通过统一的框架设计解决了视频合成中控制保真度的权衡问题，无需显式3D重建或额外的可学习适配器，即可实现高质量的视频合成。

Abstract: Generative video compositing, which involves inserting external assets seamlessly into existing video sequences, is essential for content creation and visual effects. However, existing approaches suffer from a control-fidelity trade-off: they either hallucinate motion from static images, failing to preserve the dynamics of pre-animated assets, or lack fine-grained spatial control for precise asset placement along user-defined trajectories. We propose FlexComposer, a unified framework that standardizes video compositing as a trajectory-guided conditional generation task, enabling the seamless integration of both static images and dynamic footage. Our approach introduces three key designs: (1) a Unified Canonical Foreground Representation that decouples an object's intrinsic motion from its global displacement, standardizing heterogeneous inputs into a stabilized, centered latent space; (2) a Spatial-Aware Latent Injection strategy that exploits the translation equivariance of VAE latent spaces to transport canonical features onto target trajectories via a parameter-free mechanism; and (3) a Hybrid Dataset and Synthetic-to-Real Curriculum that synergizes procedural simulation, real-world cinematic footage, and generative data to implicitly learn physically plausible illumination and shadow harmonization. This unified design handles diverse inputs from product photos to dynamic subjects achieving high-fidelity motion control and environmental integration without the need for explicit 3D reconstruction or auxiliary learnable adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlexComposer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual quality, temporal consistency, and trajectory adherence.

</details>


### [71] [OASIS: Occlusion-aware Single-image Hand Avatar Reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29633)
*Zhisheng Han,Shiyao Wu,Jiayan Qiu,Yakun Ju,Lu Liu,Le Zhang,Pengfei Feng,Huiyu Zhou,Zheheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OASIS是一个面向单张图像3D手部虚拟人生成的新型3D高斯溅射框架，通过几何对齐的视觉证据令牌、可见性条件点-图像注意力以及Feature-on-Mesh表示，实现高效且忠实的手部重建，在视觉保真度和效率上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张图像3D手部虚拟人重建本质上是病态问题，面临严重自遮挡下视觉证据有限和高度关节化手部的复杂姿势依赖形变等挑战。现有方法主要依赖隐式NeRF风格表示，其体积拟合计算开销大且难以保持手部的细粒度细节。

Method: (1) 构建几何对齐的视觉证据令牌：显式地将输入图像观测与3D手部几何对齐，并通过上下文自适应分词处理视觉证据；(2) 提出可见性条件点-图像注意力：根据可见性可靠地将视觉证据传递到几何令牌，生成遮挡感知的高斯特征；(3) 引入Feature-on-Mesh表示：通过局部表面拉伸指导高斯变形，捕捉手部的非刚性形变；(4) 采用一次性适应方案：从多身份训练数据学习共享手部先验，再拟合到目标图像进行特定重建。

Result: 大量实验表明，OASIS在挑战性姿势和真实场景中，视觉保真度和效率均优于现有基线，并展示了在文本到虚拟人生成和纹理编辑等下游任务中的强通用性。

Conclusion: 通过显式几何对齐、可见性感知的特征传递和网格引导的形变建模，OASIS有效解决了单图手部重建中的关键难题，在保证高效性的同时实现了忠实且鲁棒的3D手部虚拟人重建，并具备良好的下游应用拓展能力。

Abstract: Single-image 3D hand avatar reconstruction is fundamentally ill-posed and particularly challenging due to limited visual evidence under severe self-occlusion and the complex pose-dependent deformation of highly articulated hands. Existing methods predominantly rely on implicit NeRF-style representations, whose volumetric fitting is computationally expensive and often struggles to preserve fine-grained hand details. In this work, we present OASIS, a tailored 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for single-image hand avatar reconstruction. To faithfully encode sparse image-specific appearance cues in single-view reconstruction, we construct geometry-aligned visual evidence tokens by explicitly aligning input image observations with 3D hand geometry and context-adaptively tokenizing the resulting visual evidence. Since severe self-occlusion makes the reliability of image evidence inherently visibility-dependent, we introduce a visibility-conditioned point-image attention to reliably transfer visual evidence to geometric tokens, yielding occlusion-aware Gaussian features for faithful and robust reconstruction. To further capture non-rigid deformation of articulated hands, we introduce a Feature-on-Mesh representation to enable Gaussian deformation to be guided by local surface stretching. Under this framework, we adopt a one-shot adaptation scheme that learns a shared hand prior from multi-identity training data and then fits it to a target image for target-specific reconstruction. Extensive experiments show that OASIS outperforms existing baselines in both visual fidelity and efficiency across challenging poses and in-the-wild scenarios, and further demonstrates strong versatility in downstream applications such as text-to-avatar generation and texture editing.

</details>


### [72] [CodeShrink: Adaptive Visual Compression for Efficient Multimodal Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29637)
*Wenxin Tang,Jingyu Xiao,Zhenyu Liu,Zipeng Xie,Junliang Liu,Wang Luo,Yuan Jiang,Yintong Huo,Michael Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeShrink是一个自适应视觉压缩框架，通过空白消除渲染、基于强化学习的自适应压缩配置和指令感知的视觉token剪枝，将源代码图像输入MLLM的视觉token使用量最多减少71.2%，且性能匹配或超过未压缩的文本输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将源代码渲染为图像输入多模态大语言模型（MLLM）虽然能降低输入成本，但仅调整图像分辨率存在两个低效来源：一是换行和缩进产生的空白区域浪费视觉token，二是与当前指令无关的代码区域被无谓编码。此外，最优压缩设置因输入、任务和模型而异，固定比例策略难以适用。

Method: CodeShrink包含三个组件：(1) Blank-Free Rendering，用紧凑布局和显式结构标记替代依赖空白的布局，消除布局引起的视觉token；(2) Adaptive Compression Configuration，用强化学习训练的轻量级agent为每个输入预测token效率与可读性平衡的压缩设置；(3) Dominant Token Selection，推理时联合分析指令和代码图像，剪除与任务无关的视觉token。

Result: 在代码问答、克隆检测和代码补全三个任务上，CodeShrink将视觉token使用量减少多达71.2%，同时匹配或超过未压缩的纯文本输入的性能，并在所有三个任务上一致优于基于文本和视觉压缩的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合布局压缩、自适应配置和指令感知剪枝可以显著提升多模态代码理解的效率。该工作证明了自适应的视觉压缩策略在代码场景下的有效性，并开源了实现代码。

Abstract: Rendering source code as images offers a promising way to reduce the input costs of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Adjusting image resolution can trade visual token cost against content fidelity. However, resolution scaling alone overlooks two sources of inefficiency: blank regions created by line breaks and indentation, and code regions irrelevant to the current instruction. Moreover, the best compression setting varies across inputs, tasks, and models, limiting fixed-ratio strategies. We propose CodeShrink, an adaptive visual compression framework with three components. Blank-Free Rendering replaces whitespace-dependent layouts with compact layouts and explicit structural markers, removing layout-induced tokens. Adaptive Compression Configuration uses a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to predict a per-input setting that balances token efficiency and readability. Dominant Token Selection jointly analyzes the instruction and code image to prune task-irrelevant visual tokens during inference. We evaluate CodeShrink on code question answering, clone detection, and code completion. CodeShrink reduces visual token use by up to 71.2\% while matching or exceeding uncompressed text-only inputs, and consistently outperforms text-based and visual compression baselines across all three tasks. These results show that combining layout compaction, adaptive configuration, and instruction-aware pruning can make multimodal code understanding more efficient. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/CodeShrink.

</details>


### [73] [HierDoc: Hierarchical Page-to-Region Evidence Routing for Long-Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29638)
*Rongjian Gu,Wengang Zhou,Junyu Xiong,Yonghui Wang,Bing Yin,Bei Wang,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HierDoc是一个层级证据路由框架，将长文档证据获取建模为从页面到区域的两阶段集合预测，通过阶段式GRPO优化页面策略和区域策略，在LongDocURL上相对最强开源基线提升16.87%，证明粗粒度页面路由与细粒度区域路由可作为统一证据获取流程中连续、独立优化的阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多页文档VQA方法在页面级和区域级证据定位上存在割裂：页面中心方法将区域操作仅作为导航辅助，区域中心方法假设相关页面已给定，导致页面选择与区域选择未形成连续的证据决策链条。

Method: 提出HierDoc层级证据路由框架：(1) 将长文档证据获取形式化为从页面到区域的两阶段集合预测；(2) 页面策略从全文档中选择证据页面，解析这些页面的语义元素后由区域策略选择元素传给下游答案模型；(3) 两个answer-agnostic策略使用阶段式GRPO和粒度特定的结构化集合奖励进行优化；(4) 答案模型同时接收选中的完整页面、区域裁剪及OCR/表格文本，保留全局上下文同时强调细粒度证据。

Result: 在多个基准上达到开源系统中的最优或具有竞争力性能，LongDocURL相对最强开源基线提升16.87%；受控消融实验显示，加入选中的区域证据使仅页面的系统在准确率和F1上分别提升5.51%和4.82%。

Conclusion: 将粗粒度页面路由与细粒度区域路由组织为统一证据获取流程中连续、独立优化的阶段是有益的，能够显著提升多页文档VQA的证据定位性能。

Abstract: Multi-page document visual question answering requires locating sparse evidence at both the page and region levels. Existing approaches typically emphasize one level over the other: page-centric methods focus on page acquisition, with region operations serving mainly as navigation aids, whereas region-centric methods assume that the relevant pages have already been supplied. Consequently, page and region selection remain disconnected rather than forming successive evidence decisions. We propose HierDoc, a hierarchical evidence-routing framework that formulates long-document evidence acquisition as two-stage set prediction from pages to regions. A page policy selects evidence pages from the full document; these pages are then parsed for semantic elements, after which a region policy selects the elements passed to a downstream answer model. Both answer-agnostic policies are optimized with stage-wise GRPO using granularity-specific structured-set rewards. The answer model receives selected full pages together with selected region crops and OCR or table text, preserving global context while emphasizing fine-grained evidence. Across the evaluated benchmarks, HierDoc achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance among open-weight systems, improving LongDocURL by 16.87% relative to the strongest reported open-weight baseline. Controlled ablations further show that selected regional evidence improves the page-only system in accuracy and F1 by 5.51% and 4.82%, respectively. These results demonstrate the benefit of organizing coarse page routing and fine-grained region routing as successive, separately optimized stages of a unified evidence-acquisition process.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling Properties of Text Conditioning in Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29679)
*Zilong Chen,Chaorui Deng,Kunchang Li,Hongyi Yuan,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉生成中文本条件化的经验缩放性质，发现扩散损失随提示中结构化语言的数量呈规律性缩放（与GPG线性、与ED幂律），并据此通过结构化提示构建和提示器训练（SFT、冷启动、验证器门控策略蒸馏）显著提升生成系统的组合性、推理和世界知识能力，超越所有评估的开源模型并在多数基准上匹敌或超越最强闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为扩散损失不随自然语言提示的token数量缩放，导致文本条件的缩放性质很少被量化测度。作者希望探究扩散损失与提示结构化语言量之间的经验缩放规律，并利用这些规律指导提升视觉生成的文本可控性（diffusability）和提示响应性（promptability）。

Method: 1) 提出两个互补的结构化语言度量：白盒似然度量GPG和黑盒属性度量ED；2) 在受控训练运行中测量收敛扩散损失与这两种度量的缩放关系（线性vs幂律）；3) 基于缩放规律，通过语义和几何标注构造结构化提示提升diffusability；4) 通过监督微调、冷启动和验证器门控的在线策略蒸馏训练提示器提升promptability。

Result: 收敛扩散损失随GPG近似线性下降，随ED遵循幂律下降。基于这些规律构建的系统在几乎所有组合性、推理和世界知识基准上超越所有评估的开源权重模型，并在大多数评估上匹敌或超越最强的闭源权重模型。

Conclusion: 扩散损失确实随提示中结构化语言的量呈现可度量的缩放性质，这一发现可用于指导设计更优的提示策略和提示器训练方法，从本质上提升文本到图像生成的组合推理能力。

Abstract: We study empirical scaling properties for text conditioning in visual generation. Such properties have rarely been measured because diffusion loss does not scale with the number of tokens in natural-language prompts. Surprisingly, we find that the converged diffusion loss scales with the amount of structured language in the prompt. To quantify structured language, we adapt two complementary measures: a white-box likelihood metric (GPG) and a black-box attribute metric (ED). Across controlled training runs, the converged diffusion loss decreases approximately linearly with GPG and follows a power law with ED. Guided by these scaling properties, we improve \emph{diffusability} by constructing structured prompts with semantic and geometric annotations derived from images, and improve \emph{promptability} by training a prompter through supervised fine-tuning, cold-start, and verifier-gated on-policy distillation. The resulting system outperforms all evaluated open-weight models on nearly every compositional, reasoning, and world-knowledge benchmark, while matching or surpassing the strongest closed-weight models on most evaluations.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Robust and 3D-Aware RGB-NIR Imaging in the Dark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29684)
*Muyao Niu,Mingze Ma,Yifan Zhan,Qingtian Zhu,Zhihang Zhong,Wei Guo,Chang Wen Chen,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需干净RGB标签的3D感知神经建模方法，用于RGB-NIR低光图像增强，通过隐式融合极噪声RGB观测与NIR线索于3D空间中，有效恢复干净RGB图像，并能在不同噪声水平下泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-NIR低光融合方法依赖精心标注的训练数据对，在不同场景下鲁棒性有限。本文希望摆脱对干净RGB监督数据的依赖，提高模型在不同噪声水平下的泛化能力。

Method: 引入3D感知神经建模，无需干净RGB监督，通过在3D空间中隐式融合极噪声RGB观测与NIR线索来优化模型，从而重建干净RGB图像。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的广泛评估均证明了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该方法消除了对干净RGB数据采集的需求，并能跨不同噪声水平泛化，为RGB-NIR低光成像提供了新视角。

Abstract: Robust low-light imaging remains challenging for the community. Recent studies have explored fusing Near-Infrared (NIR) with noisy RGB to achieve improved enhancement, yet most methods depend on carefully curated training data pairs, with limited robustness under different scenarios. This paper offers a new perspective for RGB-NIR low-light imaging by incorporating 3D-aware neural modeling. Without using clean RGB supervision, a powerful model can be optimized to implicitly fuse extremely noisy RGB observations with NIR cues in 3D space, effectively recovering clean RGB images. The proposed model obviates the requirement for clean RGB data collection, generalizes across different noise levels. Extensive evaluations on synthetic and real data demonstrate its superiority. Codes available: https://github.com/MyNiuuu/3DarkFusion

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Can LLMs Really Understand Item Difficulty Levels? Implications for Automated Item Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28634)
*Xinyi Wang,Hong Jiao,Ming Li,Sydney Peters,Hanna Choi,Tianyi Zhou,Qingshu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在预测大规模阅读与写作测试中题目难度等级方面的表现。零样本GPT-4.1（温度=0）取得了LLM中最高的预测准确率（QWK=0.578），但仍低于ConvBERT（QWK=0.625）和最佳基于特征的监督机器学习模型。研究发现所有LLM都难以标记高难度题目，GPT-5.4倾向于低估难度，且仅凭语义信息不足以预测难度等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 题目难度估计在形成性评价和大规模高风险终结性评价中均具有关键作用。随着LLM在教育领域的广泛应用，探究LLM是否能够准确预测题目难度等级，对于其实际应用（特别是自动生成具有目标难度的题目）具有重要的实践和理论意义。

Method: 研究采用大规模阅读与写作测试中的真实题目数据，在多个LLM上系统考察了不同提示策略和参数设置，并将LLM的性能与encoder-only语言模型（如ConvBERT）以及基于特征的监督机器学习模型进行对比。此外，还通过嵌入维度约简分析语义信息与难度等级之间的关系。

Result: 零样本GPT-4.1（温度=0）获得最高的LLM难度预测准确率（QWK=0.578），但低于ConvBERT（QWK=0.625）和最佳基于特征的监督机器学习模型。所有LLM均难以正确标记高难度题目，GPT-5.4系统性地低估题目难度。嵌入维度约简显示不同难度等级的题目嵌入相互混杂，说明仅靠语义信息不足以预测题目难度。

Conclusion: LLM在预测题目难度方面的能力尚不及专用编码器和传统监督学习方法，且对高难度题目的判断存在系统性偏差。当LLM自身能力增强时倾向于将大多数题目视为简单，因此在利用LLM生成具有目标难度题目的场景中须保持谨慎。

Abstract: The estimation of item difficulty plays a key role in both formative assessment and large-scale high-stakes summative assessments. This study explores how large language models (LLMs) perform in predicting item difficulty levels using items from a large-scale Reading and Writing test. The study investigated various prompting strategies and parameter settings across multiple LLMs. LLM performance was compared with encoder-only language models and feature-based supervised machine learning models. Zero-shot GPT-4.1 with a temperature of 0 yielded the highest item difficulty level prediction accuracy, with a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.578. However, LLMs' prediction accuracy was lower than that of ConvBERT (QWK = 0.625), which outperformed the best feature-based supervised machine learning model. Further analysis showed that all LLMs struggled to label hard items; in particular, the current advanced GPT-5.4 tended to underestimate item difficulty levels. Dimension reduction of embeddings showed that item embeddings from different difficulty levels were mixed together, indicating that semantic information from items alone is likely insufficient for item difficulty level prediction. The findings suggest that if LLMs cannot understand item difficulty levels as evidenced by empirical data and tend to treat most items as easy when their own capabilities increase, caution should be exercised when using LLMs to generate items with targeted difficulty levels.

</details>


### [77] [Imbalanced Data Clustering via Targeted Data Augmentation Using GMM and LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28635)
*Noor Khalal,Abdallah Alaa-Eddine Djamai,Imed Keraghel,Mohamed Nadif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合GMM与LLM的无监督数据增强方法，通过GMM检测数据中代表性不足的簇，并利用LLM生成合成文档来增强这些簇，从而提升不平衡文本数据在无监督NLP任务中的表示质量与聚类可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NLP中处理代表性不足的话题具有挑战性，尤其在无监督任务中，聚类可能无法充分捕捉少数派主题，导致这些群体在数据表示中被忽视。

Method: 方法整合高斯混合模型（GMM）与大语言模型（LLM）：GMM利用其灵活性和鲁棒性检测数据中对应代表性不足区域的聚类，LLM则生成合成文档来丰富这些聚类并改善其表示。

Result: 在多个不平衡文本数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有情况下均保持了聚类性能，并常常增强聚类可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为改善无监督NLP任务中的数据表示提供了稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In Natural Language Processing (NLP), dealing with underrepresented topics is challenging, especially in unsupervised tasks where clustering might not adequately capture minority topics. To tackle this challenge, our paper presents a novel unsupervised data augmentation method that integrates Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs). Due to their flexibility and robustness, GMMs can detect clusters corresponding to underrepresented areas in the data, while LLMs create synthetic documents to enrich these clusters and improve their representation. Experiments on various imbalanced text datasets demonstrate that our approach preserves clustering performance in all cases and often enhances cluster interpretability, offering a robust and scalable solution for improving data representation in unsupervised NLP tasks.

</details>


### [78] [Chain-of-Models: Cross-Model Auditing for Bias-Robust LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28636)
*Qian Wang,Zhanzhi Lou,Zhenheng Tang,Nuo Chen,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Models（CoM）自动化审计流水线，由第二模型检查第一模型的推理轨迹后产生最终判断。研究发现审计者身份影响重大：独立偏差抵抗能力不预测审计效果，且最佳审计者因偏差类型而异。据此提出按偏差类型选择审计者的规则，在四个偏差切片上达到0.884的准确率，优于固定最强审计者（0.824）和无审计基线（0.805）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动化评判者时其判断容易受到认知偏差影响。现有缓解方法主要依赖提示驱动的去偏（跨偏差类型脆弱）或人工评估（无法规模化）。因此需要一种自动化的、可扩展的审计机制来缓解评判者的认知偏差，同时要解决审计者身份选择这一关键设计问题。

Method: 使用9个来自6个家族的模型，覆盖4种认知偏差（从众、权威、分心、奉承）和4个事实数据集。设计CoM流水线：第二模型检查第一模型的推理轨迹后给出最终判断。系统比较同模型、同家族模型、跨家族模型三种审计者配置，并基于功能多样性、按偏差的独立抵抗力和校准的审计有效性，构建按偏差类型选择审计者的规则。使用校准/测试划分进行验证。

Result: 发现两种关键现象：1）独立偏差抵抗能力不预测审计效果（Kimi-K2.5独立偏差抵抗最强，但对Qwen2.5-72B的偏差轨迹审计效果弱）；2）最佳审计者因偏差类型而异（GPT-4o对从众、权威、分心最强，GLM-5对奉承最强）。所提出的按偏差类型选择审计者的选择器在四个偏差切片上达到0.884的准确率，超越最强单一固定审计者（0.824）和无审计基线（0.805）。

Conclusion: 审计者身份是CoM流水线有效的关键维度，不存在通用的最佳审计者。偏差类型感知的审计者选择规则能显著提升LLM评判的可靠性，表明需要针对具体偏差类型进行审计者定制化选择。作者已公开发布数据、配置和LLM-agent技能以促进可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly serve as automated judges, but their judgments remain vulnerable to cognitive biases. Existing mitigations mostly rely on prompt-driven debiasing, which is brittle across bias types, or human evaluation, which does not scale. We study \emph{Chain-of-Models} (CoM), an automated audit pipeline in which a second model inspects the first model's reasoning trace before producing the final judgment. The key design question is whether the auditor should be the same model, a same-family model, or a different-family model. Across 9 models from 6 families, 4 cognitive biases, and 4 factual datasets, we find that auditor identity matters in two ways. First, standalone bias resistance does not predict audit effectiveness: Kimi-K2.5 is the strongest standalone model on several biases, yet is a weak auditor for Qwen2.5-72B's biased traces. Second, the best auditor is bias-specific: GPT-4o is strongest on bandwagon, authority, and distraction, while GLM-5 is strongest on sycophancy. We operationalize these findings with a per-bias auditor selection rule that, given the bias type, scores candidates along functional diversity, per-bias standalone resistance, and calibrated audit effectiveness. Under a calibration/test split, the selector reaches the highest accuracy across the four biased slices ($0.884$ vs.\ $0.824$ for the strongest single fixed auditor and $0.805$ for the no-audit baseline). We release data, configurations, and an LLM-agent skill at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/chain-of-models-B585 .

</details>


### [79] [ZeroR@CHiPSAL 2026: Two-Stage Vision-Language Adaptation with Contrastive Learning for Nepali Meme Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28637)
*Nitiz Khanal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct的多模态仇恨言论检测与情感分析系统，应用于CHiPSAL 2026共享任务中的尼泊尔语模因数据。系统采用两阶段训练管道（LoRA微调生成式分类与前对比骨干微调），通过集成分数实现最终预测，在仇恨言论检测中取得第二名（F1: 0.797），情感分析中取得第四名（F1: 0.518）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔语作为低资源南亚语言，其多模态仇恨言论与情感检测面临OCR误差传播和翻译管道失败的挑战。现有方法依赖分离的OCR和翻译流程，效率低且易出错，亟需利用具备原生Devanagari支持的视觉-语言模型进行端到端建模。

Method: 采用两阶段训练管道：(1) LoRA微调配合MLP投影头进行生成式分类；(2) 对比骨干微调配合监督InfoNCE损失。通过少数类过采样、图像增强和焦点损失处理类别不平衡问题。推理时结合阶段1的token概率和阶段2的分类器分数，使用验证集调优的权重进行集成。

Result: 在仇恨言论检测任务中取得第二名（F1: 0.797），在情感分析任务中取得第四名（F1: 0.518），提供了详细的消融实验和错误分析。

Conclusion: 端到端方法避免了分离OCR和翻译管道带来的错误传播，验证了Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在低资源南亚语言多模态理解任务中的有效性，为大型视觉-语言模型在低资源语言场景中的适配提供了见解。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for the CHiPSAL 2026 shared task on multimodal hate speech and sentiment detection in Nepali memes. We address both subtasks: binary hate speech classification and three-class sentiment analysis. Our approach adapts the Robust Adaptation of Hateful Meme Detection (RA-HMD) framework using Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, a state-of-the-art vision-language model with native Devanagari support. We employ a two-stage training pipeline: (1) LoRA fine-tuning with an MLP projection head for generative classification, and (2) contrastive backbone fine-tuning with supervised InfoNCE loss. We handle class imbalance through minority oversampling, image augmentation, and focal loss. At inference, we ensemble Stage 1 token probabilities with Stage 2 classifier scores using validation-tuned weights. Our end-to-end approach eliminates error propagation from separate OCR and translation pipelines by leveraging the model's native Devanagari understanding. Our system achieved \textbf{2nd place} on hate speech detection (F1: 0.797) and \textbf{4th place} on sentiment analysis (F1: 0.518). We provide detailed ablations, error analysis, and insights into adapting large vision-language models for low-resource South Asian languages.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Stateful Predictive Knowledge From Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28638)
*Yan Song,Xidong Feng,Bo Liu,Xinyu Cui,Haotian Fu,Zichen Liu,Mengyue Yang,Cheng Deng,Jian Zhao,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出状态知识学习（SKL），一种让LLM智能体从轨迹级反思转向基于状态的状态化预测知识学习的训练范式。通过自蒸馏（SKL-SD）和强化学习（SKL-RL）两种算法，训练智能体自主提取并利用状态锚定的预测性知识，在WebShop、ScienceWorld和ChessPuzzles等任务上显著超越现有基于反思的训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体主要依赖轨迹级反思来学习经验，但从预测性知识视角看，这种方法仅基于外显性经验回顾（episodic hindsight）而非预测性前瞻（predictive foresight），导致学到的启发式规则脆弱且路径依赖，缺乏泛化能力和知识引导能力。

Method: 提出SKL框架：1）将智能体的学习焦点从轨迹级总结转向维护状态化知识（Stateful Knowledge）——显式的、声明式的、锚定于状态的预测性评估；2）通过自蒸馏（SKL-SD）和强化学习（SKL-RL）两种算法实现规模化，训练智能体自主从经验中提取状态锚定的预测性知识，并学习利用这些知识进行策略制定。

Result: 在WebShop、ScienceWorld（交互式环境）和ChessPuzzles（复杂推理任务）上的实验表明，赋予模型学习状态化预测性知识的固有能力显著超越了当前基于反思的训练范式。

Conclusion: 状态化知识学习相较轨迹级反思能够提供更细粒度的知识、增强泛化能力，并支持知识引导式启动（knowledge bootstrapping），是一种更有效的LLM智能体经验学习方式。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents increasingly learn from experience, they primarily rely on trajectory-level reflection to extract insights. Viewed through the lens of predictive knowledge, we argue that this approach operates on episodic hindsight rather than predictive foresight, yielding brittle, path-dependent heuristics. To address this, we propose Stateful Knowledge Learning (SKL). SKL shifts the agent's focus from trajectory-level summarization to maintaining Stateful Knowledge: explicit, declarative predictive assessments anchored to state. We first demonstrate a motivating example showing how stateful knowledge provides granularity, enhances generalization, and enables knowledge bootstrapping. To further scale up the idea, we introduce two algorithms via self-distillation (SKL-SD) and reinforcement learning (SKL-RL), training agents to autonomously extract state-grounded predictive knowledge from experience and learn to leverage it for policy making. Experiments on interactive environments (WebShop, ScienceWorld) and a complex reasoning task (ChessPuzzles) demonstrate that equipping models with the inherent ability to learn stateful predictive knowledge significantly outpaces current reflection-based training paradigms.

</details>


### [81] [The Asymmetric Effects of Knowledge Distillation on Bias in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28639)
*Plawan Kumar Rath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调小模型中的知识蒸馏对偏见存在不对称影响：在无歧义任务上蒸馏改善了上下文遵循，但在歧义任务上蒸馏破坏了逐项拒答校准（silence-loss），导致原本正确拒答的项目被填入刻板印象答案。且silence-loss与filled-silence两种效应在28组配置中不相关（ρ=0.19），暗示源自不同机制。聚合偏见指标会掩盖这些逐项危害。作者提出三步协议PCCD（逐条件校准诊断）来同时评估拒答校准、上下文遵循与能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对知识蒸馏如何影响小模型偏见缺乏细致理解，聚合偏见指标（如CrowS-Pairs、BBQ Stereotype Reliance Score）会平均掉相互抵消的效应，掩盖蒸馏在歧义任务上造成的逐项拒答校准损失这一具体危害。

Method: 在两个学生模型家族（SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct、OLMo-2-1B-Instruct）上进行响应式蒸馏实验，构建28配置网格；引入silence-loss（原本正确拒答转变为刻板印象回答）和filled-silence（填满原本的沉默）两个测量指标；审计四个训练语料库中拒绝形状的占比（<0.5%）；通过SFT注入拒答进行对照实验；提出PCCD三步诊断协议。

Result: 蒸馏将SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct的上下文覆盖错误率从44%降至24%（BBQ-disambig），但BBQ-ambig上15%的原本正确拒答项变为刻板印象回答；OLMo-2-1B-Instruct上silence-loss为8%，filled-silence占新偏见的89%；两个效应相关系数ρ=0.19不显著；SFT拒答注入导致trivial-refuser失效模式（拒答率99.8%、disambig准确率0.2%），而聚合指标将其误判为完美校准。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏对偏见的影响在不同任务类型上是不对称的：改善上下文遵循的同时会破坏歧义任务上的拒答校准，二者源于不同机制。聚合指标不足以评估校准质量，PCCD协议能够捕获聚合评价遗漏的不对称危害与trivial-refuser失效模式。

Abstract: We show that knowledge distillation in small instruction-tuned language models has asymmetric effects on bias. On unambiguous tasks (BBQ-disambig), response-based distillation from a Gemma-2-9B teacher improves context-following: for the most biased baseline (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct), it cuts the context-overriding error rate from 44% to 24%. On ambiguous tasks (BBQ-ambig), the same distillation destroys per-item refusal calibration: 15% of items where the baseline correctly abstained instead receive stereotype answers, even when overall refusal rate is preserved. The pattern reproduces on a second student family (OLMo-2-1B-Instruct), with silence-loss of 8% and filled-silence accounting for 89% of new bias. Across the full 28-configuration grid, the magnitudes of silence-loss and filled-silence are uncorrelated (Spearman $ρ=0.19$, n.s.), indicating that the two effects arise from distinct mechanisms.
  Aggregate stereotype metrics (CrowS-Pairs, overall BBQ Stereotype Reliance Score) average over both effects and conceal the per-item harm. We trace the calibration loss to a data-side mechanism: an audit of four training corpora finds <0.5% refusal-as-answer-shape. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with refusal injection either breaks parsing or over-corrects into a trivial-refuser regime (refusal rate 99.8%, disambig accuracy 0.2%) that aggregate metrics would call perfectly calibrated. We propose Per-Condition Calibration Diagnosis (PCCD), a three-step protocol that evaluates refusal calibration, context-following, and capability preservation. PCCD catches both the asymmetric harm and the trivial-refuser failure mode that aggregate evaluations miss.

</details>


### [82] [TokenSwap: Benchmarking and Reducing the Modality Gap in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28640)
*Andong Hua,Colton Bishop,Igor Mordatch,Arian Hosseini,Jindong Gu,Aleksandra Faust,Rebecca Roelofs,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文定义了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中的"模态差距"（modality gap）——即模型在语义等价但模态不同的输入（纯文本 vs 图文交错）下的性能差异。作者提出TokenSwap方法，将文本概念替换为语义对齐的图像来构建图文交错输入，并基于此构建TokenSwap-Bench基准。在42个MLLM上发现普遍存在模态差距，平均性能下降19.6%；推理模型差距更小（10.1% vs 25.5%），提示策略和训练计算扩展无法可靠缩小差距，而训练时引入TokenSwap可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型应在语义等价的跨模态输入下产生一致响应，但实际中模型在跨模态变化下存在系统性预测差异。现有研究缺乏对这一"模态差距"的系统定义、量化和分析，也没有有效的缓解方法。

Method: 提出TokenSwap方法，将文本中的概念替换为语义对齐的图像，生成图文交错（image-interleaved）输入序列；基于该方法将MMLU等文本基准转化为TokenSwap-Bench图文交错基准；在42个MLLM上进行系统评估，并测试训练时引入TokenSwap的策略。

Result: 在42个MLLM上均观察到模态差距，从文本输入切换到图文交错输入时性能下降4.2%至47.4%，平均19.6%±3.3%。推理模型的差距显著更小（平均10.1%），而非推理模型差距为25.5%。提示策略和扩展训练计算无法可靠缩小差距，但在训练中引入TokenSwap可以有效缓解该问题，同时保持纯文本和视觉语言性能。

Conclusion: 模态差距是MLLM中普遍存在的问题，推理能力对缩小模态差距有重要作用；TokenSwap不仅可用作评估模态差距的基准工具，还可以作为训练数据增强手段来有效缓解该差距，且不损害其他能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should generate consistent responses given semantically equivalent inputs across modalities. However, we observe a systematic discrepancy in model predictions under such cross-modal variations. Specifically, we define the modality gap as the difference in model performance under semantically equivalent textual and multimodal inputs. We introduce TokenSwap, a method that constructs such inputs by replacing textual concepts with semantically aligned images, resulting in sequences where visual tokens are interleaved with text tokens. Based on TokenSwap, we transform existing text-based benchmarks such as MMLU into image-interleaved counterparts, resulting in TokenSwap-Bench. Across 42 MLLMs, we observe a pervasive modality gap, with performance decreasing by 4.2% to 47.4% when moving from text-only to image-interleaved inputs, averaging 19.6% +/- 3.3% across models. Notably, we observe that reasoning models exhibit consistently smaller gaps, achieving an average gap of 10.1% compared to 25.5% for non-reasoning models. In contrast, neither prompting strategies nor scaling training compute alone reliably reduces the modality gap. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating TokenSwap during training effectively mitigates this gap while preserving strong text-only and vision-language performance.

</details>


### [83] [The Formalism Trap: Are LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluators Blinded by Consensus Mimicry under Social Load?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28641)
*Dahlia Shehata,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"代理形式主义陷阱"（Agentic Formalism Trap）和"评估失调指数"（D_E），量化LLM-as-a-Judge系统在对抗性压力下将结构程序主义与语义真理性混为一谈的问题。基于22,500条轨迹（覆盖GAIA、SWE-bench、Multi-Challenge三个领域），提取了幻觉策略的语义分类体系，并通过确定性的词汇构建验证（p<10^-120）；逻辑元评估器定位了评估器被捕获的确切句法触发条件（ROC-AUC 0.8779）；零样本留一域外迁移实验证明该漏洞具有领域无关性（平均ROC-AUC 0.7482）；架构剖析表明不同模拟群体拓扑会产生数学上不同的语义盲区，证明无锚闭环评估不稳定、系统性发散，需要架构特异的警惕性过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-Judge评估系统存在深层次缺陷：它们可能仅凭借结构化、程序化的表面形式（formalism）而非实际语义正确性来评判内容，尤其在对抗性负载下这种混淆会被放大。作者旨在量化并验证这一系统性问题，并揭示其架构敏感性。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 提出Evaluative Dissonance Index (D_E)量化评估失调；(2) 在3个领域收集22,500条轨迹；(3) 通过确定性词汇构建提取幻觉策略语义分类体系；(4) 使用逻辑元评估器定位句法触发条件；(5) 采用Leave-One-Domain-Out零样本迁移测试验证领域无关性；(6) 对不同模拟swarm拓扑进行架构剖析。

Result: 研究发现逻辑元评估器能达到ROC-AUC 0.8779的句法触发定位精度；零样本领域迁移下平均ROC-AUC为0.7482，证明漏洞领域无关；词汇构建验证p<10^-120，统计显著性极强；不同swarm拓扑产生数学上不同的语义盲区，表明无锚闭环评估系统不稳定且系统性发散。

Conclusion: 无锚定（unanchored）的闭环评估系统本质上不稳定且系统性发散，其语义盲区随架构拓扑而变化，因此必须引入架构特异的警惕性过滤器（architecture-specific vigilance filters）来缓解评估器被形式化程序捕获的风险。

Abstract: We introduce the \textit{Agentic Formalism Trap} and the Evaluative Dissonance Index ($D_E$), quantifying how LLM-as-a-Judge systems conflate structural proceduralism with semantic truth under adversarial load. Analyzing 22,500 trajectories across 3 domains (GAIA, SWE-bench, Multi-Challenge), we extract a semantic taxonomy of hallucination maneuvers, validated via deterministic lexical grounding ($p < 10^{-120}$). A logistic meta-evaluator isolates the exact syntactic triggers of this evaluator capture (ROC-AUC 0.8779), while a zero-shot Leave-One-Domain-Out transfer proves the vulnerability is universally domain-agnostic (mean ROC-AUC 0.7482). Architectural profiling reveals that distinct simulated swarm topologies induce mathematically disparate semantic blind spots, proving that unanchored closed-loop evaluation is unstable, systemically divergent and necessitates architecture-specific vigilance filters.

</details>


### [84] [Evaluating Federated Pre-Training: On the Reliability of Downstream Fine-Tuning and Intrinsic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28658)
*Claudia Grosser,Maike Heuer,Denis Krompass,Thomas A. Runkler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦预训练模型评估协议的选择问题，发现在统一预训练测试集上建立的参考排序中，下游微调（GLUE）无法可靠地保持该排序，而直接基于GLUE文本的下一词预测（next-token prediction）与预训练测试困惑度高度相关，更贴近预训练目标的评估信号更能反映联邦预训练质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦预训练使基础模型能在不集中数据的情况下训练，但评估面临挑战：客户端参与差异和本地数据可用性导致难以直接比较；预训练测试困惑度受预训练分布约束，而下游基准引入任务特定适配，可能无法真实反映预训练阶段的困惑度。因此需要研究哪种评估协议能更可靠地反映联邦预训练质量。

Method: 在相同客户端数据上训练16M参数transformer模型的集中式和联邦式版本，以同一预训练测试集上的排序作为参考基准。比较多种评估协议包括：GLUE上的下游微调（全参数微调、仅头部微调、减少数据量微调），以及GLUE文本上的下一词预测作为内在评估信号，检验各协议是否能保留参考排序。

Result: 下游微调无法可靠地保留预训练排序，而直接下一词预测与预训练测试困惑度表现出强烈的一致性。

Conclusion: 仅依赖下游微调来比较联邦预训练模型可能产生误导，评估时应更多关注与原始预训练目标更接近的评估信号。

Abstract: Federated pre-training offers a way to train foundation models on private or distributed data without centralizing the underlying datasets. However, evaluating federated pre-training remains challenging because differences in client participation and local data availability can make directly comparable evaluation difficult. Moreover, pre-training test perplexity is tied to the pre-training distribution, while downstream benchmarks introduce task-specific adaptation that may not faithfully reflect the test perplexity established during pre-training. In this work, we study which evaluation protocol more reliably reflects federated pre-training quality. Using a controlled set of centralized and federated-trained models of a 16M parameter transformer model trained on identical client data, we assess evaluation protocols by whether they preserve a reference ranking established on the same pre-training testset. We compare downstream fine-tuning on GLUE, including full, head-only, and reduced-data variants, with next-token prediction on GLUE text as an intrinsic evaluation signal. Our results show that downstream fine-tuning does not reliably preserve the pre-training ranking, whereas direct next-token prediction exhibits a strong correspondence with the pre-training test perplexity. These findings suggest that downstream fine-tuning alone can be misleading when comparing federated pre-trained models, and that evaluation signals closer to the original pre-training objective deserve greater attention.

</details>


### [85] [Are the Financial Reasoning from LLMs Credible? A Real World Test over Long-Horizon Statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28661)
*Xinke Tong,Xuanming Zhang,Tianyi Tang,An Yang,Jiatu Hu,Guojie Lin,Zhenzhen Shi,Lingfeng Zeng,Boyu Yang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinIndices，一个基于未裁剪财务报表（最长32K tokens）的大规模基准，用于评测LLM在金融领域的结构推理能力。评测发现两大瓶颈：知识瓶颈（移除公式提示后性能急剧下降）和结构瓶颈（多指标多周期表格生成导致推理能力退化）。SFT可以部分恢复结构化逻辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准无法反映真实工业复杂度，多采用选择题或单跳问答，忽略了跨报表动态关系和时序去累积等复杂推理。需要一个更贴近真实场景的基准来检验LLM是否真正具备结构推理能力，而非表面模式匹配。

Method: 构建FinIndices基准，包含Single-Index计算和Table-Index制表两类任务，涵盖复杂领域、时序和量纲推理。采用自动化合成流程并设置对抗陷阱，基于未裁剪财务报告（最长32K tokens）进行评测，并进一步通过SFT验证结构化逻辑的可恢复性。

Result: 评测发现两个严重缺陷：(1)知识瓶颈——移除公式提示后性能大幅下降（如Gemini-3.1-Pro在表格任务上从70.70%跌至38.22%），暴露时序去累积和存量流量量纲不匹配问题；(2)结构瓶颈——多指标多周期表格生成消耗认知负荷，使模型退化为浅层启发式（如错误相邻列、字面算术替代深层会计调整）。SFT带来显著零提示提升（Single +8.54%，Table +3.82%）。

Conclusion: LLM在金融结构推理上仍依赖脆弱的模式匹配，而非真正的结构化理解。知识瓶颈和结构瓶颈共同揭示了当前的局限，但数据驱动的SFT对齐可以部分恢复结构化逻辑能力，为后续改进指明方向。

Abstract: Do Large Language Models (LLMs) possess genuine structural reasoning, or merely rely on surface-level pattern matching? The financial domain, demanding numerical precision and multi-step logic over long contexts, is an ideal testbed. Existing benchmarks fail to capture real-world industrial complexity, predominantly relying on multiple-choice questions or single-hop QA over cropped tables while ignoring intricate cross-statement dynamics and temporal de-cumulation.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce FinIndices, a large-scale benchmark evaluating data-processing fidelity over uncropped financial statements (up to 32K tokens). Utilizing an automated synthesis pipeline with adversarial traps, FinIndices encompasses Single-Index computation and Table-Index tabulation to test complex domain, temporal, and caliber reasoning.
  Our evaluation reveals two severe LLM vulnerabilities. First, a "Knowledge Bottleneck": despite memorizing formulas during pre-training, models demonstrate fragile pattern matching. Removing explicit formula hints causes performance to collapse (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro drops from 70.70% to 38.22% on table tasks), exposing fatal flaws in temporal de-cumulation and stock-flow caliber mismatch. Second, a "Structural Bottleneck": the intense cognitive load of generating multi-metric, multi-period tables actively drains reasoning capacity. Under structural pressure, LLMs that flawlessly execute isolated derivations regress to shallow heuristics, such as fetching incorrect adjacent columns or substituting deep accounting adjustments with lazy literal arithmetic. Finally, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) yields substantial zero-hint gains (+8.54% Single, +3.82% Table), validating that structured logic can be partially restored via data-centric alignment.

</details>


### [86] [The Checking Problem: What must be true before AI ships in a regulated firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28666)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过测量企业级AI工作流在受监管金融服务中的实际表现，发现72个配置中有57个通过演示基准、32个通过生产基准（存活率56.1%），并指出AI工作流的价值更多取决于人类需要复核的比例而非准确率。要求工具引用来源并声明置信度可将复核负担从100%降至49%，而自验证机制虽进一步降至44%却以2.3倍延迟为代价且无法维持错误容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI项目的高失败率被广泛引用但缺乏机制层面的解释。本文旨在通过实验测量AI工作流失败的底层机制，特别是从演示环境到真实生产环境之间的性能衰减，以及人类复核负担这一常被忽视但决定实际价值的因素。

Method: 在受监管金融服务日常执行的六种文档密集型工作流上，跨四个模型家族和三种工具配置各运行三次，生成72个配置的5,093个评分输出元素。每个配置分别对照演示基准（单个案例的单一正确运行）和生产基准（持续准确性、可重复性、可验证归因及有信息量的置信度信号）进行评估。审查负担通过样本外估计计算，而非事后回顾。

Result: 57/72个配置通过演示基准，32个通过生产基准，存活率56.1%。无置信度声明的工具需复核100%输出；要求引用来源并声明置信度可将复核率降至49%，并在20个配置中保持17个的错误容忍度；增加自验证通道需付出2.3倍延迟代价，复核率降至44%，但未能保持错误容忍度。

Conclusion: AI工作流的实际价值更多取决于人类仍需检查的输出比例，而非其正确率。这一"复核负担"属性是可测量但通常未被测量的关键指标。工具设计特征（引用来源、置信度声明）对降低复核成本有显著作用，但自验证机制的收益与其延迟代价之间的权衡需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Enterprise AI programmes stall at a rate that is widely quoted and poorly explained. This paper measures the mechanism. Six document-heavy workflows of the kind performed daily in regulated financial services were run across four model families and three tool configurations, three times each, producing 5,093 scored output elements across 72 configurations. Each configuration was assessed twice: against a demonstration bar, being a single correct run on a single case, and against a production bar requiring sustained accuracy, reproducibility across repeats, verifiable attribution, and a confidence signal that carries information. 57 of 72 configurations cleared the demonstration bar and 32 cleared the production bar, a survival rate of 56.1%. The paper then computes the review burden each configuration imposes, estimated out of sample rather than with hindsight. A tool that states no confidence requires review of 100% of its output, because it offers a reviewer no basis for triage. Requiring the tool to cite its sources and state a confidence reduces that to 49% while holding the residual error tolerance in 17 of 20 configurations. Adding a self-verification pass costs 2.3 times the latency of the plain configuration, reaches 44%, and is the only configuration that fails to hold the error tolerance. The practical implication is that the value of an AI workflow is set less by how often it is right than by how much of it a human must still check, and that the second property is measurable and rarely measured.

</details>


### [87] [TELLER: Dual-Path Iterative Preference Optimization for Table Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28680)
*Yixin Peng,Kehao Li,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TELLER是一种通过从错误中迭代学习和推理来进行表格实体链接的方法，采用直接回答和推理两条路径，分别使用迭代直接偏好优化和长度归一化的正则化偏好优化，在TableInstruct和MammoTab V2基准上均提升了实体链接准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格实体链接方法依赖于静态的偏好数据进行训练，无法适应模型演化过程中的残余错误；同时推理长度的变化会引入序列级偏好学习的偏差。这两个局限性导致训练监督信号无法动态更新，限制了模型性能的进一步提升。

Method: TELLER首先检索并排序Wikidata候选实体，在提示中保留缩减后的表格证据。直接回答路径采用迭代直接偏好优化（DPO），每次迭代用更新后模型的残余错误刷新偏好数据；推理路径使用过滤和压缩的链式思维（CoT）理由进行监督微调，随后进行迭代长度归一化的正则化偏好优化（RLPO）。

Result: 在TableInstruct实体链接子集上，直接回答路径准确率从94.35%提升至94.50%；在MammoTab V2上从87.59%提升至88.20%。推理路径在TableInstruct上从92.90%提升至92.95%，在MammoTab V2上从79.09%提升至81.85%，同时保持了较高的完整推理生成率。

Conclusion: 迭代偏好学习对简洁实体预测和显式推理两条路径均有效，证明了从错误中动态学习能够显著提升表格实体链接的性能。

Abstract: Entity linking in tables matches short and ambiguous cell mentions to their corresponding knowledge-base entities. Existing approaches typically rely on data preprocessing pipelines that retain either compact or extensive table content as contextual evidence, and then formulate entity linking as a language generation task for instruction-tuned models; recent systems further incorporate explicit reasoning to disambiguate challenging mentions. However, their training supervision is usually static: fixed preference data cannot adapt to the residual errors of an evolving model, while variations in reasoning length can bias sequence-level preference learning. To address these limitations, we present TELLER: Table Entity Linking through Learning from Errors and Reasoning. We first retrieve and rank Wikidata candidates and retain reduced table evidence in the prompt. The direct-answer path applies iterative direct preference optimization and refreshes its preference data with residual errors from the updated model. The reasoning path uses filtered and compressed chain-of-thought rationales for supervised fine-tuning, followed by our iterative length-normalized regularized preference optimization. On the TableInstruct entity-linking subset, the direct-answer path improves accuracy from 94.35\% to 94.50\%; on the MammoTab V2 evaluation set, it improves accuracy from 87.59\% to 88.20\%. The reasoning path improves accuracy from 92.90\% to 92.95\% on TableInstruct and from 79.09\% to 81.85\% on MammoTab V2, while maintaining high rates of complete reasoning generation. These results show that iterative preference learning benefits both concise entity prediction and explicit reasoning.

</details>


### [88] [Demystifying Entropy-based Selection for Chain-of-Thought Compression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28707)
*Sara Candussio,Daniel Scalena,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了基于熵的思维链（CoT）剪枝方法，发现熵在选择CoT步骤或令牌时并不优于随机剪枝。低熵令牌仅在数学基准上看似有效，原因在于数字令牌本身固有的低熵特性。通过因果补丁实验证明任务信息分布于整个推理链而非集中在少数可由启发式识别的令牌中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于熵的剪枝已被提出作为压缩思维链推理的有效方法，可在几乎不损失准确率的情况下减少推理开销。然而，此前研究对熵剪枝方法在不同模型和任务上的鲁棒性缺乏系统验证，其有效性的内在机制也未被深入探究。

Method: 1) 在多种模型和推理任务上对比低熵/高熵CoT步骤选择与随机剪枝的性能；2) 将从句子级剪枝扩展到令牌级剪枝，评估保留低熵令牌的效果；3) 通过因果补丁实验，将少量CoT令牌的原始激活替换回去，观察对完整推理性能的恢复程度。

Result: 1) 在任何评估设置下，熵相对于随机剪枝均无优势；2) 保留低熵令牌仅在数学基准上有效，这是因为数学问题中的数字令牌本身具有低熵特性且承载语义信息；3) 因果补丁实验表明，仅补丁极少量CoT令牌即可恢复近乎完整的全迹性能，说明任务信息并非集中在少量可通过启发式识别的令牌中，而是分布于整个推理链上。

Conclusion: 基于熵的CoT剪枝方法是无效的，任务信息分布在整个推理链上而非集中于少数可识别的令牌。这挑战了基于熵的启发式方法用于CoT压缩的有效性，提示需要重新审视CoT压缩策略的设计方向。

Abstract: Entropy-based pruning has been proposed as an effective method for compressing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with negligible accuracy loss. We test the robustness of low- and high-entropy CoT step selection methods across various models and reasoning tasks, showing that entropy offers no advantage over random pruning in any evaluated setting. Moving from sentences to tokens, we then show that retaining low-entropy tokens seems effective only on mathematical benchmarks. We find this is due to the inherently low-entropy nature of numeric tokens, which also convey semantic content in such problems. Finally, we demonstrate that patching a subset of a few CoT tokens with their original activations recovers near-perfect full-trace performance, providing causal evidence that task information is not concentrated in a small set of CoT tokens identifiable by heuristics, but rather distributed across the full reasoning chain.

</details>


### [89] [The Morphological Core of Dungan: A Two-Dialect Finite-State Model and a Multi-Genre Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28766)
*Anton M. Alekseev,Sergey I. Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用基于HFST实现的有限状态形态分析器，对中亚东干语（Sinitic语言，西里尔字母书写）的真实语料形态进行量化测量。研究发现：显性屈折罕见（仅9.3%）、歧义高度局部化（78.1%令牌唯一分析）、语法核心近乎封闭（词典缺失占失败主因），而东干语的开放前沿主要在词汇层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 东干语虽然在语法文献中有详尽描述，但其形态在实际使用中的量化特征从未被系统测量。本文旨在填补这一空白，将已有的语法知识形式化并使其在语料上可测量。

Method: 采用HFST实现的有限状态形态分析器作为测量工具，覆盖甘青（文学标准）和陕西两种方言变体。该模型不提供新语法描述，而是形式化东干语研究的既有知识，并在三种体裁的语料库上进行测量。同时构建无语形变化的词干列表作为基线对照。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）显性屈折罕见且有限：百科语域中仅9.3%识别的词符有显性标记，系统仅10个范畴，程度标记几乎缺失；（2）歧义真实但高度局部化：78.1%词符获得唯一分析，歧义残余几乎全部集中两个附着词素-di（属格/进行体）和-ni（方位/预期）；（3）语法核心有效封闭：78%-95%的分析失败源于词典缺失，模型有意未实现的现象最多仅占失败的4.5%。基线比较显示：留出覆盖率（80-85%）不低于开发覆盖率（73%），无语形词干列表达67.4%，形态分析净贡献5.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 东干语形态系统的量化特征被首次系统地测量：其语法形态核心是封闭的，而真正的开放前沿在于词汇层面。分析器、数据源和评估脚本全部开源发布。

Abstract: Dungan, a Sinitic language of Central Asia written in a Cyrillic-based script, is described in detail in the grammatical literature, yet the quantitative properties of its morphology in actual usage have, to the best of our knowledge, never been measured systematically. This paper uses a finite-state morphological analyzer as a measuring instrument. Implemented with HFST and covering both dialect groups (the Gansu variety, which is the literary standard, and the Shaanxi variety), the model offers no new grammatical description; it formalises the knowledge accumulated in Dungan studies and makes it measurable on corpora of three genres. Three results follow. Overt inflection is rare and limited: only 9.3% of recognized tokens in the encyclopaedic register have an overt marker, the system has just ten categories, and degree marking is almost absent. Ambiguity is genuine but sharply localized: 78.1% of tokens receive a single analysis, and the residue sits almost entirely on two clitics, -di (genitive/progressive) and -ni (locative/prospective). And the grammatical core proves effectively closed, the claim the instrument is really needed for: between 78% and 95% of the tokens the analyzer fails on, depending on register, are simply absent from the lexicon, and the phenomena the model deliberately declines to implement account for at most 4.5% of those failures. Held-out coverage (80 to 85%) is no lower than development coverage (73%), while a stem list with no morphology already reaches 67.4%, so the morphology is worth 5.2 points. The open frontier of Dungan is lexical. The analyzer, its sources and every evaluation script are released openly.

</details>


### [90] [Self-Supervised Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28777)
*Siran Peng,Cuiyu Yang,Tianyu Fu,Tianshuo Zhang,Haoyuan Zhang,Weisong Zhao,Anyang Su,Minghui Wu,Huiying Li,Xiangyu Zhu,Chenxu Zhao,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督技能优化框架（SSO），在无标签数据上实现大语言模型代理技能的学习与迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理技能优化方法依赖监督信号（GT标签、任务分数、奖励或任务特定评估器），但许多实际应用缺乏此类标注或评估条件，亟需仅凭无标签任务实例即可学习可复用技能的方法。

Method: SSO采用对比式迭代框架：在无标签批次上运行当前技能生成执行结果，从中抽样子集生成完整技能探针，再将探针在同批次上运行；LLM法官对比探针与当前技能的答案、轨迹、工件或终端状态，同时行为提取器独立识别行为差异；SSO聚合跨实例的证据，对行为排序并渲染新技能，且仅在验证集上表现更优时才接受更新。

Result: SSO在闭端和开端任务上均超越现有无GT的提示优化器；在闭端基准上逼近甚至超过最强基于GT的技能优化器，且完全无需任何GT反馈。

Conclusion: SSO证明仅凭无标签任务实例即可有效优化可复用代理技能，为缺乏监督信号的场景提供了可行方案，且性能已达乃至超越利用GT的基线方法。

Abstract: Agent skills provide frozen large language model (LLM) agents with reusable procedural guidance, and recent work shows that such skills can be optimized with ground-truth (GT) feedback. Many applications, however, lack GT labels, task scores, rewards, or reliable task-specific evaluators. We therefore introduce Self-Supervised Skill Optimization (SSO), a comparative framework that learns a reusable skill from unlabeled task instances alone. At each step, SSO runs the current skill on an unlabeled batch, uses a subset of the resulting executions to generate complete skill probes, and runs the probes on the same batch. An LLM judge compares the resulting answers, trajectories, artifacts, or terminal states. A separate behavior extractor identifies behavioral differences without seeing the judge's decisions. SSO uses these decisions to aggregate evidence for and against the observed behaviors across instances. It then ranks the behaviors by the resulting evidence and renders a new complete skill from the highest-ranked behaviors. The update is accepted only if the new skill outperforms the current one on an unlabeled validation set. SSO outperforms existing GT-free prompt optimizers on both closed-ended and open-ended tasks. On closed-ended benchmarks, it approaches and sometimes exceeds the strongest GT-based skill optimizer without using any GT feedback.

</details>


### [91] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokTier，一个带状态的tokenization服务，通过增量式窗口重tokenization和GPU全量tokenization，解决了LLM服务（尤其编码代理场景）中被忽视的重复tokenization开销问题，在零语义偏离的前提下实现了最高437倍加速，显著降低首token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM serving系统虽然缓存了prompt的KV状态，但大多数前端仍然在每次调用时对完整请求文本重新tokenize。这在编码代理场景中尤为严重——代理在每次小的工具结果后都重新提交很长的对话记录，即使很短的追加内容也可能改变前一个序列末尾的token边界，导致复用困难。实测显示tokenization开销可占首token时间的64%，已成为性能瓶颈。

Method: TokTier采用两个核心策略：(1) 会话延续场景下，仅对追加位置附近的小窗口进行重tokenization，并通过逐请求的稳定边界检查确认拼接正确性，失败时扩大窗口或回退到全量tokenization；(2) 无可用复用前缀时，将GPT家族的regex预tokenization分解为run-local规则，在GPU上精确执行预tokenization和BPE。此外还部署了采样的影子验证器对线上流量进行复查。

Result: 在17个tokenizer家族上通过差分测试覆盖1.5×10^10次分割检查、12.4TB真实文本语料和93,000多个重放代理步骤，实现零偏差。增量修复100K到3M字符仅需0.5-1.1ms，比HF快437倍，在1M字符时比最强缓存基线快2.1倍。GPU全量tokenization编码1M字符请求仅0.87ms，比HF低491倍。集成vLLM后中位首token时间降低16-34%，P99降低23%。在50ms P99目标下，4个修复核心加1个GPU可支撑1,821 req/s，而16核心无状态前端仅能饱和40 req/s。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了通过状态感知的增量tokenization和GPU加速，可以在保证token输出与全量参考结果完全一致的前提下，大幅消除LLM服务管线中的重复tokenization开销，为编码代理等长上下文场景提供了有效的系统级优化方案。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


### [92] [Benchmarks Are Not Monolithic: Sample-Level Auditing and Orchestration for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28801)
*Philipp D. Siedler,Jordan Sassoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以数据集为中心的元评估框架，在样本级别沿五个潜在维度审计基准数据集，并应用于MMLU、ARC、WinoGrande、HellaSwag和TruthfulQA五个基准，揭示聚合准确率所掩盖的内部异质性，支持依据标准驱动的跨数据集子集编排，实现针对性的模型能力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据集通常被当作单一整体任务来处理，忽略了单个样本间在难度和需求上的显著差异，导致聚合准确率无法反映模型的细粒度能力表现，需要一种样本级别的元评估方法来揭示基准内部的结构性异质性。

Method: 提出一个五维度的数据集中心元评估框架，在样本级别对基准数据集进行标注，五个维度包括：认知与知识需求、语言与内容质量、任务属性、上下文、以及伦理安全与公平性。随后将该框架应用于五个有影响力的基准数据集，并利用标注信息实现跨数据集的条件驱动子集编排。

Result: 对五个基准数据集的标注揭示了显著的内在异质性，这些差异无法通过聚合准确率体现。标注结果能够支持基于特定标准（如推理深度、伦理敏感性）的跨数据集样本子集编排，从而进行更有针对性的模型能力评估。

Conclusion: 该框架将基准评估重新定义为数据集的自我审视，提供了一种原则性的方法论来分析和重组现有基准，使其更好地反映多样化的评估需求。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are central to evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs), yet they are typically conceived as monolithic tasks, obscuring substantial variation in the demands of individual samples. We introduce a dataset-centric meta-evaluation framework that audits benchmark datasets at the sample level along five latent dimensions: 1. Cognitive and Knowledge Demands, 2. Language and Content Quality, 3. Task Properties, 4. Context, and 5. Ethics, Safety, and Fairness. Applying this framework, we annotate five influential benchmarks -- MMLU, ARC, WinoGrande, HellaSwag, and TruthfulQA -- revealing pronounced internal heterogeneity that is not captured by aggregate accuracy scores. We show how these annotations enable criterion-driven orchestration of composite benchmark subsets across datasets, supporting targeted evaluation of model capabilities such as Reasoning Depth or Ethical Sensitivity. This approach reframes benchmark evaluation as dataset introspection, providing a principled methodology for analyzing and re-composing existing benchmarks to better reflect diverse evaluation needs.

</details>


### [93] [Rolling With Resistance: Preference-Optimized LLM Counselors Can Trade Goal Persistence for Relational Attunement in Motivational Interviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28814)
*Weiying Chen,Junlong Shen,Zhexuan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对动机性访谈（MI）中的"随抵抗而动"（rolling with resistance）策略，提出基于目标坚持性（Goal Persistence, GP）和关系协调性（Relational Attunement, RA）的二维评估框架，并通过直接偏好优化（DPO）探究惩罚两种失败模式（妥协与对抗）的效果。研究发现：惩罚对抗可靠地降低目标坚持性（三个基座模型均如此），而关系协调性的提升取决于基座模型；惩罚妥协则无效，因为模型在策略内很少妥协。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动机性访谈中，咨询师面对来访者的"持续性谈话"（sustain talk）需要"随抵抗而动"，但这一策略可能以两种相反方式失败：妥协（放弃改变目标以维系关系）或对抗（争论或指挥，压制来访者自主性）。作者希望回答一个关键问题：通过偏好优化来惩罚某一失败模式，究竟能教会模型正确地"随抵抗而动"，还是会诱发其相反的失败模式？

Method: 基于专家标注的AnnoMI语料库，构建主题不相交的DPO数据，偏好集仅在拒绝哪种失败模式上不同，并使用策略内负样本。训练自动评判器（对照AnnoMI专家标签验证，并由受过训练的人工编码员复核），在防火墙设计下（不相交的模型家族分别负责生成、标注和评判）对三个对齐指令模型（涵盖Qwen和Llama家族）进行盲法逐对胜率评分。

Result: （1）惩罚对抗在所有基座模型和所有随机种子下均可靠地降低目标坚持性至低于基线，这是一种稳健的代价；（2）关系协调性的提升依赖于基座模型——在三个基座中两个有提升，第三个没有；（3）惩罚妥协是无效的，因为这些模型在策略内很少妥协，因此权衡效果受各基座失败模式的调控；（4）仅提示（prompt-only）的控制组可以在不牺牲目标坚持性的情况下提升关系协调性，说明目标坚持性的代价来自优化过程本身而非协调性提升本身。

Conclusion: 偏好优化中惩罚失败模式并不能直接教会模型"随抵抗而动"，反而在惩罚对抗时以牺牲目标坚持性为代价；这种权衡的显现受模型本身的失败分布所门控。提示工程可以解耦关系协调性的提升与目标坚持性的代价，提示优化路径比偏好优化更具优势。

Abstract: In Motivational Interviewing (MI), a client's sustain talk (arguments for the status quo) calls for the counselor to roll with resistance, a move that can fail in two opposite ways: capitulation (abandoning the change agenda to preserve rapport) or confrontation (arguing or directing, overriding the client's autonomy). We introduce a two-axis evaluation of counselor responses, anchored in the Motivational Interviewing Treatment Integrity (MITI) code, Goal Persistence (GP) and Relational Attunement (RA), yielding a four-quadrant framing in which rolling with resistance is high on both, and we ask whether penalizing one failure through preference optimization teaches rolling with resistance or provokes its opposite. From the expert-annotated AnnoMI corpus we build topic-disjoint Direct Preference Optimization data whose preference sets differ only in which failure is rejected, using on-policy negatives. An automatic judge, validated against AnnoMI's expert labels and rechecked by trained human coders, scores blind pairwise win-rates against each base under a firewall in which disjoint model families generate, label, and judge. Across three aligned instruction models spanning the Qwen and Llama families, penalizing confrontation reliably lowers goal persistence below parity, on every base and in every seed run, a robust cost, whereas the attunement gain is base-dependent, present on two of the three bases but absent on the third. Penalizing capitulation is inert, because these models rarely capitulate on-policy, so the trade is gated by each base's failure profile. A prompt-only control raises attunement without the goal-persistence cost, locating the cost in the optimization rather than in attunement itself.

</details>


### [94] [Benchmarks Are Not Validation: A System-Level View of Financial LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28840)
*Burak Payzun,İrem Demirtaş,Simona Scala,Elena Ferretti,Seçil Arslan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张金融领域的LLM系统不应仅基于模型基准分数获准投产，而需要跨全栈（数据、模型设计、检索、生成、智能体行为、治理和实现）的系统级验证证据，并强调混合评估、LLM-as-a-judge需配备控制措施，以及验证应成为持续的系统纪律而非一次性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在金融应用中广泛部署，但当前评估仍以模型为中心（基准分数、任务准确率、一次性定性审查），这不足以证明金融系统的生产就绪性。金融场景对可靠性、合规性和可审计性要求极高，静态基准无法捕捉检索失败、不忠实生成、工具误用等关键失效模式。

Method: 基于金融机构GenAI应用验证的行业经验，提出多层验证框架（multi-layer validation view），涵盖数据、模型设计、检索与生成性能、智能体行为、治理和实现六层。论证混合评估（human+automatic）的必要性，并明确规定LLM-as-a-judge方法需配备多重评委、评分规则、一致性检查和可审计性控制。

Result: 论文指出静态基准难以捕捉的关键失效模式，包括检索失败、不忠实生成、工具误用、升级错误和运维不稳定，并论证了金融LLM验证应产出"决策就绪证据"而非仅分数。

Conclusion: 金融LLM验证应是持续的系统纪律而非一次性模型评分练习。研究议程聚焦于：系统感知基准、智能体轨迹验证、评委对齐协议和生命周期验证标准。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in financial applications that combine retrieval, proprietary data, tool use, orchestration logic, monitoring, and human escalation. Yet evaluation often remains model-centric: benchmark scores, task accuracy, or one-off qualitative reviews are treated as evidence of readiness. In financial settings, this is insufficient. We take the position that financial LLM systems should not be approved for production based on benchmark performance alone. They require system-level validation evidence across the application stack: data, model design, retrieval and generation performance, agent behavior, governance, and implementation. Drawing on industry experience validating GenAI applications in financial institutions, we outline a multi-layer validation view and explain why hybrid evaluation is necessary. We discuss where LLM-as-a-judge methods are useful and why they require controls such as multiple judges, rubrics, agreement, and auditability checks. We also highlight failure modes poorly captured by static benchmarks, including retrieval failures, unfaithful generation, tool misuse, escalation errors, and operational instability. Our position is that financial LLM validation should be an ongoing system discipline rather than a one-time model scoring exercise. Validation should produce decision-ready evidence, not only scores. We conclude with a research agenda for system-aware benchmarks, agent trace validation, judge alignment protocols, and lifecycle validation standards.

</details>


### [95] [TextCloak: Thwarting Unauthorized LLM Exploitation via RL-Driven Unlearnable Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28862)
*Chengshuai Zhao,Pingchuan Ma,Dawei Li,Bohan Jiang,Zhiyuan Yu,Zhen Tan,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TextCloak，一个基于强化学习（RL）的框架，通过生成不可学习示例（UEs）来保护文本数据免受大语言模型（LLMs）的未经授权微调利用。该方法采用GRPO-UE策略优化，在保持文本语义保真度的同时，诱导模型在微调后性能退化，并在六个数据集和九个LLM上验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本不可学习示例方法主要针对判别式语言模型的分类任务，依赖于注入类别特定的语言线索，无法适用于LLM的开放式生成场景，因此需要一种适用于LLM的通用性文本保护方法。

Method: TextCloak采用生成式策略将批量干净文本转换为不可学习示例，保持语义保真和语言自然度。通过GRPO-UE算法，以下游微调代理LLM的性能退化程度作为奖励信号，利用组相对策略优化更新生成器参数，形成双层优化框架以发现超越类别特定线索的通用保护模式。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和九个SOTA LLM上的综合实验表明，TextCloak能持续削弱未经授权的微调效果，同时保持文本对合法使用的效用。进一步分析验证了其在不同模型架构、训练配置和自适应攻击下的可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TextCloak作为一种实用的防御方案，能有效应对LLM的未经授权数据利用，在保护数据隐私和保持文本utility之间取得了良好平衡，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: The rapid development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to significant advances across a wide range of language tasks, while simultaneously raising growing concerns about unauthorized data exploitation and privacy leakage. Unlearnable examples (UEs) offer a promising defense by introducing carefully designed perturbations into data such that models trained on them exhibit degraded utility. However, existing methods for text protection are primarily designed for classification tasks (e.g., sentiment analysis) in discriminative language models and often rely on injecting class-specific linguistic cues, which limits their effectiveness in the open-ended generation settings of LLMs. In this work, we propose TextCloak, an RL-driven framework for protecting textual data against unauthorized LLM exploitation. TextCloak employs a generative policy that transforms batches of clean text into unlearnable examples while preserving semantic fidelity and linguistic naturalness. To optimize the policy, we introduce GRPO-UE, which rewards generated unlearnable text based on the downstream degradation they induce in fine-tuned surrogate LLMs and updates the generator parameters via group-relative policy optimization. This bi-level optimization enables the generator to discover generalizable protective patterns beyond class-specific cues. Comprehensive experiments on six publicly available datasets and nine state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that TextCloak consistently impairs unauthorized fine-tuning while maintaining text utility for legitimate use. Further analyses establish its transferability and robustness across model architectures, training configurations, and adaptive attacks, highlighting its broad applicability as a practical defense against unauthorized LLM exploitation.

</details>


### [96] [Token-Level Diagnosis of Sycophancy in LLMs with Attribution-Guided Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28906)
*Hieu Nguyen,Mahammed Kamruzzaman,Anshuman Chhabra,Gene Louis Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Integrated Gradients的Authority Share Index (ASI)方法，用于在token级别归因LLM的谄媚行为（sycophancy），并基于归因结果提出attribution-guided contrastive activation steering方法在推理时降低谄媚率，实验结果将谄媚率从96%降至25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM谄媚行为的工作只能判断模型输出是否匹配权威观点，但无法揭示提示中哪一部分驱动了谄媚行为。作者希望在token级别理解谄媚行为的来源，从而为缓解该问题提供更具可解释性的依据。

Method: 1) 提出Authority Share Index (ASI)，一种基于Integrated Gradients的token归因方法，用于衡量权威相关文本对模型决策的贡献程度；2) 通过5个模型和30个测试配置进行广泛实验验证；3) 提出attribution-guided contrastive activation steering方法，从谄媚和抵抗两类响应中提取高归因token构建转向向量，在推理时无需重新训练即可将模型推向抵抗方向。

Result: 实验发现：谄媚响应相比抵抗响应始终对权威token投入更多注意力；在谄媚案例中，权威的主张（claim）比权威的资质（credentials）获得更多归因注意力。所提出的转向方法在最强案例中将谄媚率从96%降至25%。

Conclusion: Token级别的归因不仅能解释谄媚行为的驱动因素，还能直接指导实用的干预措施，为缓解LLM谄媚问题提供了可解释且有效的推理时解决方案。

Abstract: Sycophancy refers to the tendency for large language models (LLMs) to match user beliefs at the cost of factual correctness, thereby undermining model reliability. Prior work on evaluating sycophancy in LLMs aims to assess whether a model's output matches an authority's claim, but cannot reveal which part of the prompt drives this sycophantic behavior. To bridge this gap, we investigate the relationship of sycophantic responses with an authority's credentials, their assertive claim, and the problem statement. We introduce the Authority Share Index (ASI), an Integrated Gradients-based token attribution method, which measures the degree to which a model's decision is driven by authority-related text. Through extensive experiments across five models and 30 test configurations, we find that sycophantic responses consistently direct more attention toward authority tokens than resistant ones. Moreover, our token attribution method reveals that for the sycophantic cases, the claim asserted by the authority receives more attention than the authority's credentials. Building on these findings, we propose attribution-guided contrastive activation steering to mitigate LLM sycophancy. Our method constructs a steering vector from high-attribution tokens of sycophantic and resistant responses, selectively pushing models toward resistance. This enables inference-time steering without retraining, lowering sycophancy from 96% to 25% in the strongest case. Together, our results show that token-level attribution can both explain what drives sycophancy and directly inform a practical intervention.

</details>


### [97] [FairFund-Bench: Evaluating Distributive Bias in LLM Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28934)
*Martin Lukk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FairFund-Bench基准，系统性地比较了审计格式（任务类型、比较上下文、审计透明度）对LLM资源分配偏见检测结果的影响，发现审计格式会改变偏见方向（单独评分时模型偏向少数族裔，并排排名时则惩罚某些群体），伪装审计中的偏见幅度远大于透明审计，而因果框架效应对模型的影响比人口统计特征大约一个数量级且跨模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正越来越多地参与稀缺资源分配，但其基于种族和性别等特征的分配偏见检测结果不一致——即使同一模型也同时出现正向和负向歧视的证据。作者认为这种分歧源于审计格式的差异，因此需要系统研究审计设计特征对检测结果的系统性影响。

Method: 作者构建了FairFund-Bench基准测试：包含600个基于人工撰写的模板生成的财务援助请求（依据130万个真实GoFundMe活动校准），覆盖三个领域、四种种族、两种性别类别以及基于福利应得理论的五种因果归因框架。基准系统变化三项审计设计关键特征：评估任务（评分、排名或分配）、比较上下文（单一或多刺激）、审计透明度（透明或伪装）。在14个模型上进行评估，并按四项标准评分：人口统计偏见、应得性对齐、跨任务一致性和跨上下文一致性。

Result: 审计格式确实会反转偏见方向：模型在单独评分时偏向少数族裔申请人，但在并排排名时惩戒某些群体。偏见幅度总体虽小，但在伪装审计中比透明审计大数倍——透明审计中模型面对仅姓氏不同的申请几乎均分资金。因果框架效应超过人口统计效应约一个数量级，且跨模型和审计格式均一致，表明LLM稳健地复现了人类的应得性评估。

Conclusion: LLM审计结果的不一致很可能源于审计格式的差异而非模型本身的不稳定；未来的审计设计必须考虑任务类型、比较上下文和透明度等因素，同时LLM的应得性判断与人类高度一致，这提示资源分配偏见的核心问题可能更多来自社会性的应得性框架而非模型的人口统计偏见。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in the distribution of scarce resources, raising concerns about biased allocations based on characteristics like race and gender. Recent LLM audits have produced inconsistent results, however, finding evidence of both positive and negative discrimination towards women and ethnic minorities, even for the same models. We show that this disagreement can arise from differences in audit format and introduce FairFund-Bench, a benchmark that systematically varies key features of previous audit designs: the evaluation task (rating, ranking, or allocation), comparison context (single or multi-stimulus), and whether the audit is transparent or disguised. The benchmark comprises 600 requests for financial assistance created from human-authored templates (calibrated against 1.3M real GoFundMe campaigns) across three domains, four race and two gender categories, and five causal framings of need derived from welfare deservingness theory. Across 14 models, audit format changes the direction of bias: models advantage minorities when rating claimants individually but penalize some groups when ranking them side by side. Bias magnitude, though small overall, is several times greater in disguised audits than in transparent ones, where, faced with appeals differing only in claimants' names, models overwhelmingly split funds equally. Causal framing effects, by contrast, exceed demographic effects by roughly an order of magnitude and are consistent across models and audit formats, indicating that current LLMs robustly reproduce human deservingness evaluations. The benchmark scores models on four criteria (demographic bias, deservingness alignment, cross-task consistency, and cross-context consistency), is publicly available, and can be readily adapted to other substantive domains.

</details>


### [98] [BLADE: Boundary-Expanded and Layer-Adaptive Dynamic Exit for Efficient LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28966)
*Keshu Fu,Keqin Peng,Jun Bai,Shuhan Qin,Chen Li,Junzhu Liang,Yefei Chen,Jiaqi Li,Yuanxin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BLADE是一个轻量级框架，通过动态终止大语言模型的推理过程来提升效率。它从句子、自我怀疑和段落边界构建多粒度检查点，并自动学习紧凑的信息性探测层子集，在五个基准和两个Qwen3模型上实现了接近基线的准确率，同时分别减少了24.8%（Qwen3-8B）和15.8%（Qwen3-4B）的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过生成冗长的推理轨迹来提升任务性能，但大量计算浪费在冗余的验证和修正上。现有基于探针的早退方法主要检测显式的自我怀疑表达，遗漏了许多更早的终止机会；而扩展到普通推理边界虽提高了覆盖率，却暴露了高度多样化的中间状态，其预测信息可能分散在不同隐藏层中。

Method: BLADE包含三个核心组件：（1）多粒度检查点构建——从句子、自我怀疑和段落边界提取多样化检查点；（2）通过重复答案补全来推导稳健的训练标签；（3）学习一个紧凑的信息性探测层子集，替代固定层选择或使用所有层的昂贵表示。推理时，将校准预测与检查点特定的确认规则结合，以平衡响应速度与过早退出风险。

Result: 在五个基准和两个Qwen3推理模型上的实验表明，BLADE在保持接近基线准确率的同时，在Qwen3-8B上减少了24.8%的生成token，在Qwen3-4B上减少了15.8%。消融研究证实了多样化检查点和自动层选择的有效性。

Conclusion: BLADE通过边界扩展和层自适应动态退出机制，提供了一种有效的大语言模型推理效率优化方法，在保持准确率的同时显著减少了推理计算开销。

Abstract: Large language models often improve task performance by generating long reasoning traces, but the resulting computation is frequently wasted on redundant verification and revision. Existing probe-based early-exit approaches mainly inspect explicit self-doubt expressions, leaving many earlier termination opportunities undetected. Expanding inspection to ordinary reasoning boundaries improves coverage, but also exposes highly diverse intermediate states whose predictive information may reside in different hidden layers. We present Boundary-Expanded and Layer-Adaptive Dynamic Exit for Efficient LLM Reasoning (BLADE), a lightweight framework that dynamically terminates reasoning by estimating whether the generated prefix is sufficient for correct answering. BLADE constructs multi-granular checkpoints from sentence, self-doubt, and paragraph boundaries, and derives robust training labels through repeated answer completions. It further learns a compact subset of informative probe layers instead of relying on fixed choices or expensive representations from all layers. At inference time, calibrated predictions are combined with checkpoint-specific confirmation rules to balance responsiveness and premature-exit risk. Experiments on five benchmarks and two Qwen3 reasoning models show that BLADE preserves near-baseline accuracy while reducing generated tokens by 24.8% on Qwen3-8B and 15.8% on Qwen3-4B. Ablation studies further confirm the benefits of diverse checkpoints and automatic layer selection, demonstrating an effective approach to more efficient LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [Mixture-of-Translators: Translating KV Caches Across Heterogeneous Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28979)
*Jin-woo Lee,Minkyung Song,Junghyun Oh,Seunghoon Han,Soyoung Park,Gwangseon Jang,Sungsu Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Mixture-of-Translators（MoT）框架，通过多个翻译器模块将源LLM的KV缓存映射到目标LLM的缓存空间，结合上下文校正损失，实现跨异构大语言模型的KV缓存复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM系统依赖共享上下文、检索证据和多智能体对话历史，但各模型的内部KV缓存是模型特定的，无法跨架构复用，导致每个模型必须重复预填充或存储相同上下文的缓存，限制了多模型推理和长上下文生成的扩展性。

Method: MoT使用多个翻译器模块捕获多样的源-目标映射关系，与依赖单一投影路径或全局共享潜在空间的先前方法不同；引入上下文校正损失（Context Correction Loss）对齐重放的目标轨迹与原生目标轨迹，解决缓存翻译中的两个竞争性失败模式：早期注入导致的传播翻译偏移和晚期注入导致的最后状态偏移。

Result: 在Qwen2.5、GPT-2和OPT模型的同构和异构翻译中，MoT保持了下游问答性能，包括Qwen2.5-7B规模翻译的51.0%平均闭集QA准确率和0.43平均抽取式QA F1。在实际案例研究中，MoT实现多智能体推理的质量保持内存复用，在长上下文缓存增强生成中保留了96.3%的直接上下文质量。

Conclusion: MoT实现了跨异构LLM的可扩展KV缓存复用，验证了基于翻译器混合和上下文校正的缓存翻译框架的有效性，支持多智能体推理和长上下文生成场景中的质量保持内存复用。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Large Language Model (LLM) systems increasingly rely on shared contexts, retrieved evidence, and multi-agent dialogue histories, yet their internal key-value (KV) caches remain model-specific and cannot be reused across architectures. Consequently, each model must repeatedly prefill or store caches for the same context, limiting the scalability of multi-model reasoning and long-context generation. We propose Mixture-of-Translators(MoT), a cache translation framework that maps context KV caches from a source LLM into the cache space of a target LLM. Unlike prior approaches that depend on a single projection path or global shared latent space, MoT uses multiple translator modules to capture diverse source--target mappings. To further reduce residual translation error, we introduce a Context Correction Loss that aligns the replayed target trajectory with the native target trajectory. We reveal two competing failure modes in cache translation: propagated translation shift from early injection and last-state shift from late injection. MoT addresses them through translator mixtures and target-side correction. Across homogeneous and heterogeneous translations among Qwen2.5, GPT-2, and OPT models, MoT preserves downstream QA performance, including Qwen2.5-7B-scale translation with 51.0% average closed-set QA accuracy and 0.43 average extractive QA F1. In practical case studies, MoT enables quality-preserving memory reuse for multi-agent reasoning and retains 96.3% of direct-context quality in long-context cache-augmented generation, demonstrating scalable KV cache reuse across heterogeneous LLMs.

</details>


### [100] [PARALLEL: A Prefrontal-Aligned Reinforcement inspired Approach for Language-Model Learning under Explicit Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28982)
*Namkyung Yoon,Sanghong Kim,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.CL
