TL;DR: 提出PARALLEL方法，一种受前额叶皮层和强化学习启发的语言模型适应方法，通过学习何时以及以多大强度更新每个训练样本，在保留全量适应性能的同时实现更高效、更稳定的流式适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型适应方法对所有训练样本施加统一的更新强度，忽略了不同样本在局部更新中带来的收益差异，导致不必要的参数更新和资源浪费。

Method: PARALLEL借鉴了大脑前额叶皮层中目标相关控制和不确定性相关控制的互补角色，将这些信息表示为独立的控制器信号并与当前模型表示结合。一个受强化学习启发的控制器利用即时效用-成本反馈为每个样本分配依赖样本的更新强度，从而在适应每个样本时决定"是否更新"以及"更新的强度"。

Result: PARALLEL在多种任务中保留了全量适应94.1--99.2%的性能；在XSum和CNN/DailyMail上保留了96.9--98.6%的ROUGE-1和ROUGE-2分数以及98.8--98.9%的ROUGE-L分数；在相同累计适应时间或GPU能耗下，PARALLEL在ARC上取得更高的准确率，且后期适应轨迹更稳定。

Conclusion: 学习每个样本的更新时机和更新强度能够支持稳定且高效的部署后流式适应，在避免不必要参数更新的同时维持接近全量适应的性能。

Abstract: Recent language models achieve strong performance across a variety of tasks, but conventional adaptation applies updates uniformly across training samples regardless of their local update benefit. We propose PARALLEL, a prefrontal-aligned reinforcement inspired approach for language-model learning. Inspired by the complementary roles of goal-related and uncertainty-related control, PARALLEL represents these forms of information as separate controller signals and combines them with the current model representation. A reinforcement-inspired controller assigns sample-dependent update intensity using immediate utility-cost feedback. PARALLEL therefore learns when and how strongly to adapt to each sample, prioritizing beneficial updates while limiting unnecessary parameter changes. PARALLEL uses available updates more efficiently than selective baselines while retaining 94.1--99.2\% of Full-adaptation performance. Beyond multiple-choice reasoning, experiments on XSum and CNN/DailyMail show that PARALLEL retains 96.9--98.6\% of the ROUGE-1 and ROUGE-2 scores achieved by Full adaptation and 98.8--98.9\% of the corresponding ROUGE-L scores. When compared at the same cumulative adaptation time or GPU energy, PARALLEL achieves higher ARC accuracy and exhibits a more stable late-stage adaptation trajectory than Full adaptation in the representative run. These results show that learning when and how strongly to update each sample supports stable and efficient post-deployment stream adaptation while avoiding unnecessary updates.

</details>


### [101] [From Inline Notes to Collected Commentaries: Toward Context-Preserving Organization of Exegetical Knowledge in Classical Chinese Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29044)
*Ke Liang,Qi Su,Churen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将传统儒家经学中的"集注"编纂形式化为NLP任务，提出一个保留行内注释上下文依赖的自动编纂与经学知识组织框架，通过两步提示链和跨版本提及聚类，在《山海经》案例研究中CoNLL F1超过97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行内注释和集注是儒家经学传统中重要的学术交流形式，但长期以来缺乏计算层面的关注，其自动编纂和知识组织尚未得到系统性处理。

Method: 提出一个计算框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 两步提示链（two-step prompt chaining）用于识别注释所关联的主文本段落及其经学功能；(2) 跨版本提及聚类（cross-source mention clustering）用于整合不同版本中的注释内容。框架建立在传统中国经学与语文学的理论基础上，保留了行内注释的上下文依赖性。

Result: 在《山海经》案例研究中取得CoNLL F1分数超过97%的效果，显示出框架在注释识别、功能分类和跨版本整合方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为历史经学知识的大规模组织奠定了基础，可支持广泛的后续语文学和NLP应用。

Abstract: Inline notes and collected commentaries are important forms of scholarly communication that evolved within the Confucian exegetical tradition, yet have received little computational attention. Drawing on traditional Chinese exegetics and philology, this paper formulates collected commentary compilation as an NLP task and proposes a computational framework that preserves the contextual dependency of inline notes while enabling their automatic compilation and exegetical knowledge organization. It combines two-step prompt chaining for identifying the associated main-text segments and exegetical functions of annotations with cross-source mention clustering for integrating commentary across editions, achieving a CoNLL F1 score above 97% in a case study on the Classic of Mountains. Our framework lays the foundation for the large-scale organization of historical exegetical knowledge, thereby supporting a broad range of downstream philological and NLP tasks.

</details>


### [102] [Tokenizer-Agnostic Engram Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29065)
*Jia Peng Lim,Hai Leong Chieu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对DeepSeek Engram条件记忆模块的哈希方法进行改进，将基于token级别的N-gram异或哈希替换为多项式哈希，并建立跨N的联合嵌入空间，使Engram模型对不同tokenizer具有兼容性（tokenizer-agnosticism），在保持相当性能的同时实现字节等价序列的哈希等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek的Engram条件记忆模块使用token级别的N-gram哈希进行嵌入查找，这与所使用的tokenizer紧密耦合——若使用不同的tokenizer，则必须从头训练自己的Engram嵌入。这种耦合限制了Engram嵌入的可复用性和跨模型兼容性，因此需要改进哈希机制以解耦tokenizer依赖。

Method: 将N-gram视为从所有可能的跨token字节序列中采样潜在有用字节序列的方法，而非建模互不相交的N-gram空间。用通用的多项式哈希（polynomial hashing）替代基于XOR的哈希，并建立跨N的联合嵌入空间（joint embedding space），从而实现字节等价token序列的哈希等价。

Result: 这种简单的替换在保持与原始方法相当性能的同时，实现了tokenizer无关性：字节等价的token序列具有哈希等价性，使Engram嵌入可以在不同tokenizer之间复用。

Conclusion: 通过将N-gram哈希从token级改为字节级并结合多项式哈希与联合嵌入空间，Engram模块成功解耦了与tokenizer的紧耦合关系，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现了跨tokenizer的嵌入复用能力。

Abstract: Deepseek's Engram, a conditional memory module, was introduced to trade-off storage versus reasoning in large language models. However, the module relies on token-level $N$-gram hashing for Engram embedding lookup, introducing a tight coupling to the tokenizer used: a model with a different tokenizer would have to train its own Engram embeddings from scratch. To improve the reusability of Engram embeddings, we propose a change to the hashing routine, enabling compatibility between Engram models using different tokenizers. Instead of modelling disjoint $N$-gram spaces, we treat $N$-gram as a method to sample potentially useful byte sequences, from all possible byte sequences across tokens. We replace the XOR-based hashing with the general polynomial hashing with a joint embedding space across $N$. This work investigates the possible trade-offs and shows that this simple substitution produces comparable performance and achieves tokenizer-agnosticism: hash equivalence for byte-equivalent token sequences.

</details>


### [103] [Semantics of Subterfuge: Benchmarking Legal Deception Detection Against General-domain State-of-the-Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29066)
*Theekshana Samaradiwakara,Nisansa de Silva,George C. Lobb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域基于NLP的自动欺骗检测（ADD）进行了综述与对比分析，在7个数据集上统一评估了6个微调Transformer模型与7个大语言模型（LLM）在4种提示策略下的表现。研究发现存在显著的领域敏感性：微调模型在数据丰富的通用领域表现优异，而少样本LLM在低资源法律场景中保持竞争力；思维链（CoT）提示通常不如直接分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在欺骗检测上的判断存在准确性有限和可扩展性差的局限，而法律诉讼、执法和在线安全等领域对准确、可扩展的欺骗检测有迫切需求。NLP提供了数据驱动的替代方案，但需要系统性地评估不同方法在真实法律场景中的适用性。

Method: 采用综述与实证相结合的混合方法：（1）梳理从基于特征的机器学习到大规模语言模型的ADD技术演进；（2）设计统一评估框架，在2个法律领域和5个通用领域的7个数据集上，对比6个微调Transformer模型（如BERT类）与7个LLM，并考察4种提示策略（含CoT）的表现。

Result: 微调模型在数据丰富的通用领域数据集上表现最佳；少样本LLM在低资源法律领域保持竞争力；Chain-of-Thought提示在欺骗检测任务中往往不如直接分类提示。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测存在显著的领域敏感性，法律领域的高风险场景需要领域适配和可解释系统，不能简单依赖通用模型或单一提示策略。

Abstract: Deception detection has critical implications for legal proceedings, law enforcement, and online security. Although human judgment is limited in accuracy and scalability, Natural Language Processing (NLP) offers a data-driven alternative. We present a survey and comparative analysis of NLP-based Automatic Deception Detection (ADD) focusing on the legal domain, reviewing the evolution from feature-based machine learning to Large Language Model (LLM) approaches. We conduct a unified empirical evaluation across seven datasets (two legal, five general-domain), comparing six fine-tuned transformer models and seven LLMs under four prompting strategies. The results show strong domain sensitivity, with fine-tuned models excelling in data-rich general domains and few-shot LLMs remaining competitive in low-resource legal settings. Chain-of-Thought prompting often underperforms direct classification. These findings highlight the need for domain adaptation and interpretable systems in high-stakes legal contexts.

</details>


### [104] [Faster but Different: Diagnosing and Controlling Content Drift in Accelerated Multimodal Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29079)
*Yaoxuan Dou,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散的多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）在训练免加速（training-free acceleration）场景下出现的输出内容漂移问题。通过在300张真实图像上进行Fast-dLLM与未加速基线输出的对比诊断，发现状态刷新间隔是影响一致性的关键因素，缩短KV缓存刷新间隔可在1.3倍加速下实现近乎精确的一致性，同时揭示现有加速方法存在真实的内容替换风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练免加速使基于扩散的多模态大语言模型更易部署，但加速过程可能静默改变生成内容，这种serving-time一致性问题此前未被系统研究。作者旨在通过成对诊断实验，识别加速导致内容漂移的根源，并提供可操作的实现层面一致性控制。

Method: 在300张真实图像上进行成对诊断（accelerated vs. unaccelerated）：1）在长文本场景（每步1.05-1.25个committed tokens）下比较Fast-dLLM与基线；2）进行置信度阈值调参、状态刷新消融和图像交换干预实验；3）对50个低一致性对进行针对性审计；4）双标注者盲评评估事实性错误差异；5）对比固定间隔与自适应/平滑刷新变体。

Result: 置信度阈值调参改变解码行为但不改变基线一致性；陈旧的可视状态和生成的文本状态是漂移的根源。缩短KV缓存刷新间隔可获得单调的速度-一致性前沿，在实测1.3倍加速下实现近乎精确的一致性。该诊断在dLLM-Cache和LaViDa中同样出现，但dLLM-Cache需同时收紧两个缓存才能恢复一致性，从而失去速度优势。50个低一致性对中一半存在真实内容替换；双标注盲评显示加速-基线事实误差差值为0.00（95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]），未检出差异但无法建立事实等价性。自适应或平滑刷新变体在匹配计算量下均未优于固定间隔。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是提供一种成对诊断方法和实现范围的一致性控制手段，而非精确性或安全性保证。研究发现加速可能引发真实的内容替换，提示部署者需对加速输出进行一致性验证，强调了serving-time一致性评估应成为加速方法部署的关键环节。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration makes diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) more deployable, but it may silently change generated content. We study this serving-time consistency problem on 300 real images, comparing Fast-dLLM outputs with the same model's unaccelerated outputs. Across the mild parallelism induced in our long-form setting (1.05--1.25 committed tokens per step), confidence-threshold tuning changes decoding behavior but not baseline agreement. State-refresh ablations and an image-swap intervention instead identify stale visual and generated-text states as contributors to drift. For the tested Fast-dLLM implementation, shortening the KV-cache refresh interval yields a monotonic speed--agreement frontier and near-exact agreement at a measured 1.3x speedup. The initial diagnosis also appears with dLLM-Cache and LaViDa, although dLLM-Cache recovers agreement only after both caches are tightened, which removes its speed advantage. Independent prompts and images reproduce the threshold-insensitivity and refresh recovery. A targeted audit finds genuine content substitution in half of 50 low-agreement pairs. In a separate blinded two-annotator evaluation, the pooled accelerated-minus-baseline factual-error difference is 0.00 (95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]); this sample detects no difference but does not establish factual equivalence. Finally, none of the tested adaptive or smoothed-refresh variants beats the fixed interval at matched compute. Our contribution is a paired diagnostic and an implementation-scoped consistency control, not an accuracy or safety guarantee.

</details>


### [105] [Can Zero-Shot LLMs Predict Child Malnutrition? A Fairness and Temporal Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29082)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Md Ahshanul Haque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在零样本设置下使用预训练大语言模型（GPT-4o-mini）进行儿童发育迟缓预测的可行性、公平性和时间稳健性。基于孟加拉国2007-2022年的人口健康调查数据，该方法在平衡准确率上与随机森林基线相当，但召回率更高，同时在跨性别和时间上表现稳定，但在居住地和家庭财富类别上存在公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家（尤其是南亚地区）的儿童营养不良问题依然严峻，早期识别高危儿童对及时干预和资源配置至关重要。然而传统监督学习方法需要大量标注数据，本研究旨在探索是否可以利用预训练大语言模型在零样本条件下进行儿童发育迟缓预测，以降低对标注数据的依赖并评估其部署可行性。

Method: 利用2007-2022年孟加拉国人口健康调查（BDHS）数据，将母亲、儿童、医疗保健和家庭特征转化为基于语义可解释的提示（prompt）表示，使用GPT-4o-mini进行零样本发育迟缓预测，并与随机森林基线模型进行比较。同时评估了不同人口统计和社会经济群体间的公平性以及跨调查波次的时间稳健性。

Result: GPT-4o-mini的零样本推理在平衡准确率上与有监督的随机森林基线相当，但在识别发育迟缓病例时具有显著更高的敏感性；在儿童性别分组上表现相对一致，在BDHS各波次间预测行为稳定。然而，在居住地（城乡）和家庭财富类别上观察到了重要的公平性差异。

Conclusion: 虽然预训练大语言模型在零样本儿童发育迟缓预测中展示了与监督学习相当的潜力和更优的敏感度，但其在不同居住地和财富群体间的公平性差异需要进一步研究。在基础模型部署于公共卫生预测场景之前，必须解决这些问题。

Abstract: Child malnutrition remains a major public health challenge in low- and middle-income countries, particularly in South Asia, where early identification of vulnerable children is critical for timely intervention and resource allocation. This study aims to evaluate the feasibility, fairness, and temporal robustness of using a pretrained large language model (LLM) in a zero-shot setting for child stunting prediction using population health survey data. Using Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey (BDHS) data collected between 2007 and 2022, we transformed maternal, child, healthcare, and household characteristics into semantically interpretable prompt-based representations and evaluated GPT-4o-mini for zero-shot stunting prediction, comparing its performance against a random forest baseline and assessing fairness across demographic and socioeconomic groups as well as temporal robustness across survey waves. The results demonstrate that zero-shot inference using GPT-4o-mini achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the supervised baseline while exhibiting substantially higher sensitivity for identifying stunting cases, relatively consistent performance across child sex groups, and stable predictive behaviour across BDHS waves; however, important fairness disparities were observed across residence and household wealth categories, highlighting the need for further investigation before deployment of foundation models in public health prediction settings.

</details>


### [106] [M3-DuplexBench: A Multi-Turn, Multilingual, Multidomain Benchmark for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29125)
*Ryo Fukuda,Atsushi Ando,Hiroki Kanagawa,Takatomo Kano,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Yuya Chiba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-DuplexBench，一个面向全双工口语对话系统（FDSDSs）的多轮、多语言、多领域基准测试，支持英语和日语，覆盖日常对话和多轮问答，并在多种对话上下文设置下评估最新FDSDS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话系统虽能边听边说，实现自然的轮流说话、反馈词处理和用户插话处理，但多轮对话中的公平比较仍具挑战性，且现有基准对语言和对话领域的覆盖有限。

Method: 提出M3-DuplexBench基准，支持英语和日语，覆盖日常对话与多轮问答两个领域；并在多种对话上下文设置下评估模型，包括单轮、仅用户、教师强制全上下文设置，以分析对话历史对模型行为的影响。

Result: 对最新FDSDS模型的实验揭示了模型特定的轮流说话特征、跨语言和跨领域的明显性能差距，以及对话上下文带来的混合影响。

Conclusion: M3-DuplexBench为FDSDS提供了更全面的评测框架，有助于揭示模型在不同语言、领域和上下文条件下的能力差异与特性。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) can listen while speaking, enabling natural behaviors such as smooth turn-taking, backchannel handling, and user barge-in handling. However, fair comparisons in multi-turn conversations remain a challenge. In addition, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of languages and dialogue domains. We propose M3-DuplexBench, a multi-turn, multilingual, multidomain benchmark for FDSDSs. M3-DuplexBench supports English and Japanese and covers both casual conversation and multi-turn question answering. In addition, we evaluate models under multiple dialogue context settings, including single-turn, user-only, and teacher-forced full-context settings, to analyze how dialogue history affects model behavior. Experiments with recent FDSDSs reveal model-specific turn-taking characteristics, clear performance gaps across languages and domains, and mixed effects of dialogue context.

</details>


### [107] [Authorship Verification of Transcribed German-Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29168)
*Oren Halvani,Sophie Titze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将作者验证（AV）应用于德语视频转录文本中，通过十个AV方法在三个自建语料库（300个视频，150个说话人）上的实验评估，发现基于字符和词元n-gram的传统AV方法表现最佳（最高88%准确率、90% AUC），而基于transformer的现代方法表现显著较差，表明传统AV方法在跨语言和跨模态场景下仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV研究主要集中于书面文本和英语语料，对口语形式（如视频转录文本）及其他语言（如德语）的关注不足。作者希望通过填补这两个研究空白，探索传统AV方法在德语视频口语场景中的适用性和有效性。

Method: 作者将AV任务应用于德语视频的转录文本，构建了三个自编译语料库，包含300个视频和150个说话人，并评估了总共十种AV方法（包括基于字符和词元n-gram的传统方法以及基于transformer的现代方法）在验证视频对中说话人身份一致性方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于简单字符和词元n-gram表示的传统AV方法表现最佳，在全部语料库中达到最高88%的准确率和90%的AUC；相比之下，基于transformer的现代方法在所有评估语料库上表现显著较差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在德语视频口语转录这一新场景下，传统基于n-gram的AV方法仍然具有竞争力和实用价值，而现代transformer方法在该领域的应用效果尚不理想，提示AV领域需要继续重视与改进传统方法或开发更适合口语场景的新型方法。

Abstract: Authorship Verification (AV) represents an important subfield of digital text forensics and addresses the fundamental question of whether two texts were written by the same author. Although the field has made substantial progress over the past two decades, several important challenges remain unresolved or underexplored. For instance, most AV research has focused on written texts, despite the fact that language is expressed not only in written but also in spoken form, such as in videos. Moreover, existing AV studies have predominantly concentrated on English, while other languages, including German, have received comparatively little attention. To address these research gaps, we apply AV to spoken language in the form of transcripts of German-language videos and examine the effectiveness of established AV methods in verifying a speaker's identity across video pairs. Our experimental evaluation, based on a total of ten AV methods applied to three self-compiled corpora comprising 300 videos from 150 speakers, shows that the best performance (up to 88% accuracy and 90% AUC) is achieved by traditional AV approaches based on simple character- and token n-gram representations. In contrast, more modern transformer-based approaches perform significantly worse on all evaluated corpora. Our results therefore suggest that traditional methods in the field of AV remain both competitive and relevant.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRM提出了一种新的奖励建模框架，将中间推理轨迹作为离散潜在变量进行学习，通过端到端的on-policy优化显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然，从而将深度推理评估与精确打分紧密结合，在分布内/分布外数据集及RLHF任务中均优于标量RM、生成式RM和混合RM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型高效且具备概率可解释性，但依赖表面线索，难以泛化到复杂或分布外任务；生成式奖励模型虽然通过推理提升鲁棒性，但其基于自然语言的分数量化灵活性和概率可解释性不足。现有混合方法通过off-policy多任务学习并行优化两个范式，但无法保证生成的推理轨迹主动对齐或有益于下游标量奖励预测，存在根本性的"不匹配"问题。

Method: LatentRM将中间推理轨迹建模为离散潜在变量，在端到端框架中通过on-policy优化潜在推理空间，显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然。该方法将深度推理评估与精确打分紧密耦合，实现二者的联合优化。

Result: 在分布内（in-distribution）和分布外（OOD）数据集以及RLHF场景中的广泛验证表明，LatentRM在偏好建模和策略对齐方面均优于标量RM、生成式RM和混合RM，覆盖从开放式对话到复杂推理的任务。

Conclusion: LatentRM证明了将推理过程与标量打分通过潜在变量进行端到端耦合优化是有效的奖励建模范式，能够在保持精确打分的概率可解释性的同时，显著提升复杂与分布外任务上的泛化能力和策略对齐性能。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [109] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"经验互文性检测"任务，即自动化识别不同迁徙路线（如撒哈拉以南与巴尔干走廊）的移民叙事中共享的经验共鸣。在108篇法语移民叙事、816条专家标注数据上，对比了词汇基线、句嵌入、POS结构特征、主题词典、叙事特征及零样本LLM评分等多种无注释方法。结果显示：表面/结构/嵌入方法与专家判断仅弱相关（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本最佳（r=0.38）；少样本对Qwen有损但对Mistral增益显著；叙事位置显著预测互文性；结合全部31个特征的监督混合模型达到r=0.45，较最优单方法提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同地理路线的移民（如撒哈拉以南和巴尔干走廊）在叙述中往往呈现惊人相似的经历——警察暴力、走私者剥削、危险过境、家庭分离。这种跨路线的"经验互文性"尚未被系统研究，且缺乏现成的标注训练数据。作者希望在不依赖人工标注的前提下，自动识别这些叙事之间的经验共鸣，从而为大规模跨文化、跨路线的移民研究提供计算工具。

Method: 从108篇法语移民叙事中自动生成句子对，采用多种无注释方法评分：词汇基线、句嵌入、基于词性的结构特征、迁徙特定主题词典、上下文感知的叙事特征（包括叙事位置），以及使用Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B在三种提示策略下进行零样本LLM评分。所有方法均与816条专家标注的互文性判断（标注者间Krippendorff α=0.27）进行验证。最终还构建了结合全部31个特征的监督混合模型。

Result: 所有表面、结构和嵌入方法仅与专家判断弱相关（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本达到最佳单方法相关（r=0.38）；少样本示例损害Qwen但显著提升Mistral；叙事位置显著预测互文性——出发阶段句子对的经验共鸣最强；结合全部31个特征的监督混合模型达到r=0.45，比最优单方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 经验互文性是一个可计算但具挑战性的任务。零样本LLM（尤其Qwen2.5-7B）优于传统表面/结构/嵌入方法，但仍有较大提升空间。叙事位置作为关键特征揭示了结构性的经验模式（出发阶段最具共鸣），且有监督特征融合能显著提升性能。标注者间一致性较低（α=0.27）也表明该任务本身存在主观性，未来需进一步探索更稳健的标注方案和更强的LLM推理能力。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [110] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hy-MultiTurn是一个面向中文深度多轮对话理解的新型基准测试集，基于真实聊天机器人故障分析提炼出六种失败机制，构建了209个跨12-76轮的控制式任务，覆盖六种评估模式。在22个前沿模型配置上的评估显示该基准极具挑战性，最强模型GPT-5.5也仅在41.1%的回答中满足全部要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文多轮对话基准大多只覆盖短对话，未能全面评估模型在长多轮交互中对早期细节的记忆、后续修改的追踪、指代对象识别以及条件未满足时的行动抑制等能力，且缺少对模型失败原因和机制的深入分析。

Method: 通过对真实聊天机器人失败案例的分析，识别出六种反复出现的失败机制，并据此定义了六种受控评估模式：约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制和指代消解。构建了209个控制式任务（12-76轮），并通过对话长度、无关话题干扰和口语化表达增加难度。

Result: 在22个前沿模型配置上的评估显示，Hy-MultiTurn具有广泛挑战性：最强整体配置GPT-5.5在所有要求均满足的回答中也仅占41.1%，且没有任何模型在全部六种模式中表现最佳。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn弥补了中文长多轮对话深度理解评估的空白，通过细粒度的错误机制分析为理解模型在多轮交互中的失败原因提供了系统性的评估框架，表明当前前沿模型在长对话深度理解方面仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [111] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了大语言模型在超出能力范围任务上产生的"徒劳推理"现象，并提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，通过奖励塑形和事后拒绝增强，使模型在能力边界处倾向于拒绝而非生成表面上合理但错误的推导，从而在保持性能的同时大幅减少徒劳推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上会产生计算成本高昂却语义空洞的推理过程。这些看似合理但实际错误的推导可能误导用户，带来风险。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的系统性分析与针对性解决方案。

Method: (1) 通过系统性分析刻画"徒劳推理"现象，揭示能力过度自信和能力与行为之间的系统性失配；(2) 提出CaRL框架，包含两个核心组件：奖励塑形（reward shaping）以激励模型在能力边界处拒绝而非进行徒劳推理；事后拒绝增强（hindsight refusal augmentation），将失败样本转化为拒绝监督信号。

Result: 实验表明，CaRL在保持各难度任务性能的前提下，显著减少了徒劳推理的发生，有效实现了能力与行为对齐，且不牺牲模型实用价值。

Conclusion: CaRL验证了通过强化学习引导模型"知道何时退出"的可行性，为构建更可信、更可靠的大语言模型提供了一种实用性方案，使模型在能力边界处展现诚实行为。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [112] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle是一个隐私优先的单用户系统，利用本地辅助LLM构建配对训练数据，将小型语言模型（0.5B-7B参数）适配为按用户个人写作风格重写AI编辑文本的工具，推理时无需指令提示词。在219个科学论文评估对上，自动综合评分在不同模型大小和解码策略下均稳定在0.69，表明小型模型足以完成重写任务，模型大小仅决定权衡而非质量排序。二次评估中，400条来自五个LLM评审的评分显示InMyStyle输出的感知AI感比辅助AI生成的输入低20%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI编辑文本缺乏个性化、依赖推理时指令提示词以及云端处理带来的隐私问题，旨在实现无需指令提示的单用户写作风格适配。

Method: 利用多个本地辅助LLM从用户文档构建配对训练样本，对0.5B至7B参数的基座模型微调LoRA适配器；采用长度感知生成预算和自动分块以支持不同长度的输入。

Result: 在219个科学论文评估对上，自动综合评分在所有模型大小下（贪心与采样解码）均稳定在0.69；400条LLM评审评分显示InMyStyle输出的平均感知AI感比辅助AI生成输入低20%以上，且感知AI感随模型大小减小而降低。

Conclusion: 小型模型足以胜任所测量的重写任务，模型大小主要决定性能权衡而非稳定的质量排名；InMyStyle能有效降低文本的感知AI感，实现个性化风格适配。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [113] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Data Turnstile，一个开源的合成数据生成框架，通过将多轮工具调用分解为带验证和错误反馈的分步生成流程，为小型语言模型（SLM）生成高质量的函数调用训练数据。在BFCL单轮基准上，用Turnstile数据微调的Qwen3-0.6B达到75.9%准确率，超越其基础模型67.4%（含思维链），接近3-7倍大的模型；在τ²-bench多轮基准上，1.7B模型达到31.1%的pass²，超过19倍大的Qwen2.5-32B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在代理式部署中具有低延迟、低成本、端侧隐私等优势，但在工具使用任务上表现不佳，主要瓶颈是训练数据稀缺且噪声大。由于SLM不能像大模型那样通过模型容量补偿低质量监督，数据质量成为关键瓶颈。现有方法缺乏对工具调用训练数据质量的可控生成手段。

Method: Data Turnstile将多轮工具调用交互分解为约束化的逐步生成流程，引入验证和错误反馈循环。该框架接受用户定义的API规范，提供对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性的细粒度控制，从而生成高质量的函数调用合成训练数据。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，用Turnstile数据微调的Qwen3-0.6B达到75.9%整体准确率（基础模型开启思考仅67.4%），与Qwen3-1.7B（78.4%）和Qwen3-4B（79.9%）差距缩小，尽管模型分别小3倍和7倍。在τ²-bench的多轮代理基准上，Turnstile训练的Qwen3-1.7B在电信领域达到31.1%，较基础6.6%提升4.7倍，超过19倍大的Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct（27.4%）；Qwen3-0.6B达24.6%，较基础3.5%提升7倍。同时发布了覆盖1000+API和10万+多轮交互的数据集。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile证明了通过可控、可验证的合成数据生成，小型语言模型可以在工具调用任务上大幅缩小与更大模型的差距，甚至超越远超自身规模的模型，表明数据质量而非模型容量是SLM工具使用能力的关键制约因素。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [114] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [115] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TwT（Translation with Thought）是一种资源理性框架，通过学习在直觉推理与深思熟虑推理之间动态调节，实现多领域机器翻译（MDMT）中对不同难度输入的适应性推理。模型通过两阶段训练（困难感知的长思维链SFT + 混合奖励RL），在15个基准、3种已见和59种未见语言上，以7B和14B的参数量超越更大规模SOTA推理模型的翻译质量，同时减少32-60%的token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译面临不同领域间语言复杂度差异大的挑战，而现有模型对所有输入采用统一的推理策略，缺乏根据不同难度动态调整推理努力的机制。受人类译者根据难度调节推理投入的启发，作者提出了一种资源理性的翻译框架。

Method: TwT采用两阶段训练：(1) 监督微调（SFT）阶段，使用从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏并经GPT-4o重写的难度感知长思维链（CoT）轨迹，以模拟人类式推理经济性；(2) 强化学习阶段，使用混合奖励函数同时优化翻译质量和推理效率。该框架在推理过程中学习在直觉（快速）推理与深思熟虑（慢速）推理之间进行调节。

Result: 在15个涵盖领域内和领域外场景的基准、3种已见语言和59种未见语言上评估，并在三个骨干模型上进行消融实验。TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上超越更大规模的SOTA推理模型，同时将token使用量减少32-60%。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则（即根据难度调节推理投入）相结合，能够在多领域机器翻译中实现强健的泛化能力、高质量的翻译以及高效的推理。该工作验证了资源理性推理在机器翻译中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [116] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了五个突厥语系语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语、吉尔吉斯语）之间的跨语言迁移行为，通过成对迁移矩阵发现迁移强度与语言亲缘关系密切相关，且迁移方向、迁移目标语言及拉丁化处理都会显著影响迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言迁移对低资源机器翻译至关重要，但它在亲缘关系密切的语言族内如何表现尚未得到充分刻画。理解同语族语言间的迁移规律可以指导低资源翻译系统的源语言选择和数据处理策略。

Method: 以五种突厥语（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语、吉尔吉斯语）为研究对象，构建成对迁移矩阵：每次用一种转移源进行微调，在另一种转移目标上进行评估，而翻译目标保持不变。基于mT5模型进行实验，并考察拉丁化、数据集和模型设置等因素的影响。

Result: （1）迁移在亲缘关系密切的突厥语对中最强，特别是土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语；（2）迁移方向具有不对称性，同一转移源-转移目标对在不同翻译目标下表现不同；（3）拉丁化在脚本不匹配的场景中能提升BLEU和chrF，但效果在不同指标间并不一致；（4）迁移源在不同的数据集和模型设置下基本保持稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明突厥语族内的跨语言迁移受语言亲缘关系驱动，且迁移方向、翻译目标及脚本标准化（拉丁化）等因素均会影响迁移效果，为低资源突厥语机器翻译的源语言选择和预处理策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [117] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种零token记忆访问机制，通过实体-上下文图和时间层级两种互补结构组织原始交互记录，仅在最终问答阶段调用一次LLM，消除了记忆操作阶段的所有LLM调用和token消耗。在长记忆和长上下文QA基准上达到竞争性性能，同时将记忆操作时间成本降低57.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体记忆系统在维护和检索记忆时需要额外的LLM调用，产生反复的token和时间成本，且生成中间记录可能丢失或合并原始证据，导致证据不可靠。

Method: Zero-Mem不生成任何中间表示，而是保留原始交互轨迹作为唯一记录来源，并采用两种互补的确定性组织方式：(1) 实体-上下文图（entity-context graph）捕捉跨交互的连接；(2) 时间层级（temporal hierarchy）保持会话局部性和状态。查询时根据查询依赖权衡两种视图，从两者中检索并通过结构恢复支持关系和上下文，再进行确定性校准以丢弃冲突证据，最终仅由最终QA阅读器（唯一调用LLM的组件）生成答案。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文问答基准上实现与基线相当的性能；与最快的基线相比，记忆操作时间成本降低57.6%；消融实验验证了两种视图及其查询依赖协调机制的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化智能体记忆无需生成过去经历的中间表示，零token记忆操作是可行且高效的，为LLM智能体的长期记忆设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [118] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒、免微调预训练模型的LLM逆向方法：直接在目标LLM自身生成的合成数据上从头训练一个逆向语言模型，采用"前向下一词预测"的对偶形式——"前一词预测"——来实现提示词重构，无需访问模型权重或logits。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的自回归生成使提示词与响应之间存在多对多映射，现有逆向工作依赖微调预训练的序列到序列模型、访问模型权重或logits，且需要大规模外部数据集，缺乏真正黑盒、免外部辅助的解决方案。

Method: 提出功能化逆向方法：仅使用目标LLM自身合成的数据，从头训练一个显式的逆语言模型。训练方式为"前一词预测"（previous-token prediction），与前向的"下一词预测"形成对偶生成链路，支持通过采样生成多样化的提示词重构，保证所有这些重构提示词在目标LLM下诱导出相似响应。

Result: 该方法在多种数据集上具有泛化能力，并展现出跨不同LLM的可迁移性（可用一个LLM训练，重构其他LLM生成的响应所对应的提示词）。在基于token的提示词与响应重构评估指标上，优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 实证表明，仅靠从目标LLM自身采样的数据训练逆向模型，即可实现高质量且多样化的黑盒提示词重构，无需外部预训练模型微调或模型内部信息的辅助，且具备跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [119] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在真实服务器环境中部署大规模语言翻译模型的量化权衡问题，对比了EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族（1.7B到22B参数）在A100或H100 GPU上的表现。研究发现文档分块策略结合W4A8或W8A8量化可以改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线；同时引入WMT24++的文档级评估，发现标准片段级评估无法预测量化与长上下文文档翻译之间的交互。Hy-MT2对量化稳健，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感，翻译质量会迅速崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在真实服务器环境中部署面临低延迟和高响应质量的挑战。虽然量化是常见的内存压缩和推理加速手段，但其对延迟和吞吐量的影响很少在受控的编排级工作负载下进行评估。此外，标准MT基准依赖孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态，缺乏对量化条件下文档级翻译质量的系统性研究。

Method: 研究涵盖EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族的五个模型（1.7B-22B参数），在单张A100或H100 GPU上进行部署。采用文档分块策略与W4A8、W8A8量化相结合的方式，评估延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线；并引入基于WMT24++的文档级评估框架，系统比较不同文本分块策略在量化条件下的翻译质量表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化结合能显著改善延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线。标准片段级评估无法预测量化与长上下文文档翻译之间的交互效应。Hy-MT2在量化下保持稳健，而EuroLLM对量化极为敏感，所有量化格式下翻译质量均迅速崩溃。

Conclusion: 推理效率与翻译质量之间的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略的选择。这为大规模翻译模型在实际服务器环境中的高效部署提供了重要指导，强调了在评估量化方案时必须考虑文档级长上下文场景的必要性。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [120] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦评估范式，将LLM智能体的记忆能力拆分为"记住"（Know）与"应用"（Act）两个层面，通过配对测试揭示智能体即使能回忆用户偏好，也不一定能在行为中体现这些偏好，即存在"知识利用率"缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在个性化交互中即使上下文中包含用户偏好，仍可能无法据此调整响应。但现有评估无法区分失败原因是"没记住"还是"记住了但没用"。为了隔离这一失效环节，作者提出解耦评估范式。

Method: 设计配对的Know测试（回忆测试）和Act测试（行为场景测试），将同一用户偏好分别作用于两类测试；在16个系统和5种记忆架构上进行大规模实验，评估1,000条偏好，并设置三个表达强度级别。

Result: Know与Act结果之间存在显著差距：智能体在偏好回忆测试中常常通过，但在配对行为场景中未能体现同一偏好。记忆架构虽能缩小这一差距，但在健康和治疗相关偏好上利用率仍然特别薄弱，而这些场景中未能应用的后果最为严重。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的个性化失效根源主要在于"记住了但没应用"而非"没记住"，记忆架构有助于缓解但不足以根本解决该问题，尤其在健康/治疗领域需要专门改进。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [121] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在受控合成环境中系统检验了"干预数据是教授模型因果推理的金标准"这一假设，发现该假设以有启发性的方式失败：在辛普森悖论世界中，增加预训练中干预样本的比例并不能改善模型的因果方向判断——do()响应的幅度单调增长，但其符号仍从观测上下文复制而来。决定干预证据是否被使用的是推理时的上下文证据类型而非训练混合比例。模型因果推断能力存在于权重中，但开关位于上下文中，激活修补将该开关定位到中间层的观测行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"干预数据是教模型因果推理的金标准"这一广泛假设是否成立。作者在完全受控的合成环境中将观测相关性与因果效应进行对比，揭示干预数据在预训练中的比例与模型实际因果推理能力之间的关系并非直觉所预期。

Method: 在辛普森悖论世界（观测相关与因果效应符号系统性相反）中进行受控合成实验。通过：(1) 改变预训练中干预样本的比例；(2) 在推理时操纵上下文中的证据类型（纯观测、混合、纯干预探针）；(3) 擦除上下文中的观测证据；(4) 四状态内容操纵；(5) 跨训练种子稳定性检验；(6) 在0.93B参数规模上进行鲁棒性验证；(7) 在CLadder上进行外部审计；(8) 使用激活修补（activation patching）定位功能开关；(9) 设计证据平均化协议降低符号错误率。

Result: 干预样本比例增加只使do()响应幅度单调增长，但符号仍从观测上下文复制，28/50、19/50、41/50分别对应纯观测、混合、纯干预探针上下文的正确率。擦除观测证据后因果插值能力立即释放（ratio_true=+0.56）。该抑制跨11/11训练种子稳定，在0.93B参数下以31.8%对6%的逆转率保持鲁棒。CLadder审计揭示一个两层结构的正效应先验。基线采样噪声使符号误差达26%，所提证据平均化协议降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型的因果推理能力存在于权重中，但触发开关位于推理时的上下文中。增加预训练干预数据比例不足以改变模型的因果方向判断，关键在于推理时上下文中是否存在干预证据。激活修补将这一"开关"定位于中间层的观测行，说明模型学到的是内容介导的分级抑制机制而非简单的训练分布统计。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [122] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB)，证明现有的AI文本检测基准测试结果无法迁移到"人类文本被LLM改写"这一场景：五个检测器在直接LLM生成文本上的检测率高达91-93%，但当人类文本被LLM改写后，检测率骤降至15-31%，下降了60-78个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI文本检测基准仅比较人类原创文本与LLM直接生成的文本，但实际应用中大量场景是人类文本被LLM改写（如润色、编辑），现有基准无法反映这一关键场景的检测器性能。作者希望探究传统基准上的表现是否能预测检测器在重写场景下的行为。

Method: 构建ARB基准，包含1,800篇人类源文本（分别来自XSum、WritingPrompts和OpenWebText各600篇），使用四个开源生成器（Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B）。每个源文本生成四种匹配变体：人类原文、LLM直接生成、LLM改写的人类文本、同一生成器改写的LLM文本。使用五个检测器（FastDetectGPT、Binoculars-falcon-7b、RADAR、BERT-Defense、RoBERTa-Defense），在1%假阳性率（TPR@1%FPR）的严格条件下评估。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本的检测率分别为91.2%和93.5%，但对LLM改写后的人类文本仅检测出30.8%和15.1%，下降60-78个百分点。同一检测器对LLM文本被同模型改写的场景保持了78.3%和83.0%的召回率，仅下降10-13个百分点。RADAR呈现相同模式（66.8%降至12.2%），而BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统人类vs-LLM基准上的检测性能无法迁移到"由LLM改写的人类文本"这一实际场景。检测器对纯LLM改写仍相对鲁棒，但对人类文本被LLM改写极度脆弱，表明现有基准严重高估了检测器在现实应用中的表现。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [123] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务来测量视语言模型在合作对话中的认知警觉性（epistemic vigilance），发现模型经常忽略自己私有图像中的关键证据而去迎合对话伙伴（即奉迎行为/sycophancy），并证明通过任务无关的奉迎示例学习到的向量来引导模型，可以减少认知警觉相关错误，使模型更加可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作对话中，人类会随着新信息的到来不断更新信念，并具备认知警觉性——当新信息与既有信念冲突时能够察觉并修复冲突。为了让AI系统在复杂合作任务中成为可靠伙伴，它们需要同样地权衡新信息与自身私有证据及共享上下文，并适当地表面化不一致之处。当前视语言模型在这方面的表现缺乏系统度量。

Method: 设计了一个信息不对称的对话式"找不同"（spot-the-difference）任务：两个模型各自私下看到一张图片，通过对话判断两张图片是否相同或找出差异。模型需要权衡对话伙伴提供的信息与自己私有图像中的证据。此外，作者将模型在任务中的认知警觉性违背与奉迎行为（sycophancy）联系起来，并使用从任务无关的奉迎示例中学习到的向量对模型进行引导以降低奉迎倾向。

Result: 模型在该任务中经常失败：它们频繁忽略私有图像中的关键证据而去赞同对话伙伴，即使这种赞同是没有根据的。这种认知警觉性违背表现为合作目标导向对话中的过度迁就（over-accommodation）和薄弱的证据立足（weak evidential grounding）。通过任务无关的奉迎示例学习到的引导向量可以有效降低认知警觉性相关错误，使模型更忠实地报告自己的证据。

Conclusion: 模型在信息不对称的合作任务中表现出奉迎行为导致的认知警觉性缺陷，但可以通过任务无关的奉迎减向（steering vector）进行缓解，使模型成为更可靠的信息报告者和合作伙伴。这为提升AI系统在合作对话中的可靠性提供了可行方向。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [124] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV提出了一种新的KV缓存压缩方法，将固定的KV预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，通过重建被驱逐token对softmax注意力分子和分母的贡献来恢复信息损失，在LongBench和RULER等基准上实现了同预算下更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存压缩方法存在不足：驱逐式方法（eviction）会永久丢弃未被选中的token，导致其在注意力中的聚合贡献被完全移除；而合并式方法（merging）虽然保留了更多信息，但会扰动本应保持精确的保留token的键和值。因此需要一种既能保留被驱逐token信息贡献又不干扰精确主缓存的方法。

Method: ResKV将固定的KV预算划分为两部分：精确主缓存（exact main cache）和紧凑残差缓存（residual cache）。关键创新在于：1) 将被驱逐token的遗漏信息形式化为softmax注意力分子和分母中的残差统计量；2) 主缓存token和残差条目参与同一个softmax归一化，使残差条目同时恢复注意力的分子和分母质量而非事后校正；3) 构建时通过验证代理（validation proxy）确定每层和每个KV头的残差分配；4) 解码时通过动态门控（dynamic gate）调整每个查询的残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER基准上，覆盖查询感知与查询无关场景、多种骨干网络、不同缓存预算和代表性压缩基线，ResKV在相同保留KV预算下实现了广泛的性能提升，同时保持了压缩解码的实际效率（包括峰值内存使用和长上下文解码吞吐量）。

Conclusion: ResKV通过残差统计量重建被驱逐token对softmax注意力分子和分母的贡献，在保持精确主缓存不受扰动的同时有效恢复了信息损失，实现了高效的KV缓存压缩，在性能与推理效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [125] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FriendBench是一个用于从20秒双人破冰对话片段中判断两人是熟人还是陌生人的基准测试。研究发现，最佳模型与人类群体在准确率上统计上无差异，但模型偏向预测"陌生人"，而人类在两个答案间保持平衡——这是有效先验的差异而非判别能力的差异。仅在人类中，可见行为（视频）在语音之上提供了额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交情境的理解往往依赖于非语言行为而非单纯的言语内容。然而，现有的多模态基准测试很少聚焦于"关系状态推断"这一具体而微妙的社交认知任务。作者希望系统性地评估当今前沿多模态AI模型是否具备人类水平的社交情境解读能力，特别是在"相识vs陌生"这一基础二元判断上。

Method: 作者构建了FriendBench基准，包含96个平衡的双人破冰对话样本（20秒片段），每对参与者回答相同类型的提示，确保只有互动方式能揭示答案。他们横跨文本、音频、视频三种模态，比较了来自7家公司的26个模型，并与匹配的人类小组进行对比。数据集包含刺激材料、人类评分和模型预测。

Result: 最佳模型在每种模态下的准确率与人类群体在统计上无显著差异。但模型与人类达到该准确率的方式不同：人类在两个答案间保持平衡，而最强模型倾向于预测"陌生人"。更丰富的通道（从文本到音频到视频）对模型和人类有不对称的提升作用，且只有人类能从视频中的可见行为在语音基础上获得额外增益。

Conclusion: 当前最强的多模态模型在"熟人vs陌生人"判断任务上已达到人类水平准确率，但其决策模式与人类存在系统性偏差（偏向"陌生人"），属于有效先验差异而非判别能力差异。此外，视频中的人类行为信息目前仅对人类有帮助，对模型而言语音已提供足够的判别信号。作者发布了所有数据资源以促进该方向的研究。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [126] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迭代学习模型研究频率分布对语言组合性演化的影响。研究发现，当频率施加于整体意义向量时，高频率意义可逃脱语法压力（与自然语言一致）；但频率施加于意义子部分时，语言无法跨代传递，尽管高频元素能被可靠学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明迭代学习模型中语言组合性可在学习瓶颈下自发产生，但尚未系统探讨意义频率分布如何影响组合性的性质。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究不同频率结构对语言演化方向的影响。

Method: 使用迭代学习模型，系统操控意义频率分布方式（整体意义向量频率 vs. 部分/子单元频率），观察跨代传递过程中语言结构的演化，包括组合性特征及其可传递性的变化。

Result: （1）频率作用于整体意义时，高频率意义可脱离主导低频意义的语法约束，与自然语言中的高频不规则现象一致；（2）频率作用于意义部分时，尽管高频元素被可靠习得，语言仍无法跨代稳定传递，无法形成组合性泛化所需的关系统结构。

Conclusion: 频率对涌现语言结构的塑造作用仅在频率分布定义于学习者可整体习得的形式-意义单元时成立；当频率分布于更小的子单元时，无法支撑组合性泛化所需的关系结构，从而阻碍语言的稳定跨代传播。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Optical Flow Sensor: A Direction-Selective Bionic Retina Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28686)
*Juchen Zhou,Bonan Yan,Yuchao Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于像素级的光流传感器（OFS）集成电路，结合DVS事件比较与时间差测量实现片上全并行光流计算，并进一步提出基于光学忆阻器的OFS方案；相比FPGA加速的DVS系统功耗降低303倍，输出数据量减少约3.3倍，同时保持微秒级延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的光流计算依赖高密度采样和全局计算，存在延迟高、功耗大的问题。生物学视网膜通过局部ON/OFF通路和并行处理高效提取运动信息，受此启发，需要一种低功耗、低延迟的片上光流计算方案。

Method: 提出像素级光流传感器（OFS）集成电路，结合动态视觉传感器（DVS）的ON/OFF事件比较与时间差测量技术，实现片上全并行光流计算；设计了光流专用地址事件表示（OF-AER）接口以支持低功耗、高吞吐读出；并进一步在CMOS基础上提出基于光学忆阻器的OFS方案以降低功耗和面积开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提OFS相比FPGA加速的DVS系统功耗降低303倍，并保持微秒级延迟；直接输出光流向量使输出数据量减少约3.3倍。

Conclusion: 该OFS展示了在超高速、低功耗视觉感知应用中的巨大潜力，为仿生视觉系统中的高效运动感知提供了新的硬件实现路径。

Abstract: Optical flow characterizes motion in the visual field and is fundamental to motion perception and tracking in biological and artificial vision systems. Biological retinas extract motion efficiently through local ON/OFF pathways and parallel processing, while conventional frame-based optical flow relies on dense sampling and global computation, resulting in high latency and power consumption. To overcome these limitations, we present a pixel-level Optical Flow Sensor (OFS) integrated circuit. The design combines Dynamic Vision Sensor (DVS) ON/OFF event comparison with time-difference measurement to enable fully parallel optical flow computation on-chip. An optical-flow-specific Address-Event Representation (OF-AER) interface supports low-power, high-throughput readout. \rev{Based on the CMOS-based OFS, we further propose optical memristor-based OFS to reduce sensor power consumption and area overhead.} Experimental results show that the proposed OFS achieves a 303$\times$ reduction in power consumption compared with FPGA-accelerated DVS systems while maintaining microsecond-level latency. Moreover, by directly outputting optical flow vectors, the OFS reduces output data size by approximately 3.3$\times$, demonstrating strong potential for ultra-high-speed, low-power vision sensing applications.

</details>


### [128] [WitCert: Sound Runtime Risk Observability and Gating for KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28699)
*Fanzhe Wei,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种KV缓存量化的运行时可验证方法，为每个（层、头、步）提供精确注意力与压缩注意力之间总变化距离的上界，使部署系统能够实时判断压缩是否损害了当前请求，而非仅依赖离线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化仅通过离线基准平均值验证，部署中的系统无法判断压缩是否正在损害当前正在服务的请求，缺乏运行时可感知的量化损伤度量。

Method: 提出双层级运行时度量器：（1）确定性band-norm-witness界，适用于任何保持缓存的即插即用量化器及任意查询（自适应安全，基于最坏情况柯西-施瓦茨不等式和RoPE带的幺正性）；（2）针对受控减法抖动INT8量化器的概率性证书，在显式请求级失败预算下成立（针对非自适应查询，核心定理在Lean 4中机器验证）。此外，提出度量器驱动的门控修复机制和交叉层误差取消分析。

Result: 度量器通过环境保护补丁集成到SGLang，可在实时服务中测量任意注册的张量函数方案。度量器驱动的门控可在基准规模下恢复质量底线：raw-cast FP8在困难的RULER任务上从22.8恢复至79.7，与未压缩差异的配对检验界为[+0.0, +0.8]。28层扫描显示单层污染不会造成性能损失（0/28），但激进的方案依赖交叉层误差取消而非逐层保真度。经认证的INT8缓存以相同内存服务1.88倍KV token。

Conclusion: 该工作为KV量化提供了类似DTrace的部署时健全性度量，将量化验证从离线统计转向在线确定性/概率性保障，并揭示了交叉层误差取消机制，为非保守量化方案提供了理论依据与实用工具。

Abstract: KV-cache quantization is validated today by offline benchmark averages; a deployed system cannot tell whether compression is damaging the request it is serving right now. We give it a provably sound runtime meter, a "DTrace for KV quantization": a per-(layer, head, step) upper bound on the total variation between exact and compressed attention. The meter has two tiers: a deterministic band-norm-witness bound, sound for any cache-preserving black-box quantizer and for any query (adaptive-safe, worst-case Cauchy-Schwarz plus RoPE band-unitarity), and a tighter probabilistic certificate for a controlled subtractively-dithered INT8 quantizer under an explicit request-level failure budget (stated for non-adaptive queries; core theorems machine-checked in Lean 4). Three results. Observability: the meter enters SGLang through an environment-guarded patch, and any scheme registered as one tensor function is measured in live serving. Repair: meter-driven gating, risk-ranked where the witness is saturated and certified where it is informative, empirically restores the quality floor at benchmark scale. For example, raw-cast FP8 improves from 22.8 back to 79.7 on hard RULER tasks, with the difference from uncompressed bounded at [+0.0, +0.8] by a paired test. Analysis: aggressive schemes survive on cross-layer error cancellation, not per-step fidelity. In a 28-layer sweep, no single layer's pollution alone loses anything (0/28), and the certified INT8 cache serves 1.88 times more KV tokens at the same memory in SGLang.

</details>


### [129] [Characterizing LLM Kernel Access and Memory Interaction in Multi-Partition NUMA GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28824)
*Donghyeon Joo,Sooraj Puthoor,Nuwan Jayasena,Bahar Asgari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对多分区GPU上LLM工作负载的访存局部性挑战，提出了一种内存轨迹分析方法，将LLM内核操作数划分为三种工作组间共享模式（全局、部分、私有），并指出不同类别需要不同的优化策略（从简单的逐工作组绑定到子组感知协同调度），强调需要放置感知的内核编程和更智能的架构支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工作负载推动了多分区GPU的发展以扩展计算和内存容量，但其非均匀内存访问特性和分区间通信会加剧争用并降低局部性，导致内核延迟次优。现有方法缺乏对多分区GPU上LLM内核数据访问模式的系统性理解。

Method: 首先引入内存轨迹分析方法论来推导工作组级别的数据访问和共享行为；然后使用周期级模拟器评估局部性对延迟的影响；基于此将LLM内核操作数分为三类工作组间共享模式（全局、部分、私有），涵盖权重投影、混合专家和注意力变体等性能关键内核。

Result: 发现不同类型的共享模式需要不同的优化策略：从简单的逐工作组数据固定（pin）到子组感知的协同调度，揭示了现有LLM内核在多分区GPU上的访存局部性瓶颈及优化空间。

Conclusion: 多分区GPU需要放置感知的内核编程范式以及更智能的架构级支持，以改善工作和数据的局部性，从而降低LLM内核延迟。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) workloads motivate multi-partition GPUs as a path to scaling compute and memory capacity, but their non-uniform memory access characteristics and inter-partition communication can amplify contention and degrade locality, leading to suboptimal kernel latency. To address this, we analyze performance-critical LLM kernel implementations spanning weight projection, mixture-of-experts, and attention variants of state-of-the-art serving engines to present a characterization of data access patterns in multi-partition GPUs. First, we introduce memory trace analysis methodology to derive workgroup-level data access and sharing behavior, then evaluate the locality implications on latency using a cycle-level simulator. Using these tools, we categorize LLM kernel operands into three inter-workgroup sharing patterns (global, partial, or private) and show that the required optimization strategies differ across categories, from simple per-workgroup pinning to subgroup-aware co-scheduling. Our findings highlight the need for placement-aware kernel programming and smarter architectural support for work and data locality in multi-partition GPUs.

</details>


### [130] [Open-Source LLM-Driven Formal Verification: A Multi-Agent Pipeline for RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28877)
*Ha Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体流水线，将大语言模型（LLM）与开源形式验证后端（Yosys、SymbiYosys、Z3）结合，通过反例引导迭代来修复RTL设计。该流水线自动生成形式化属性、验证设计并将反例反馈给LLM，直到通过k-归纳法证明设计正确或预算耗尽。在ALU案例研究中成功修复真实功能缺陷并获得形式化证明，但在六基准测试套件中仅一个设计被可靠修复，论文详细分析了四种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证占用了现代芯片设计的大部分工作量，但提供数学正确性保证的形式验证工具昂贵且许可受限。现有的LLM驱动的RTL修复方法要么依赖仿真（仅覆盖部分输入），要么依赖商业工具，很少有方法能同时结合形式化证明与完全开源的工具链。

Method: 提出一个多智能体流水线：LLM生成形式化属性（properties），通过Yosys、SymbiYosys和Z3进行形式化验证，将反例反馈给LLM进行迭代修复，直到k-归纳法证明正确或达到迭代预算上限。

Result: 在ALU案例分析中，该流水线能检测并修复一个真实的功能缺陷并给出形式证明。在六个基准测试中，仅有一个设计被可靠修复。论文识别了四种失败模式：有界覆盖空洞（bounded-cover vacuity）、规格歧义、时序逻辑缺陷、多属性压力。此外还发现Yosys bind指令的实际局限性。

Conclusion: 本文将工作定位为可行性研究，提供了详细的失败分析，并为开源形式验证社区报告了Yosys bind指令的实用限制。虽然初步验证了LLM+开源形式验证的可行性，但可靠性仍需显著提升。

Abstract: Verification consumes the majority of modern chip design effort, yet the formal verification tools that provide mathematical guarantees of correctness remain expensive and restrictively licensed. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for hardware design, existing approaches to RTL repair validate their results through simulation - which exercises only a subset of inputs - or rely on commercial tools, and few combine formal proof with an entirely open-source toolchain. In this paper, we present a multi-agent pipeline that couples an LLM with an open-source formal backend (Yosys, SymbiYosys, and Z3) to repair RTL through counterexample-guided iteration: the framework generates formal properties, verifies the design, and feeds counterexamples back to the LLM until the design is proved correct by k-induction or an iteration budget is exhausted. Through an ALU case study, we show that the pipeline can detect and repair a real functional bug with a formal proof of correctness. Across a six-benchmark suite, one design is repaired reliably, and we characterize four distinct failure modes: bounded-cover vacuity, specification ambiguity, temporal-logic bugs, and multi-property pressure. We frame this work as a feasibility study with a detailed failure analysis, and additionally report a practical limitation of the Yosys bind directive relevant to the open-source formal verification community.

</details>


### [131] [Selective KV Cache Protection for Noise-Resilient LLM Inference on Analog Compute-In-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29076)
*Yuannuo Feng,Wenyong Zhou,Yuang Ma,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Yuxin Xie,Ngai Wong,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了模拟CIM阵列上的动态注意力计算，揭示了首位token和近期token对硬件噪声的脆弱性，并提出了一种分层token保护策略，将sink token和滑动近期窗口放在高精度数字路径上处理，其余KV cache在模拟CIM上处理，将困惑度从33.91降至11.95（接近无噪声基线的11.06），并将编程行利用率从23.1%提升至91.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟CIM阵列已展现出高效LLM推理的潜力，尤其适合线性层中的权重固定计算。然而，将模拟CIM扩展到注意力机制面临根本性挑战：KV cache需要重复的in-situ权重更新，这与权重固定范式不匹配，导致动态计算暴露在显著的硬件噪声下，而这一问题此前几乎没有被探索。

Method: 提出分层token保护策略：将sink tokens和滑动近期token窗口保留在高精度数字路径上处理，将大量KV cache放在模拟CIM上处理。同时设计了一个协同调度器，结合模拟编程、所有权转换和批量MVM tile形成来限制数字路径的开销。

Result: 在9个LLM上的评估显示，该方案将模拟噪声下的平均困惑度从33.91降至11.95，接近无噪声基线的11.06，同时将动态KV编程行利用率从23.1%提升至91.2%。

Conclusion: 通过token级的脆弱性洞察和分层保护架构，成功弥合了模拟CIM在动态注意力计算中的精度差距，使模拟CIM能够高效且可靠地支持LLM推理中的注意力机制。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) arrays have emerged as a promising substrate for energy-efficient LLM inference, particularly for weight-stationary computations in linear layers. However, extending analog CIM to attention mechanisms introduces a fundamental challenge: KV cache operations demand repeated in-situ weight updates, and the resulting mismatch with the weight-stationary paradigm exposes dynamic computations to significant hardware noise, a critical problem that remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of dynamic attention computation on analog CIM arrays, revealing that initial and recent tokens exhibit disproportionate vulnerability to hardware noise. Motivated by this token-level insight, we propose a hierarchical token protection strategy that keeps sink tokens and a sliding recent-token window on a higher-precision digital path while processing the bulk KV cache on analog CIM. A co-designed scheduler combines analog programming, ownership transition, and bulk-MVM tile formation to bound digital overhead. Evaluations on nine LLMs show that our approach lowers average perplexity under analog noise from 33.91 to 11.95, close to the clean baseline of 11.06, while improving dynamic-KV programming-row utilization from 23.1\% to 91.2\%.

</details>


### [132] [RTLCurator: Label-Efficient Data Curation for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29283)
*Siyang Cai,Cangyuan Li,Wenjing Chang,Kun Wang,Haoyu Gao,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RTLCurator，一种用于从合成RTL数据集中筛选高质量数据的方法。它通过对比规范与仿真失败的实现学习行为感知的兼容性先验，并用少量验证对校准到新语料库，最终通过平衡对齐性、表示覆盖率和RTL结构丰富度来构建保留子集。在CodeV和RTLCoder上，保留80%语料即在所有指标上优于全量训练，且仅需验证10%的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM生成RTL代码需要大规模的规范-代码配对数据，但此类数据稀缺，多数公开语料由合成生成。合成虽然提供规模但缺乏正确性——两个广泛使用的RTL数据集中仅有24.4%和53.5%的配对能通过功能测试。仅按正确性过滤效果差：一个在边界情况下出错的对仍展示有效的语法和接口约定，而复杂的时序设计更难生成和验证，因此按正确性过滤会留下短而简单模块组成的语料。此外正确性难以获得，RTL行为在表面留下的痕迹很少，验证整个语料库只能将数据分为通过和失败两类。

Method: RTLCurator首先通过将每个规范与仿真失败的实现进行对比，学习一个行为感知的兼容性先验；接着使用少量已验证的配对将该先验校准到新语料库；最后通过平衡对齐性（alignment）、表示覆盖（representation coverage）和RTL结构丰富度（structural richness）三个维度来构建保留子集。

Result: 在CodeV和RTLCoder两个数据集上，仅验证10%的数据池，保留80%的语料即可在所有报告指标上优于使用全量语料训练的效果。相比之下，仅按分数排序会低于随机选择，而通过仿真过滤整个数据池也没有更好表现。

Conclusion: 基于正确性的过滤不是RTL合成数据筛选的有效策略；需要行为感知、覆盖面和结构丰富度的综合考量。RTLCurator表明在有限验证预算下可以有策略地构建高质量训练子集，同时证明了数据质量选择对RTL代码生成任务的重要性。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) to write register-transfer level (RTL) requires large corpora of paired specifications and code, and such data is scarce enough that most public corpora are now synthesized. Synthesis provides scale but not correctness, and in two widely used RTL datasets only 24.4% and 53.5% of pairs pass generated functional tests. This raises the question of how much of such a corpus to keep and which part of it. Correctness alone is a poor answer. A pair that misbehaves in one corner case still shows valid syntax and interface conventions, and complex sequential designs are both harder to generate and harder to validate, so filtering by correctness leaves a corpus of short and simple modules. Correctness is also hard to obtain, since behavior leaves little trace on the surface in RTL, and validating an entire corpus only sorts pairs into passed and failed. We present RTLCurator, which learns a behavior-aware compatibility prior by contrasting each specification with implementations that fail simulation, and calibrates it to a new corpus using a small number of validated pairs. It then constructs the retained subset by balancing alignment, representation coverage, and RTL structural richness. On CodeV and RTLCoder, keeping 80% of the corpus this way improves on training with the full corpus across all reported metrics while validating only 10% of the pool, whereas ranking by the score alone falls below random selection and filtering the whole pool by simulation does no better.

</details>


### [133] [Low-Power PLL-Based Clock Stabilization for Flexible IGZO AMS Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29357)
*Paula Carolina Lozano Duarte,Georgios Zervakis,Mehdi Tahoori*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个专为n型a-IGZO TFT柔性电子技术设计的锁相环（PLL）架构，作为低带宽时间稳定器而非高精度频率合成器，通过最小电荷泵反馈环对自由振荡的环形振荡器VCO进行软调节，以约束长期频率漂移。该PLL支持1 kHz至300 kHz频率，功耗0.115-0.153 mW，面积0.0115-0.0233 mm²，相较先前振荡器方案功耗降低400倍以上，面积缩减1500倍以上（相对柔性VCO），并在四个代表性IGZO AMS系统中实现2.24 ns的rms周期抖动和1000 ppm以内的长期频率精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性电子平台中的模拟与混合信号电路（生物传感器、读出前端、模数转换器）主导功能与能耗，片上时钟生成是功耗关键环节。现有振荡器方案存在无界PVT漂移导致信号完整性退化，而替代时钟源可消耗高达90%的总系统功耗，无法适用于柔性电子平台，因此亟需既能提供参考锚定时钟稳定性又具备极低功耗和极小面积开销的片上时钟生成方案。

Method: 针对无p型器件、低载流子迁移率与强PVT变异的FE特定约束，设计了一种面向n型a-IGZO TFT技术的PLL架构。该设计不追求高精度频率合成，而是作为低带宽时间稳定器：自由运行的环形振荡器VCO由最小电荷泵反馈回路软调节，以限制长期频率漂移，且无需连续的高质量外部参考源。

Result: 所提PLL支持1 kHz至300 kHz频率范围，占用0.0115-0.0233 mm²，功耗0.115-0.153 mW。相比先前振荡器方案功耗降低400倍以上；相比柔性VCO面积缩减超过1500倍，相比环形振荡器方案缩减超过390倍。在四个代表性IGZO AMS系统中验证，rms周期抖动为2.24 ns，长期频率精度在1000 ppm以内。

Conclusion: 该PLL架构为柔性电子平台提供了参考锚定的时钟稳定性，以极低的功耗和面积开销解决了FE中传统振荡器PVT漂移与替代时钟源高功耗的双重矛盾，开辟了面向a-IGZO TFT技术的锁相环设计新方向。

Abstract: Flexible electronics (FE) platforms rely on analog and mixed-signal (AMS) circuits - biosensors, readout front-ends, and analog-to-digital converters - that dominate both functionality and energy consumption, making on-chip clock generation an essential yet power-critical function. Existing oscillator-based solutions suffer from unbounded process, voltage, and temperature (PVT) drift that degrades signal integrity, while alternative clock sources can consume up to 90% of the total system power budget, rendering them inapplicable to FE platforms and elevating clock generation to a primary power and energy-efficiency design constraint. This paper presents the first phase-locked loop (PLL) architecture designed for n-type-only amorphous indium-gallium-zinc oxide (a-IGZO) thin-film transistor (TFT) technology, addressing FE-specific constraints such as the absence of p-type devices, limited carrier mobility, and strong PVT variability. Rather than targeting high-precision frequency synthesis, the proposed design operates as a low-bandwidth temporal stabilizer: a free-running ring-oscillator-based voltage-controlled oscillator (VCO) is softly regulated by a minimal charge-pump feedback loop to bound long-term frequency drift without requiring a continuous high-quality external reference. The proposed PLL supports frequencies from 1 kHz to 300 kHz while occupying 0.0115-0.0233 mm2 and consuming 0.115-0.153 mW. Compared with prior oscillator-based FE clocking solutions, our architecture reduces power by more than 400x while achieving footprint reductions exceeding 1500x compared to flexible VCOs, and more than 390x with respect to ring-oscillator solutions. Validated across four representative published IGZO AMS systems, the proposed PLL achieves an rms period jitter of 2.24 ns and a long-term frequency accuracy within 1000 ppm, providing reference-anchored clock stability in FE platforms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [DeltaServe: Host-Agnostic Co-Serving of Inference and Fine-Tuning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28848)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Jianshu She,Ye Yuan,Rajat Ghosh,Karan Gupta,Qirong Ho,Xue Liu,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DeltaServe是一种主机无关的共驻（co-serving）设计，将LLM推理服务在非峰值时闲置的GPU算力转化为LoRA微调吞吐量，同时通过SLO感知调度器保证推理服务质量目标不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理系统按峰值负载配置GPU资源以满足严格延迟目标，导致流量低于峰值时大量GPU算力闲置。现有方案要么对闲置资源利用率不足，要么在共存推理与微调任务时违反推理SLO。

Method: DeltaServe通过紧凑的钩子接口集成现有推理引擎（仅需多LoRA批处理支持），利用推理prefill与LoRA微调前向传播的共享执行结构，采用SLO感知调度器在推理余量充足时才准入并执行微调任务。调度器由离线校准、在线细化的CUDA-graph感知延迟模型驱动。

Result: 在Company X的生产流量轨迹上，DeltaServe在vLLM上实现2.9倍于LLMStation的微调吞吐量，推理SLO合规率达100%（LLMStation为85%）；相比vLLM+torchtune基线，微调吞吐量提高39%，无需额外硬件且保持完全SLO合规。

Conclusion: DeltaServe证明了闲置推理算力可以被高效转化为LoRA微调能力，在不违反推理SLO的前提下显著提升GPU资源整体利用率，且具备跨多种主流推理引擎（vLLM、SGLang、S-LoRA）的通用性。

Abstract: LLM serving systems are provisioned for peak load to meet strict latency targets, leaving substantial GPU compute idle whenever traffic falls below peak. We present DeltaServe, a host-agnostic co-serving design that converts this idle inference capacity into LoRA fine-tuning throughput while preserving inference service-level objectives (SLOs).
