DeltaServe integrates with existing inference engines through a compact hook interface that requires only multi-LoRA batching support. It exploits the shared execution structure of inference prefill and LoRA fine-tuning forward passes, and uses an SLO-aware scheduler to admit and execute fine-tuning only when sufficient inference headroom is available. The scheduler is driven by a CUDA-graph-aware latency model calibrated offline and refined online. We integrate DeltaServe with vLLM, SGLang, and S-LoRA. On a production trace from Company X, DeltaServe on vLLM delivers 2.9x higher fine-tuning throughput than LLMStation at 100% inference SLO compliance, versus 85% for LLMStation. It also achieves 39% higher fine-tuning throughput than a baseline running vLLM+torchtune, using no additional hardware and maintaining full SLO compliance.

</details>


### [135] [LayoutBench: Performance Benchmarking of Cloud Storage Layouts for Multimedia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28880)
*Debopam Sanyal,Hongjie Chen,Alexey Tumanov,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LayoutBench是首个系统评估多媒体机器学习数据存储布局性能的基准测试工具，对比了三种布局策略（单个对象L1、tar打包L2、Parquet列式L3）在云对象存储上的检索延迟、数据传输量和货币成本，发现L2在小规模检索时最快而L3在大规模检索时最快但成本高出一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多媒体机器学习工作负载将大规模数据集存储在云对象存储服务中，样本的物理存储布局直接影响数据检索的速度和成本。然而现有基准测试主要面向数据库引擎和查询处理，缺乏对多媒体数据检索在不同存储布局下性能的系统评估。

Method: 提出LayoutBench基准测试框架，评估三种代表性布局策略：每个样本作为独立对象存储（L1）、样本顺序打包进tar归档文件（L2）、样本以列式存储在Parquet文件中（L3）。在ImageNet数据集上使用11种不同返回集大小的查询，跨越6种具有不同网络带宽和内存层级的AWS EC2实例配置，测量检索时间、数据传输量和货币成本。

Result: 实验结果表明：L2通过连接复用实现比L1和L3更低的延迟，但在检索规模非常大时失去这一优势；L3在超大规模检索时速度最快，但由于行组粒度导致所有查询规模下传输的数据量都显著更多，且需要更多内存；在所有布局中数据传输成本占主导，L3的成本比L1或L2高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: 不同存储布局在多媒体数据检索中各有优劣：L2适合中小规模检索场景，L3适合大规模检索但成本和内存开销高，L1在最简单场景下具有竞争力。存储布局的选择应权衡检索延迟、数据转移量和成本之间的关系。

Abstract: Modern multimedia machine learning workloads increasingly store large-scale datasets in cloud object storage services such as AWS S3. How these samples are physically organized in storage (i.e.,storage layout) directly affects how quickly and cheaply they can be retrieved. Yet the benchmarks used to guide storage decisions today focus on database engines and query processing, and none systematically evaluates how different storage layouts perform for multimedia data retrieval. We present LayoutBench, the first benchmark designed to fill this gap. It evaluates three representative layout strategies: storing each sample as an individual object (L1), sequentially packing samples into tar archives (L2), and organizing samples as columns in Parquet files (L3). We measure retrieval time, data transferred, and monetary cost using 11 queries of varying result-set sizes on ImageNet across six AWS EC2 instance configurations that span different network bandwidth and memory tiers. Our experiments reveal that L2 achieves lower latency than L1 and L3 through connection reuse, but loses this advantage as retrieval sizes become very large. L3 is the fastest for very large retrievals but transfers substantially more data across all query sizes due to row-group granularity, and requires significantly more memory. Across all layouts, data transfer cost dominates total expenditure, with L3 costing an order of magnitude more than L1 or L2.

</details>


### [136] [Rethinking AI Cloud Infrastructure for Agentic Serving Systems with the Aries Experimentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29069)
*Leonid Kondrashov,Hongrui Liu,JooYoung Park,Boxi Zhou,Zonghao Liu,Chengzhi Lu,Riccardo Mancini,Esha Choukse,Haris Javaid,German Sviridov,Tao Peng,Chen Zhao,Anastasia Avdeeva,Aleksei Gusev,Marios Kogias,Luo Mai,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Aries，一个全栈实验框架，用于分离任务语义与执行配置、重构跨组件的Agent轨迹并关联系统遥测，以及通过统一接口管理异构沙箱中的状态化工具执行。基于Aries的实证研究发现：token中心指标会遗漏非推理瓶颈、额外上下文带来递减的精度收益却降低服务容量、工具沙箱呈现长空闲与短资源突发的交替模式且快照式状态管理导致激进挂起成本高昂，并由此提出Agent原生服务系统的设计愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主Agent通过重复推理、持久上下文和沙箱化工具执行的组合方式给传统LLM服务带来了新的挑战，而现有系统缺乏能够将任务语义与执行配置分离、并跨组件重构Agent轨迹与系统遥测关联的实验框架，导致低层系统研究缺乏可复现的实证基础和生产数据支撑。

Method: 提出Aries全栈实验框架，包含三个核心能力：(1) 分离任务语义与执行配置；(2) 重构跨组件Agent轨迹并关联相关系统遥测；(3) 通过一致接口在异构沙箱子系统上暴露状态化工具执行。同时结合公开Agent基准上的可复现实验与商业平台的生产轨迹数据进行互补分析，并开展补充安全性分析。

Result: 研究发现：(1) token中心指标无法捕获非推理瓶颈；(2) 保留更多上下文带来递减的准确率收益但同时降低服务容量；(3) 工具沙箱在长时间空闲与短时资源突发之间交替，而基于快照的状态管理使激进的沙箱挂起策略代价高昂。安全性分析进一步表明需要减少沙箱攻击面。

Conclusion: 基于实证发现，提出Agent原生服务系统的愿景，围绕以下设计方向：轨迹级指标（trajectory-level metrics）、自适应上下文管理（adaptive context management）、弹性沙箱资源管理（elastic sandbox resource management）以及最小化攻击面的沙箱设计。

Abstract: Autonomous agents challenge conventional LLM serving by coupling repeated inference with persistent context and sandboxed tool execution. We present Aries, a full-stack experimentation framework that separates task semantics from execution configurations, reconstructs cross-component agent trajectories with correlated system telemetry, and exposes stateful tool execution through a consistent interface across heterogeneous sandbox substrates. We use Aries to conduct reproducible experiments on open agent harnesses and benchmarks. We complement these experiments with production traces from a commercial platform, grounding low-level systems research in observed production behavior. Our results show that (1) token-centric metrics miss non-inference bottlenecks, (2) retaining additional context yields diminishing accuracy benefits while reducing serving capacity, and (3) tool sandboxes alternate between long idle periods and short resource bursts, while current snapshot-based state management makes aggressive suspension costly. A complementary security analysis further highlights the need to reduce the sandbox attack surface. We then discuss the vision for agent-native serving systems designed around trajectory-level metrics, adaptive context management, elastic sandbox resource management, and sandboxes with minimized attack surface.

</details>


### [137] [Knox: Fortifying Smart Spaces With Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29198)
*Rishabh Menezes,Jadon T. Schuler,Kaimeng Zhu,Oliver Rogalski,Indranil Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Knox是首个为IoT智能空间提供安全检测的系统，通过新的安全规范语言和两种静态安全检测算法，在NP难问题背景下实现了高效且可解释的安全违规检测，显著降低了检测时间并保持了高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能空间和建筑中的IoT设备构成了一类具有关键安全需求的分布式系统，但此前缺乏对其进行安全检测的系统化方法。

Method: Knox提出：(i) 一种用于智能空间安全子句的新规约语言；(ii) 两种用于在智能空间运行的多个例程上静态验证多重安全属性的新算法；同时提出了进一步降低运行时的优化策略。

Result: 由于该问题为NP难问题，作者提出了新颖且可解释的算法。实验结果表明，Knox与基线方法相比显著缩短了检测时间，同时在捕获安全违规方面保持了高准确率。

Conclusion: Knox是第一个支持IoT智能空间安全检测的系统，通过静态分析与优化算法，在可解释性的前提下实现了高效的安全违规检测。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices in smart spaces and buildings are an emerging class of distributed systems with critical safety requirements. This paper presents Knox, the first system to enable safety checking in IoT-enabled smart spaces. Knox's contributions include (i) safety specifications: a new language for safety clauses in such smart spaces, and (ii) static safety checking: two new algorithms for static verification of multiple safety properties across multiple routines running inside a smart space. Since the latter problem is NP-hard, we present and analyze novel and explainable algorithms for the static version of the problem. We also present optimizations that further reduce runtime. Our analysis and experimental results with real datasets show that Knox reduces checking time significantly compared to baselines, while providing high accuracy in catching safety violations.

</details>


### [138] [Allocation Tracking and Parameter Checking for Parallel Programming Models using Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29303)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了CoVer框架的契约语言，引入通用参数检查和分配追踪功能，以捕获更广泛的错误类别，在保持跨编程模型和跨语言通用性的同时，显著提升了代码正确性检查的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算程序的正确性检查工具通常局限于特定的并行编程模型（如MPI或OpenSHMEM）。CoVer框架此前通过基于契约的通用方法解耦了API需求与核心工具，但其有效性受限于底层契约语言的表达力，无法验证更多类型的错误。

Method: 对CoVer的契约语言进行扩展，新增通用参数检查（generic parameter checking）和分配追踪（allocation tracking）两大功能，同时保持跨编程模型和跨语言的通用性。

Result: 实验评估表明，扩展后的框架在多种语言上分析准确性保持一致，显著提升了CoVer的实用性和准确性，尽管额外的运行时分析带来了性能开销。

Conclusion: 通过契约语言扩展，CoVer能够检查更广泛的错误类别，在保持框架通用性的前提下大幅提升了正确性检查的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Correctness checking tools for High-Performance Computing programs are typically limited to specific parallel programming models such as MPI or OpenSHMEM. The CoVer framework previously addressed this by introducing a generic, contract-based approach that decoupled API requirements from the core tool. However, CoVer's effectiveness remains bounded by the expressiveness of its underlying contract language, restricting the types of errors it can verify. This paper presents an extension to the CoVer contract language designed to capture and check a broader range of error classes. Our extensions introduce generic parameter checking and allocation tracking, while keeping generality across both programming model and language. We evaluate these extensions and demonstrate that analysis accuracy remains consistent across multiple languages, reinforcing the framework's general applicability. While the additional runtime analyses naturally incur a performance overhead, these improvements greatly enhance CoVer's utility with a significant accuracy improvement.

</details>


### [139] [CARA: Exact Local Repair with Fresh One-Action Certification for Cloud Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29465)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA)，一种基于基线的仿真指导放置管道，将自适应候选生成与一次性部署决策分离，通过配对二值/固定下注证书顺序在有限邻域内恢复精确的top-P动作，并使用全新配对认证保证四向改进的假阳性率不超过0.05。在128个独立合成环境的前瞻性冻结研究中，该策略在所有五个评估端点上全面改进，J下降3.57个百分点，连续负担降低21-28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仿真器的放置管道在部署前会检查大量修复方案，但仅在多个可靠性指标同时改善时才会部署。存在两个核心问题：(1) 重用搜索场景测试所选动作会使名义证据失效；(2) 标量化（scalarization）可能牺牲最弱的可靠性准则。因此需要一种既能高效搜索、又能保证统计有效性的部署决策框架。

Method: CARA采用锚定于当前基线的流水线，将自适应候选生成与一次性部署决策分离。核心方法包括：(1) 在受限的双主机邻域内，利用打包特定（packing-specific）的容许界在混合配对二值/固定下注证书顺序下恢复精确的top-P不同动作；(2) 使用留出视图选择一个动作并提交其投注计划，然后开启全新的配对认证（Certification）；(3) 在符号独立性与条件矩假设下，控制四向改进的误报概率至多0.05。

Result: 在前瞻性冻结研究中，覆盖128个独立合成环境和四个重复上下文，完整的fail-closed终端策略在所有五个评估端点上均优于开发时选择的同主机数量基线。J值下降3.57个百分点，连续负担降幅为21-28%。配对的排序敏感性分析显示绝对平均差距低于5.3×10^-4，未建立顺序优越性。

Conclusion: CARA将精确可审计的候选恢复（exact auditable proposal recovery）与选择鲁棒的fresh certification相结合，解决末端局部修复(last-mile local repair)问题。该方法在保证统计有效性的同时维持了上游搜索的灵活性与规模无关性，为模拟指导部署提供了可靠的决策框架。

Abstract: Simulator-based placement pipelines may inspect many repairs but deploy only when several reliability criteria improve together. Reusing search scenes to test the selected action invalidates nominal evidence, while scalarization can trade away the weakest criterion. We introduce Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA), an incumbent-anchored pipeline that separates adaptive proposal generation from a one-use deployment decision. In a bounded two-host neighborhood, a packing-specific admissible bound recovers the exact top-$P$ distinct actions under a mixed paired-binary/fixed-bet certificate order. Held-out views then select one action and commit its betting plans before fresh paired Certification opens. Under the stated sign-independence and conditional-moment assumptions, the probability of falsely declaring four-way improvement is at most $.05$, irrespective of the size or complexity of upstream search. In a prospectively frozen study over 128 independent synthetic environments and four repeated contexts, the complete fail-closed terminal policy improved a development-selected same-host-count incumbent on all five Evaluation endpoints in every environment. $J$ fell by 3.57 percentage points and the continuous burdens by 21--28%. A matched ordering sensitivity produced absolute mean gaps below $5.3\times10^{-4}$ and does not establish order superiority. CARA thus couples exact auditable proposal recovery to selection-robust fresh certification for last-mile local repair.

</details>


### [140] [System-Wide Termination in Distributed Betweenness Centrality Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29474)
*Siamak Abdi,Lucia Cavallaro,Giuseppe Di Fatta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分布式介数中心性计算的轻量级全局终止检测算法，使顶点能够在检测到系统级收敛后安全停止，相比局部停止策略避免了在异构网络上的过早终止和误差。实验在合成与真实网络上验证了该方法零最终误差的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模网络上计算介数中心性代价高昂，现有分布式实现缺乏轻量级的系统级终止检测机制，导致本地收敛后产生多余消息，或配置不当导致过早停止。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的系统级全局终止检测算法，使顶点能够基于局部信息自主判断整个系统是否已全局收敛；并通过自定义Python模拟器，将全局终止检测与局部停止策略在Erdos-Renyi、几何、Email和Road网络上进行对比评估。

Result: 全局终止检测方法在所有测试网络上均实现零最终误差，使顶点能在检测到全局收敛后安全停止；而局部停止策略在异构网络上会导致过早终止并产生误差。

Conclusion: 在分布式介数中心性计算中，需要协调统一的终止机制而非依赖各顶点独立的局部收敛判断，以确保计算的正确性和完整性。

Abstract: Computing betweenness centrality on large networks is inherently expensive, as it requires aggregating shortest-path dependencies across all pairs of vertices and becomes increasingly difficult to scale as network size grows. Scalable distributed algorithms can facilitate such computations, particularly when centralised processing is not feasible, and message exchanges must be carefully controlled, for example, in bandwidth-limited or very large-scale networks. However, existing distributed betweenness centrality implementations do not integrate a lightweight, system-wide termination detector. As a consequence, this can lead to extra messaging after local convergence or, if misconfigured, premature stops. In this work, a lightweight, system-wide global termination detection algorithm for this task is presented. The proposed method enables vertices to decide locally when the overall system has converged. The method is evaluated against a local stopping strategy in which vertices terminate individually once their own estimates stabilise. To compare these two approaches, namely global termination detection and local stopping, a custom Python simulator is implemented, and both approaches are tested on synthetic (Erdos-Renyi and geometric) and real (Email and Road) network topologies. Our results show that system-wide termination detection lets vertices stop safely after detecting global convergence, as indicated by zero final error in the evaluated networks, rather than stopping independently based only on local convergence. The local stopping approach, on the other hand, results in premature termination and some errors on heterogeneous networks. This work emphasises the need for coordinated halting in distributed centrality computation.

</details>


### [141] [SLIM: Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Modeling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29575)
*Pol G. Recasens,Ferran Agullo,Yue Zhu,Chen Wang,Jordi Torres,Josep Ll. Berral*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving commonly increases batch size to improve throughput, but performance eventually reaches a deployment-dependent plateau beyond which larger batches provide marginal gains while increasing latency and GPU memory consumption. Previous studies have attributed this behavior to HBM/DRAM bandwidth limitations, but the underlying causes have primarily been supported by conceptual arguments or high-level performance observations. As our first contribution, we present a detailed GPU characterization using hardware profiling techniques, demonstrating that throughput saturation originates in the attention kernels during the decode phase. Specifically, we show that their nearly constant arithmetic intensity as active-context lengths increases -not merely larger batch sizes- drives DRAM-bandwidth saturation, while the achieved compute throughput remains far below the hardware limit. Building on this analysis, we present the Batching Configuration Advisor (BCA), which selects the highest-throughput batching configuration satisfying a target latency constraint and identifies up to 55 GB of GPU memory allocation that can be avoided for the evaluated OPT models with minimal throughput loss. To enable these recommendations, we introduce SLIM (Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Model), a semi-analytical model that predicts LLM inference throughput and latency from analytical formulations of Transformer computation and memory traffic. Across the evaluated scenarios, SLIM outperforms representative performance-modeling baselines while successfully generalizing to previously unseen operating conditions.

</details>

### [142] [Geographically Weighted Surrogate Models for Rapid Small-Area Chronic Disease Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28655)
*Aanya Gupta,Szandra Péter,Sara Von Hoene,Emma Von Hoene,Taylor Anderson*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文评估了机器学习模型作为小区域估计（SAE）替代方法的可行性，通过高频更新的区域级预测变量学习现有SAE输出的关系，以生成调查数据缺失或延迟年份的及时估计。研究发现地理加权机器学习框架（如地理加权随机森林和地理加权回归）能够作为可扩展的开放数据替代方案，快速生成美国县级十种慢性病的SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于调查的SAE产品存在固有滞后性，如CDC PLACES这类黄金标准估计通常在调查数据收集后约两年才发布，限制了其用于时间敏感的决策。因此需要一种能够生成及时、可比较估计的方法来弥补这一时间差。

Method: 评估了多种全局和地理加权机器学习模型，用于美国县级十种慢性病（慢阻肺、哮喘、心脏病、关节炎、癌症、抑郁症、糖尿病、高血压、高胆固醇和中风）的小区域估计。模型利用高频更新的区域级预测变量学习与现有SAE输出之间的关系。

Result: 地理加权机器学习框架（地理加权随机森林和地理加权回归）表现优于全局模型，能够作为可扩展且开放的数据替代方案，快速生成SAE以支持数据驱动的决策。

Conclusion: 地理加权机器学习方法能够有效弥合传统SAE的发布滞后问题，为公共卫生决策提供及时、可扩展的小区域估计手段。

Abstract: Small-area estimation (SAE) enables researchers and policymakers to identify spatial disparities in health outcomes, but survey-based SAE products carry an inherent lag. Gold-standard estimates such as CDC PLACES are released roughly two years after the underlying survey data are collected, limiting their use for time-sensitive decision-making. This study evaluates the potential for machine learning (ML) to serve as a surrogate, learning the relationship between frequently updated area-level predictors and existing SAE outputs to generate timely, comparable estimates in years when SAE from surveys are unavailable or delayed. We evaluate several global and geographically weighted ML models for county-level SAE of ten chronic conditions across the US: COPD, asthma, heart disease, arthritis, cancer, depression, diabetes, high blood pressure, high cholesterol, and stroke. Our findings suggest that geographically weighted ML frameworks like geographically weighted random forest and geographically weighted regression offer scalable and open data surrogates for rapidly generating SAE and supporting data driven decision making.

</details>


### [143] [SymPerturb converts symptom-network structure into testable intervention priorities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28673)
*Zheng Zhu,Junwen Yu,Tiantian Hu,Zhongfang Yang,Jiaqing Wang*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SymPerturb，一个虚拟扰动框架，用于区分四种基本的扰动算子（虚拟敲除、虚拟敲低、边级通信阻断和节点中心通信阻断）与三种分析流程（虚拟剂量扰动、组合扰动和序列优化），并定义了虚拟扰动优先级分数（VPPS）来对症状网络中的潜在干预靶点进行排序，旨在生成可供纵向和实验验证的审计性靶点假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 症状网络虽然能编码条件依赖关系，但本身无法识别因果或临床上可操作的干预靶点。现有方法缺乏区分不同扰动类型和量化干预效果的统一框架，亟需一种可审计的虚拟扰动方法来生成干预靶点假设，以指导纵向研究和实验验证。

Method: 提出了SymPerturb框架：（1）定义四种原始扰动算子（虚拟敲除、虚拟敲低、边级阻断、节点中心阻断）和三种分析流程（剂量扰动、组合扰动、序列优化）；（2）将参考高斯实现嵌入一般的定位-尺度映射中，具有症状特异性靶点锚点，明确零锚定和均值-方差联动衰减是建模选择；（3）定义了七个效用指标（下游疗效、剂量效率、广度、跨模块覆盖、通信阻断、组合值和响应性），其方向对齐、候选集内加权均值构成VPPS分数，作为相对排序而非可转移的临床效用分数。

Result: 在已知的22节点、四模块生成网络中，解析疗效与10万次蒙特卡洛模拟估计的差异在0.0024个标准差以内。有限样本VPPS结果是在原始八组件探索性评分下生成的，需在修订的七效用维度定义下重新生成。这些模拟提供了模型兼容性下的内部计算验证，而非因果或外部验证。

Conclusion: SymPerturb旨在生成可审计的靶点假设以供纵向和实验测试，但其报告的结果需要根据修订后的七效用维度重新生成，且当前的验证仅属于内部计算验证，不具备因果或外部验证效力。

Abstract: Symptom networks encode conditional dependence but do not by themselves identify causal or clinically actionable intervention targets. We introduce SymPerturb, a virtual-perturbation framework that distinguishes four primitive perturbation operators - virtual knockout, virtual knockdown, edge-level communication blocking and node-centred communication blocking - from three analytic procedures - virtual dosage perturbation, combination perturbation and sequence optimisation. The reference Gaussian implementation is embedded in a general location-scale map with symptom-specific target anchors, making explicit that zero anchoring and linked mean-variance attenuation are modelling choices. Seven utility outcomes quantify downstream efficacy, dose efficiency, breadth, cross-module reach, communication blocking, combination value and responsiveness; robustness is reported separately as an uncertainty diagnostic. Their direction-aligned, within-candidate-set weighted mean defines the virtual perturbation priority score (VPPS), which is a relative ranking rather than a transportable clinical utility score. In a known 22-node, four-module generating network, analytical efficacy agreed with 100,000-draw Monte Carlo estimates within 0.0024 standard deviations. The reported finite-sample VPPS results were generated with the original eight-component exploratory score and therefore require regeneration under the revised seven-utility-dimension definition. These simulations provide internal computational verification under model compatibility, not causal or external validation. SymPerturb is intended to generate auditable target hypotheses for longitudinal and experimental testing.

</details>


### [144] [EasyBCI Agent: Towards Universal Neural Data Preprocessing for Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29007)
*Yu Zhu,Runkai Zhao,Zhimin Zhou,Jinyu Cai,Zhouheng Yao,Chutian Zhang,Peiyuan Li,Xiaoxing Zhang,Qihao Zheng,Jiamin Wu,Mianxin Liu,Chi Zhang,Bin Min,Lei Bai,Chengyu Li,Jingpeng Wu,Chunfeng Song*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: EasyBCI是一个两阶段大语言模型（LLM）代理系统，用于脑机接口（BCI）神经信号的自动化预处理。它通过Plan Agent将原始数据转化为不含敏感数据的文本Data Fingerprint并选择基于文献的算子序列，Execution Agent生成、运行和自纠正代码直到满足质量标准，同时通过质量门控的经验系统保留可复用的策略。系统在EEG上与手动管线相比保留了更多任务相关可分性，并在多种神经信号模态上生成完整、可复现、具有决策溯源记录的预处理管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口的性能高度依赖预处理环节，但现有预处理流程仍是手工、依赖专家且难以复现的。虽然大语言模型代理可以自动化科学编程，但现有系统缺乏神经信号预处理所需的模态覆盖范围、原始数据隔离、经验积累和领域监督能力。

Method: EasyBCI采用两阶段架构：(1) Plan Agent对每个记录生成仅含文本的Data Fingerprint（不向模型暴露原始数据），并选择基于文献的算子序列；(2) Execution Agent生成、运行并自我纠正代码直到满足质量标准。系统还包含质量门控的经验系统用于保留经过验证的策略作为可复用技能，并在两个决策关口引入领域专家干预以保留人工判断。

Result: 在EEG上使用固定线性分类器评估，所有五个EasyBCI骨干网络比手动管线保留了更多任务相关可分性。在相同骨干比较下，EasyBCI在两种标签方案中的四种配置上优于通用编码代理。EasyBCI还能扩展到另外五种模态，覆盖近三个数量级的采样率范围，生成完整可复现的管线并记录决策溯源。

Conclusion: 结果表明，领域特定的编排能够使可审计的预处理对缺乏专门专业知识的实验室也可用，并展示了适用于其他科学领域中AI代理的设计原则。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces translate neural activity into device commands, yet their performance hinges on preprocessing that remains manual, expert-dependent and poorly reproducible. Large language model agents can automate scientific coding, but existing systems lack the modality coverage, raw-data isolation, experience accumulation and domain oversight that neural preprocessing requires. We introduce EasyBCI, a two-phase LLM agent that plans and executes preprocessing pipelines for six signal types. A Plan Agent profiles each recording into a text-only Data Fingerprint that never exposes raw data to the model and selects a literature-grounded operator sequence. An Execution Agent generates, runs and self-corrects code until quality criteria are met, while a quality-gated experience system retains validated strategies as reusable skills. A domain expert intervenes at two decision gates, retaining human judgement where undetected error can invalidate downstream analyses. Evaluation on EEG with a fixed linear classifier shows that all five EasyBCI backbones preserve more task-relevant separability than the manual pipeline. Under same-backbone comparison, EasyBCI outperforms general-purpose coding agents on both label schemes for four of five configurations. EasyBCI extends to five additional modalities spanning nearly three orders of magnitude in sampling rate, producing complete reproducible pipelines with recorded decision provenance. These results indicate that domain-specific orchestration can bring auditable preprocessing within reach of laboratories lacking dedicated expertise, illustrating design principles applicable to AI agents in other scientific domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Advances, challenges, and opportunities for legged robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28952)
*Jonas Frey,Matías Mattamala,Hae-Won Park,Mayank Mittal,Georg Martius,Maike Osborne,Robert Sparrow,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综合评估了人形和四足机器人在硬件、运动控制、自主性、数据和应用等方面的当前能力，识别了关键开放挑战，并展望了腿式机器人的未来发展趋势，包括伦理、经济、政策和社会影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人（人形和四足）有潜力改变人类与智能机器的工作、交互和共存方式，需要系统性地评估其当前能力现状，以理解其社会影响并推动科学发现。

Method: 采用综合评估方法，从硬件、运动能力（locomotion）、自主性（autonomy）、数据集和实际应用五个维度系统梳理腿式机器人的现有能力，并在此基础上识别关键开放挑战和未来研究方向。

Result: 识别了腿式机器人当前所取得的最新进展以及实现广泛部署和新用例所必须克服的关键开放挑战，同时从伦理、经济、政策和社会的多角度提供了未来展望。

Conclusion: 腿式机器人在多维度上展现出巨大潜力，但需解决关键技术和伦理政策挑战才能实现大规模应用，其未来将深远影响社会各领域。

Abstract: Humanoid and quadrupedal robots have the potential to revolutionize the way we work, interact, and coexist with intelligent machines. To understand their effects on society and how they can enable scientific discovery, we assess the current capabilities of these systems along hardware, locomotion, autonomy, data, and applications. We identify recent advances and key open challenges that must be overcome to enable widespread adoption and new use cases for legged robots. Last, we provide an outlook on the future of legged robots, exploring their ethical considerations, economic potential, policy implications, and broader societal effects.

</details>


### [146] [ST-WAM: Semantic-Temporal World Action Model for Robust Manipulation under Visual Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28993)
*Mingxin Wang,Bin Hu,Bin Qian,Kaitao Jiang,Haoning Wu,Feng Yan,Bowen Jing,Ruiyang Hao,Enyi Wang,Kangning Niu,Yandan Yang,Mu Xu,Yan Wang,Houde Liu,Tianlun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-WAM（语义-时间世界动作模型），针对世界动作模型（WAM）在视觉分布偏移下因像素级未来预测导致的鲁棒性问题，利用DINOv3语义特征替代像素生成进行未来预测与历史检索，通过双空间未来专家（DSFE）和当前锚定意图检索（CAIR）实现视觉偏移下的鲁棒操作。在LIBERO上达98.7%，RoboTwin 2.0上达92.8%，零样本LIBERO-Plus性能较Fast-WAM提升21.3个百分点，真实世界视觉偏移下成功率从25.8%提升至61.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）联合建模机器人动作与未来视觉动态，但其依赖像素级生成的未来监督，会将动作相关的状态转移与任务无关的视觉内容纠缠在一起，导致在视觉分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。作者识别出"训练分布幻觉"（Training-Distribution Hallucination）现象：基于视觉偏移观测的未来预测会幻觉出训练域内容而非忠实于当前场景，因此需要一种不依赖像素生成、更鲁棒的语义级时域建模方法。

Method: ST-WAM采用DINOv3作为共享语义表示，替代像素级特征用于未来预测和历史检索，同时保留细粒度的VAE动态。具体包括两个核心模块：（1）双空间未来专家（DSFE），同时预测未来VAE潜在表示和DINO特征，实现语义与细粒度动态的双轨预测；（2）当前锚定意图检索（CAIR），在当前视觉-语言上下文下从最近的DINO历史中检索任务相关证据。整个模型端到端训练，无需额外的具身预训练或任务级标注，推理时无需显式未来生成。

Result: ST-WAM在LIBERO上达到98.7%，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到92.8%；相比Fast-WAM，零样本LIBERO-Plus性能提升21.3个百分点（绝对值），真实世界视觉偏移下的成功率从25.8%提升至61.5%（提升超过一倍）。消融实验中的帧三元组诊断显示，DINOv3特征在视觉偏移下比Wan-VAE潜在表示更稳定，同时更好地保留任务状态区分度。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，语义-时间建模能有效补充像素生成动态，显著提升视觉分布偏移下的操作鲁棒性。这验证了将预测重心从像素级未来生成转移到语义级表示上，是解决WAM训练分布幻觉问题的有效途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm by jointly modeling robot actions and future visual dynamics. However, their reliance on pixel-generative future supervision can entangle action-relevant state transitions with task-irrelevant visual content, limiting robustness under visual distribution shifts. We identify Training-Distribution Hallucination, a recurring phenomenon in which futures conditioned on visually shifted observations hallucinate training-domain content rather than remain faithful to the current scene. A controlled frame-triplet diagnosis further shows that DINOv3 features remain more stable across visual shifts while better preserving task-state distinctions than Wan-VAE latents. Rather than correcting the predicted futures, we propose Semantic-Temporal WAM (ST-WAM) to improve action robustness by using DINOv3 as a shared semantic representation for future prediction and history retrieval while retaining fine-grained VAE dynamics. Its Dual-Space Future Experts (DSFE) jointly predict future VAE latents and DINO features, while Current-Anchored Intent Retrieval (CAIR) retrieves task-relevant evidence from recent DINO history under the current visual-language context. ST-WAM is trained end-to-end without additional embodied pretraining or task-specific annotations, and requires no explicit future generation at inference. It achieves 98.7% on LIBERO and 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0; more importantly, compared with Fast-WAM, it improves zero-shot LIBERO-Plus performance by 21.3 percentage points and more than doubles real-world success under visual shifts from 25.8% to 61.5%. These results demonstrate that semantic-temporal modeling effectively complements pixel-generative dynamics for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [Receding-Horizon Next-Best-View Planner for Autonomous Leaf Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28995)
*Arif Ahmed,Sajal K. Das,Parikshit Maini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于质心信息增益(CIG)函数的下一最佳视角(NBV)规划方法，用于在移动机器人旅行约束下的大田草莓叶片表面重建，并通过滚动时域变体进一步提升重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的植物叶片建模是植物生长监测和产量估算表现型分析的基础，而大规模大田自主机器人重建需要克服机器人规划预算有限和计算资源受限的约束，同时优化视角效用以提升叶片表面重建质量。现有方法要么仅针对刚体对象，要么只关注点云覆盖或一般植物重建，未能充分解决系统资源限制或利用任务驱动的点云效用。

Method: 提出一个创新的基于质心的信息增益(CIG)函数，通过度量现有点云中观测点相对于质心的空间分布来计算视角效用；同时开发了滚动时域(前看式)变体以考虑未来视角的效用。在公开的LAST-STRAW草莓植物点云数据集上进行基准测试，与基于可见度信息增益的注意力驱动NBV方法进行对比。

Result: 在多个生长阶段的草莓植物点云上，所提出的滚动时域方法在多个生长阶段持续降低表面重建误差并提升几何保真度，尤其在叶间遮挡增加的情况下表现突出。相比基线方法，重建精度提升最高达10%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于CIG的NBV规划方法能够有效选择提升叶片表面重建质量的视角，在资源受限的大田机器人场景中优于现有基于可见度信息增益的基线方法，具有较强的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate plant leaf modeling is fundamental to downstream tasks such as plant growth monitoring, and phenotyping for yield estimation. Autonomous robotic reconstruction for large-scale field deployment must address limitations on robot planning budget and computation resources while optimizing viewpoint utility for leaf surface reconstruction. Existing approaches either focus on rigid objects, point-cloud coverage or plant reconstruction without fully addressing the system limitations or exploiting task-driven point cloud utility. In this work, we study next-best-view (NBV) planning for leaf surface reconstruction under travel constraints. We develop a novel Centroid-based Information Gain (CIG) function that measures the spatial distribution of observed points relative to the centroid of the existing point cloud to compute viewpoint utility. We also develop a receding-horizon variant that reasons over future viewpoints. To benchmark our work, we use the LAST-STRAW [1] public dataset that includes point clouds of strawberry plants over different growth stages and compare our method with attention-driven NBV [2] that uses a visibility-based information gain approach. The proposed receding-horizon approach consistently reduces surface reconstruction error and improves geometric fidelity across multiple growth stages, especially under increased inter-leaf occlusion. Results demonstrate that our approach is able to visit viewpoints that reduce surface reconstruction error and improves reconstruc-tion accuracy as compared to the baseline by upto 10%.

</details>


### [148] [D-VLC: Decentralized Vision-Language Collaboration for Heterogeneous Embodied Multi-Robot Systems in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29009)
*Yuan Zhou,Ruitong Lin,Shen Wang,Weiqi Gai,Mo zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向异构多机器人系统的通用框架，将去中心化异步推理、轻量信息共享、能力感知协作和统一动作接口相结合，使通用VLM无需任务或机器人特定训练即可生成可执行动作，在多种场景中成功率超过70%，完成时间较几何贪心基线最多减少55.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的多机器人方法依赖预定义任务模型和专家决策程序，难以理解复杂语义指令并协调异构机器人；现有LLM/VLM方法又依赖已知地图、中心化同步决策，限制了在异构机器人和未见任务上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一个包含四个关键组件的框架：(1)去中心化异步推理，避免中心化同步瓶颈；(2)轻量信息共享，降低通信开销；(3)能力感知协作，根据各机器人异构能力进行任务分配；(4)统一动作接口，使通用VLM生成机器人特定动作，并由无需学习的专家模块执行，无需任务或机器人特定的训练。

Result: 在多样化的场景和多种VLM上进行了实验，成功率均超过70%；与几何贪心基线相比，任务完成时间最多减少了55.8%。

Conclusion: 通过去中心化异步推理与统一动作接口的设计，该框架使通用VLM能够有效协调异构机器人完成复杂任务，突破了现有方法对已知地图和中心化决策的依赖，展现出良好的泛化能力和效率优势。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems, particularly heterogeneous robot swarms, can improve the efficiency of complex task execution through parallel collaboration and complementary capabilities. However, conventional rule-based methods rely on predefined task models and specialized decision making programs, making it difficult to understand complex semantic instructions and coordinate heterogeneous robots. LLMs introduce strong language understanding and task reasoning capabilities, allowing multi-robot systems to interpret instructions, decompose tasks, and assign roles according to task semantics. VLMs further incorporate visual perception, enabling robots to reason about objects, regions, and spatial relationships in physical environments. Nevertheless, existing LLM/VLM based methods often depend on known maps, centralized and synchronized decision making, limiting their generalization to heterogeneous robots and unseen tasks. We therefore propose a framework that combines decentralized asynchronous reasoning, lightweight information sharing, capability aware collaboration, and a unified action interface, enabling general purpose VLMs to generate robot specific actions executed by learning free experts without task or robot specific training. Experiments across diverse scenarios and multiple VLMs show success rates above 70\%, with completion time reduced by up to 55.8\% relative to the geometric greedy baseline.

</details>


### [149] [DART: Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29011)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Mingyuan Sang,Cheng Min,Liang Chen,Wei Li,Wenyu Kuang,Shican Chen,Jinwei Li,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究高速越障后车辆腾空阶段的姿态控制问题。作者证明飞行阶段的可控性极有限（俯仰角速度恢复能力仅约9-18°/s），因此控制的关键在于起飞前。提出的DART框架通过反向传播着陆约束到起飞前，生成保守的可行起飞集合，提供可/不可起飞的判定条件和起飞前速度整形律；飞行阶段则通过转向解耦的轮反作用力矩调节俯仰与横滚。在BeamNG.tech确定性全尺寸仿真中，DART将着陆速度降低36%，达标着陆从0/30提升到30/30，显著优于两种基线控制器；硬件验证尚待开展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速跨越山脊、崖沿和沟渠时车辆常腾空而起，着陆失当会造成严重碰撞事故。但飞行阶段车辆几乎不可控（轮角动量预算有限），因此亟需明确可控性边界，并在起飞前采取关键干预，从而保障跳坡飞行的安全着陆。

Method: 首先对1383 kg平台进行轮角动量预算分析，量化飞行中可恢复的俯仰速率变化（约9-13°/s，驱动到硬限后约16-18°/s）。据此提出DART框架：(1) 将着陆约束通过反向传播转化为闭合形式的认证可行起飞集合，提供保守的go/no-go判定与起飞前速度整形律；(2) 飞行阶段基于横摇闩锁（由偏航耦合分析导出）采用转向解耦的轮反作用力矩调节俯仰与横滚；在确定性全尺寸仿真中对比RW-PD与TOBB基线。

Result: 校准后的起飞前速度调节器使着陆速度降低36%，达标着陆从0/30升至30/30；同陡沿工况下飞行动力学控制完成29/30安全着陆（基线均为0/30）。在倾斜跑道上各横坡角下中位俯仰误差≤2°，最大基线分离出现在γ=12°。横摇闩锁在低扰动时保持纯俯仰分配，横滚约束激活时启用双轴控制。所有结果均为仿真结果，硬件验证尚未开展。

Conclusion: 车辆腾空阶段的可控性本质上受轮角动量硬上限约束，可靠安全的越障飞行应把主要控制杠杆前移到起飞前。DART通过在起飞前认证可行起飞集合并施加速度整形、在飞行中实施定向反作用力矩控制，在仿真中显著提升了着陆安全性与达标率；但结论仍待硬件实验验证。

Abstract: Traversing crests, ledges, and ditches at high speed often launches vehicles into the air, and a mishandled landing presents a substantial crash hazard. We show that the airborne phase is barely controllable: on a 1383 kg platform the wheel angular-momentum budget caps the recoverable pitch-rate change at roughly $9$-$13^\circ$/s in the tighter nose-up direction under drive at typical takeoff wheel speeds, and at about twice that in the reverse-inclusive braking direction; driving the wheels to their drivetrain hard limit raises the measured nose-up ceiling to only $16$-$18^\circ$/s. Takeoff pitch-rate disturbances beyond this directional budget are physically unrecoverable in flight, so the decisive leverage lies before takeoff. DART (Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction) back-propagates the landing constraint into a closed-form certified feasible-takeoff set, which supplies a conservative go/no-go condition and a pre-takeoff speed-shaping law. In flight, DART regulates pitch and roll via steer-resolved wheel-reaction torque, governed by a per-flight roll latch derived from the yaw-coupling analysis. In deterministic full-scale simulation in BeamNG.tech, a calibrated pre-takeoff speed regulator reduces touchdown speed by 36% and raises on-target landings from 0/30 to 30/30. Under the same steep-lip approach the airborne law completes 29/30 safe landings under crash-avoidance bounds versus 0/30 for reaction-wheel-style PD (RW-PD) and time-optimal bang-bang (TOBB). On banked run-ups DART holds the median pitch error at or below $2^\circ$ at every cross-slope, with the largest baseline separation at $γ=12^\circ$. Across disturbance regimes, the latch preserves pitch-only allocation on low-disturbance entries and enables dual-axis control when roll becomes binding. All results are from simulation; hardware validation remains open.

</details>


### [150] [Auto-JEPA: A Latent World Model of Continuous Intent for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29031)
*Jiwei Yang,Zhengxian Chen,Chaosheng Huang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auto-JEPA提出了一种面向自动驾驶规划的潜在世界模型，通过联合嵌入预测（JEPA）预测未来驾驶意图嵌入，而非密集重建未来视频、占用状态或BEV表示。它冻结视觉编码器，仅优化任务特定模块，在NAVSIM上取得91.3 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS的成绩，并通过语义遮挡实验证明其能聚焦于影响未来驾驶的关键视觉特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型通常进行密集的未来预测（视频、占用状态、BEV表示或智能体运动），但作者认为规划并不需要完整重建未来世界，只需关注影响未来自我车辆动作的场景特征，从而避免密集预测带来的高计算成本和无关信息干扰。

Method: Auto-JEPA利用联合嵌入预测架构：给定视觉观测、自身运动历史和导航命令，预测一个与未来自我轨迹潜在表示对齐的意图嵌入；预测的意图从固定轨迹记忆库中检索可执行轨迹，再由场景条件化候选选择模块排序。视觉编码器保持冻结，无需显式感知标注，也不使用学习式轨迹生成器，仅优化轨迹表示、意图预测和候选选择三个任务特定模块。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上取得91.3 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上取得89.1 EPDMS。语义遮挡实验显示，遮蔽动态智能体区域引起的平均意图变化是等面积随机遮蔽的2.97倍；遮蔽影响未来驾驶的车辆会显著改变预测意图和所选轨迹，而遮蔽不影响驾驶的车辆时两者基本不变。

Conclusion: 未来意图预测能促使模型聚焦于规划相关的视觉特征，在不进行密集未来世界建模的前提下实现高质量的规划性能，验证了"规划无需重建完整未来世界"的核心观点。

Abstract: Existing autonomous-driving world models typically perform dense prediction of future videos, occupancy states, BEV representations, or agent motion. We argue that planning need not reconstruct the complete future world, but only focus on scene features that affect future ego action. Based on this perspective, we propose Auto-JEPA, an action-oriented latent world model that learns continuous future driving intent through joint-embedding prediction. Given visual observations, egomotion history, and navigation commands, Auto-JEPA predicts an intent embedding aligned with the latent representation of the future ego trajectory. The predicted intent retrieves executable trajectories from a fixed trajectory memory, which are then ranked by a scene-conditioned candidate selection module. Auto-JEPA keeps the visual encoder frozen, requires no explicit perception annotations, and uses no learned trajectory generator. By optimizing only task-specific modules for trajectory representation, intent prediction, and candidate selection, Auto-JEPA achieves 91.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. Semantic occlusion experiments show that masking dynamic-agent regions induces an average intent change 2.97x that of equal-area random masking. Moreover, occluding vehicles that affect future driving substantially changes the predicted intent and selected trajectory, whereas both remain essentially unchanged when non-influential vehicles are occluded. These results show that future-intent prediction encourages the model to focus on planning-relevant visual features and supports high-quality planning without dense future-world modeling.

</details>


### [151] [Outcome-Guided Distillation: A Teacher-Student Framework to Advance VLM Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29052)
*Zeyu Dong,Yimin Zhu,Yu Wu,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种教师-学生架构的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过VLM生成反射式推理文本以增强可解释性和驾驶鲁棒性，在Waymo基准上超越经典推理基线，推理文本带来约24%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型通常是黑盒，难以应对复杂场景且缺乏可解释性。现有的VLM辅助方法依赖预生成的标注数据，存在标签质量差和人工成本高的问题。因此需要一种无需中间标签、同时兼顾可解释性与驾驶性能的新框架。

Method: 提出教师-学生架构：教师模型使用VLM生成逻辑解释并进行反射式推理，在地面真值动作的监督下精炼推理过程，实现无中间标签的零样本泛化；学生模型通过监督微调蒸馏教师的推理能力；另设计独立的航点解码器将文本推理转换为连续轨迹；采用分阶段推理引擎协同可解释性与驾驶性能两个目标。

Result: 在Waymo基准上，框架在零样本推理、航点精度和推理效率方面均超越经典推理基线；消融实验表明推理文本对驾驶推断贡献显著，与不含推理的相同模型相比性能提升约24%。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了推理驱动的自动驾驶向可解释、可部署的系统方向发展，验证了文本推理与驾驶预测之间的协同增效关系。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving aims to learn a direct mapping from visual observations to control actions. However, these E2E models often act as black boxes and struggle with complex scenarios. To address this, recent works incorporate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide explicit reasoning, enhancing both interpretability and driving robustness. These approaches typically rely on pre-generated annotations, which suffer from potentially flawed labels and require costly human labor. In this work, we propose a new framework that integrates structured reasoning and geometric precision through a teacher-student architecture. The teacher model introduces reflective reasoning, where the VLM generates logical explanations and then reflectively refines the reasoning under the supervision of ground-truth action. This enhances zero-shot generalization without intermediate labels. The student model distills the teacher's reasoning capabilities via supervised fine-tuning. We also design a separate waypoint decoder that interprets textual reasoning into continuous trajectories. Our proposed solution integrates two goals: providing explicit reasoning for interpretability and delivering robust and accurate driving performance. It leverages the synergy between these two objectives within a staged inference engine to enhance driving performance and explicitly uses the reasoning to guide driving prediction. Evaluated on Waymo benchmarks, our framework outperforms classical reasoning-based baselines in zero-shot reasoning, waypoint accuracy, and inference efficiency. Our experiments validate this design, demonstrating that the reasoning text makes a significant contribution to driving inference, resulting in around a 24% improvement in performance compared to an identical model that lacks reasoning. Our work advances reasoning-driven autonomous driving toward interpretable and deployable systems.

</details>


### [152] [VSTaI: Design and Characterization of Variable-Stiffness Tactile Interfaces Based on 3D-Printed Structured Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29102)
*Yiting Mo,Xinyuan Mao,Jashan Preet Singh,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于真空诱导3D打印结构化织物堵塞的可变刚度触觉接口（VSTaI），通过调节内部压力实现软组织刚度变化，测试中最高刚度提升达140%，有效模量达MPa级别，并兼具出色的几何贴合性，为体格检查训练提供了实用的可变刚度显示方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的触诊训练需要实时可靠地渲染软组织刚度变化，传统模拟器难以实现这一需求，因此需要一种紧凑、可变刚度的触觉接口来满足这一临床训练场景。

Method: 将真空密封的织物层夹在两片硅胶层之间，通过调节内部压力实现刚度调节；制备了四种不同几何参数的织物图案，并在常压和真空条件下进行力-压痕测试以评估其刚度表现。

Result: 真空堵塞显著提高了刚度，有效模量从亚MPa提升至MPa级别；其中一种配置在堵塞状态下刚度提升最高达140%；圆形链甲图案提供了最均匀的触觉刚度空间分布，而更密集的几何结构达到更高峰值刚度；VSTaI还表现出对底层几何形状的优异贴合性。

Conclusion: 结构化织物堵塞是实现形状贴合、刚度可调显示器的实用方法，适用于体格检查训练等物理检查教学场景。

Abstract: Realistic palpation training requires reliable rendering of soft tissue stiffness changes in real time, which is difficult to achieve with conventional simulators. This paper presents a compact, variable-stiffness tactile interface (VSTaI) based on vacuum-induced jamming of 3D-printed structured fabrics. A vacuum-sealed fabric layer is sandwiched between two silicone layers, and stiffness is tuned by regulating internal pressure. Four fabric patterns with different geometric parameters were fabricated and evaluated using force-indentation tests under atmospheric and vacuum conditions. Across the tested pattern and geometry combinations, vacuum jamming increased stiffness significantly, producing an effective modulus from sub-megapascal to megapascal levels. Specifically, one configuration exhibited a stiffness increase of up to 140% under the jammed state. Circular chainmail patterns provided the most spatially uniform distribution of tactile stiffness, while denser geometries reached higher peak stiffness. VSTaI was also shown to exhibit excellent conformability to the underlying geometry. These results support structured-fabric jamming as a practical approach for shape-conformable, tunable-stiffness displays aimed at physical examination training.

</details>


### [153] [ActFovea: Runtime Safeguarding for VLA Policies via Spatiotemporal Visual-Action Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29169)
*Wenda Yu,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Xin Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActFovea是一个即插即用的VLA策略运行时保护框架，通过构建动作条件化的注视区域（foveated regions）来检测和缓解视觉观测与机器人状态、动作之间的时间对齐失败，无需重新训练或修改底层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人操作中表现出色，但容易受到运行时干扰的影响，这些干扰会破坏视觉观测、机器人状态和已执行动作之间的时间对齐，导致策略失效。

Method: ActFovea利用机器人运动学、本体感受状态和最近动作，构建动作条件化注视区域以保留接触相关区域和预测运动走廊，同时抑制无关视觉内容。它评估视觉运动与观测新鲜度是否与几何、本体感受和动作转换一致来检测运行时风险；对于可恢复干扰，构造干扰特定的候选观测并在验证动作块后接受恢复；当无法可靠恢复时，触发有界安全失败程序。

Result: 在π0的多项LIBERO套件闭环评估中，ActFovea将局部视觉叠加下的成功率从49.3%提升至90.3%，缩小了93.7%与干净性能的差距；在动作漂移和视觉延迟下分别提升7.0和9.8个百分点，同时保持干净任务性能；在冻结观测重放下所有试验均触发及时安全失败，无未保护失败。

Conclusion: 时空视觉-动作一致性为VLA策略的运行时保护提供了有效基础，ActFovea展现出在不改动底层策略的情况下显著增强VLA鲁棒性的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain vulnerable to runtime disturbances that break the temporal alignment among visual observations, robot states, and executed actions. We introduce ActFovea, a plug-and-play safeguarding framework that detects and mitigates such failures without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA policy. ActFovea uses robot kinematics, proprioceptive states, and recent actions to construct action-conditioned foveated regions that retain contact-relevant areas and predicted motion corridors while suppressing task-irrelevant visual content. It detects runtime risks by evaluating whether visual motion and observation freshness remain consistent with geometric, proprioceptive, and action transitions. For recoverable disturbances, ActFovea constructs disturbance-specific candidate observations and accepts a recovery only after verifying the resulting action chunk. When stale or replayed observations make reliable recovery impossible, it invokes a bounded safe-failure procedure. In closed-loop evaluations of $π_0$ across multiple LIBERO suites, ActFovea increases success under localized visual overlays from 49.3\% to 90.3\%, closing 93.7\% of the gap to clean performance. It further improves success under action drift and visual delay by 7.0 and 9.8 percentage points, respectively, while preserving clean-task performance. Under frozen-observation replay, ActFovea triggers timely safe failure in all trials, with no unprotected failures. These results demonstrate that spatiotemporal visual-action consistency provides an effective basis for runtime safeguarding of VLA policies.

</details>


### [154] [CLIFT: Turning Gemini Robotics On-Device into Humanoid Specialists via Non-Invasive Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29172)
*Yuxin Chen,Hari Srikanth,Nathan Jew,Menglin Wu,Pengcheng Wang,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Peng Xu,Jinyu Xie,Thomas Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在托管监督微调（SFT）API范式下，闭源机器人基础模型如何实现闭环策略改进。作者提出了CLIFT（闭环迭代微调）方法，将部署时的奖励反馈转化为API兼容的监督数据，在不访问模型权重、梯度或损失的情况下实现闭环策略优化，并在真实人形机器人（Gemini Robotics On-Device）上验证了该方法在敏捷、接触丰富的操作任务中使策略达到近乎完美的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器人基础模型能力日益增强，最强模型通常基于专有数据训练且保持闭源，限制了用户对新任务、新形态和部署场景的适配能力。托管SFT API虽然允许下游用户利用强大的专有模型进行微调，但将所有策略改进限制在纯模仿学习上，排除了强化学习等依赖内部训练信号的闭环方法。这一限制在敏捷、接触丰富的人形操作任务中尤为严重，因为策略输出与实际部署行为之间存在显著差距。因此，需要研究如何在托管API范式下实现闭环策略改进。

Method: 作者首先进行了托管API范式下人形机器人适配的实证研究，使用Gemini Robotics On-Device（GROD）模型，比较了通过API直接SFT与开放权重VLA在同一演示数据上的性能。在此基础上，提出了CLIFT（Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning）框架：它将部署时的奖励反馈转化为API兼容的监督数据，通过迭代的"飞轮"循环（部署-收集反馈-生成监督数据-再次SFT）实现闭环策略改进，整个过程不依赖模型权重、梯度、似然度或损失信息的访问。

Result: 实验表明：1）通过API直接SFT的GROD在性能上显著优于使用相同演示数据训练的领先开放权重VLA；2）直接SFT在敏捷、接触丰富的任务上仍达不到部署级掌握水平；3）应用CLIFT后，经过两个飞轮循环，GROD在目标任务上达到了近乎完美的成功率，且全程未获取模型的内部访问权限。

Conclusion: 研究证明了托管SFT API范式下人形机器人闭环适配的可行性。通过CLIFT方法，闭环策略改进可以仅通过API兼容的监督数据实现，弥合了纯模仿学习与部署级任务掌握之间的差距，为闭源机器人基础模型的可适配性提供了新的途径。

Abstract: While robot foundation models are growing increasingly capable, the strongest models are typically trained on proprietary data and remain closed-source, limiting downstream users' ability to adapt them to new tasks, embodiments, and deployment settings. Following the LLM community, an emerging access paradigm for closed-weight robot foundation models is the managed supervised fine-tuning (SFT) API, where users submit training data and receive a tuned policy without access to model weights, gradients, or training internals. While such APIs let downstream users leverage powerful proprietary foundation models, they restrict policy improvement to pure imitation, ruling out reinforcement learning and other closed-loop methods that rely on internal training signals. This limitation is particularly acute for agile, contact-rich humanoid manipulation, where the gap between policy outputs and deployed behavior is large due to novel states, action tracking dynamics, latency, and controller-specific failure modes. We study how effective this managed-API regime is for humanoid adaptation, and how closed-loop improvement can be realized within it to push policies toward task mastery. We conduct one of the first empirical studies of managed-API adaptation on a real humanoid, instantiated on Gemini Robotics On-Device (GROD). We find that direct SFT through the API substantially outperforms a leading open-weight VLA trained on the same demonstrations, yet still falls short of deployment-level mastery on agile, contact-rich tasks. To close this gap, we introduce CLIFT: Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning, which turns deployment-time reward feedback into API-compatible supervised data and enables closed-loop policy improvement without accessing weights, gradients, likelihoods, or losses-pushing GROD to near-perfect success after two flywheel cycles, all without "opening the model box."

</details>


### [155] [MROPE: A Multi-Robot Safe Cooperative Strategy via combined Predictive Safety Filters and Ellipse-based Constraint Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29203)
*Alice Rosetti,Lorenzo Pichierri,Domenico Cappello,Fabrizio Schiano,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MROPE分层框架，通过将复杂障碍物聚合为安全边界椭圆、结合分布式聚合优化与预测安全滤波器，实现无人机集群在杂乱环境中对动态目标的高效实时跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群在杂乱环境中追踪动态目标面临严峻的计算和安全挑战，集中式方法在密集空间中存在计算瓶颈，难以兼顾协同监控效率与局部安全约束。

Method: 分层架构：高层采用分布式聚合优化实现集群协同；中层通过去中心化共识算法计算安全区域（将复杂障碍物动态聚合为单一安全边界椭圆）；底层使用局部预测安全滤波器(PSF)实现实时碰撞避免。该架构将协同监控任务与严格局部安全需求解耦。

Result: 虚拟与实物实验验证了框架的有效性，相比集中式方法展现出优越的实时效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: MROPE通过层次化解耦策略有效解决了无人机集群动态目标追踪中计算复杂性与安全性的矛盾，在实时性和可扩展性方面优于集中式方案。

Abstract: Deploying drone swarms to track a dynamic target in cluttered environments presents severe computational and safety challenges. We propose MROPE, a hierarchical strategy that decouples the cooperative monitoring mission from strict local safety requirements. To overcome the computational bottlenecks typical of dense spaces, our approach dynamically aggregates complex obstacle geometries into a single safe bounding ellipse for each drone. Methodologically, this architecture is realized by combining distributed aggregative optimization for high-level swarm coordination, a decentralized consensus scheme for the safe area computation, and local Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) for real-time collision avoidance. Virtual and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior real-time efficiency and scalability compared to centralized approaches.

</details>


### [156] [Event-Based Upper-Body Humanoid Teleoperation Under Challenging Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29227)
*Haoyu Fu,Zhou Ge,Chengze Li,Chenzhao Sun,Ze Cui,Wenjing Zhou,Xulei Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由神经形态事件相机驱动的实时上半身人形机器人运动模仿框架。通过Prophesee EVK4事件相机在HDR场景和快速运动中的优势，结合低延迟感知模块（事件累积+惯性融合）与因果运动模块（TWIST），在嵌入式平台和18自由度人形机器人上实现了23-34ms的光子到动作端到端延迟，优于RGB基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧基RGB传感器存在固定积分时间导致的HDR场景和快速运动中感知性能退化问题，尤其在严重逆光和低于5勒克斯的极低光环境下无法稳定跟踪，需要一种不受此限制的替代视觉方案。

Method: 采用Prophesee EVK4事件相机（异步、高时间分辨率、动态范围超120dB），架构由两部分组成：低延迟感知模块（优化事件累积+重力对齐惯性融合）和因果运动模块（TWIST，执行在线运动学重定向）。系统在NVIDIA Booster T1嵌入式平台和18自由度人形机器人上半身上验证。

Result: 端到端光子到动作延迟为23-34ms，在实验设置下优于RGB基线。结果表明事件相机在快速或光照不良的上半身遥操作中更具优势，而光照良好的静态场景中RGB或混合感知更为合适。

Conclusion: 事件相机为上半身人体到人形机器人的遥操作提供了实际可行的感知方案，尤其在传统相机难以应对的恶劣光照和快速运动场景中展现出明显优势，但需根据具体应用场景在事件、RGB或混合感知之间做出权衡。

Abstract: We present a real-time upper-body human-to-humanoid motion imitation framework driven by neuromorphic event-based vision. This work addresses practical perceptual bottlenecks of conventional frame-based RGB sensors, specifically their difficulty in high dynamic range (HDR) scenes and rapid motions due to fixed integration times. By leveraging the Prophesee EVK4 event camera, which operates asynchronously with high temporal resolution and a dynamic range exceeding 120 dB, our system supports stable tracking in conditions where standard vision pipelines degrade, such as severe backlighting and very low light environments below 5 lux. The architecture integrates a low-latency Perception Module, utilizing optimized event accumulation and gravity-aligned inertial fusion, with a causal Motion Module (TWIST) that performs online kinematic retargeting. We validate the system on an embedded NVIDIA Booster T1 platform and an 18-DoF humanoid upper-body setup, demonstrating an end-to-end photon-to-action latency of 23-34 ms and advantages over RGB baselines under our experimental setup. The results indicate a practical trade-off: events can be preferable for fast or poorly lit upper-body teleoperation, whereas well-lit static scenes may favor RGB or hybrid sensing.

</details>


### [157] [TacPrint: A Wearable Fingertip Tactile Sensor for Human-to-Robot Contact Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29231)
*Yongxi Liu,Chaofan Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Xiangyin Bao,Boyue Zhang,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacPrint，一种可穿戴指尖触觉传感器，通过硅胶皮肤内表面凸起与24个电容式taxel一一对应实现局部电容响应，并通过实到仿到实（real-to-sim-to-real）管线从24通道电容信号估计35×26的接触深度图。实验表明其在接触区域估计的高精度，并显著提升了人-机器人遥操作中抓取和擦拭任务的成功率，以及闭环抓取在边缘接触条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的数据收集正成为机器人技能获取的重要范式，但如何在保持自然操作的同时，低成本、可扩展地集成能够捕获细粒度指尖交互的触觉传感系统仍是一个关键挑战。传统触觉传感系统在接触密集任务中导致人-机器人技能迁移的可靠性下降。

Method: 1) 设计TacPrint可穿戴指尖触觉传感器，硅胶皮肤内表面凸起与24个电容式taxel一一对应（1:1对齐），实现局部电容响应；2) 构建real-to-sim-to-real管线，从24通道电容信号估计35×26的接触深度图；3) 采用仿真的深度学习模型，通过校准引导的接触中心进行深度预测；4) 在人-机器人遥操作中使用触觉引导补偿，并在闭环抓取中利用密集深度反馈。

Result: 1) 仿真标签下：接触区域RMSE为0.223±0.161 mm，加权质心误差为1.213±2.379像素，IoU为0.829±0.169；2) 实测电容输入下：校准接触中心处的平均绝对误差为0.085±0.057 mm（40次试验），接触位置平均误差为0.250±0.208 mm（37次试验）；3) 人-机器人重放中，触觉引导补偿将抓取和擦拭成功率从0%提升至91.67%和90%；4) 闭环抓取中，密集深度反馈在全部测试位置和边缘接触条件下分别达到87.5%和85%成功率，而原始taxel反馈仅为67.5%和45%。

Conclusion: TacPrint通过低成本可扩展的可穿戴触觉传感设计与real-to-sim-to-real深度估计管线，实现了高精度的指尖接触深度感知，显著提升了接触密集场景下人-机器人技能迁移的可靠性和闭环抓取性能，验证了密集触觉反馈相比原始taxel信号的优越性。

Abstract: Human-centric data collection is emerging as a significant paradigm for robot skill acquisition, but seamlessly integrating low-cost, scalable tactile sensing systems that capture fine-grained fingertip interactions without compromising natural operation remains a key challenge. This reduces the reliability of human-to-robot transfer in contact-rich tasks. In this work, we present TacPrint, a wearable fingertip tactile sensor, where protrusions on the inner surface of the silicone skin are aligned one-to-one with 24 capacitive taxels to enable localized capacitive responses. A real-to-sim-to-real pipeline estimates a 35 $\times$ 26 contact-depth map from 24-channel capacitive signals. Against simulation-generated labels, the model achieved a contact-region RMSE of 0.223 $\pm$ 0.161 mm, a weighted-centroid error of 1.213 $\pm$ 2.379 pixels, and an IoU of 0.829 $\pm$ 0.169. With measured capacitive inputs, the network-predicted depth evaluated at the guide-calibrated contact center showed a mean absolute error of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.057 mm across all 40 controlled trials, while the mean contact-position error was 0.250 $\pm$ 0.208 mm across the 37 trials whose reference contact regions were not truncated by the sensing boundary. In human-to-robot replay, tactile-guided compensation increased grasping and wiping success rates from 0% to 91.67% and 90%, respectively. In closed-loop grasping, dense-depth feedback achieved success rates of 87.5% over all tested positions and 85% under edge-contact conditions, compared with 67.5% and 45% for raw-taxel feedback.

</details>


### [158] [FBFM: A Training-Free Asynchronous Feedback Mechanism for Flow-Matching in World-Action Models Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29235)
*Peize Li,Ruimeng Zhang,Ru Zhang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM)，一种免训练的推理机制，将重新接地（re-grounding）推入流匹配生成中的活动块内部。通过在条件速度场上应用掩蔽伪逆修正，利用前一动作块指导下一动作块生成，并用执行后观测到的真实图像指导下一帧预测，实现了跨块异步闭环校正，无需等待块边界即可修正误差，从而抑制长时间任务中的漂移并提升对突发事件的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）通过分块刷新历史或KV缓存进行重新接地，但块级反馈的时间粒度粗糙，无法在单个时间步级别修正预测误差，导致长时程任务中误差累积和漂移。

Method: FBFM是一种免训练的推理机制。在流匹配（flow matching）过程中，对条件速度场应用掩蔽伪逆修正（masked pseudoinverse correction）：利用前一动作块引导下一动作块的生成，利用执行前一动作块后观测的真实图像引导下一帧预测。这一跨块配对形成异步循环，在不等待块边界的情况下实现误差修正。

Result: 在联合生成WAM（DreamZero）和阶段式WAM（LingBot-VA）上评估，在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0任务的有利设置下，成功率提升超过5%；真实机器人观测-预测诊断显示轨迹跟踪精度显著提升。

Conclusion: FBFM提供了一种细粒度在线修正的新范式，桥接了开环流生成与闭环真实世界动力学，为长时程机器人操控中的误差校正提供了无需额外训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Although world-action models (WAMs) enhance long-horizon robot control by predicting visual evolution before acting, long-horizon reliability demands repeated re-grounding in real observations--not recursive rollout. Existing WAMs address this by refreshing history or KV cache with ground-truth data between chunks. However, such chunk-wise feedback operates at a coarse temporal granularity and thus fails to correct prediction errors at the individual time-step level. To address this, we propose Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM), a training-free inference mechanism that pushes re-grounding inside the actively generated chunk. During flow matching, FBFM applies a masked pseudoinverse correction to the conditional velocity field: it leverages the preceding action chunk to guide generation of the next action chunk, and uses the image observed after executing that preceding chunk to guide the next frame prediction. This cross-chunk pairing--where feedback from one chunk arrives in time to shape the next--creates an asynchronous loop that corrects errors without waiting for chunk boundaries. Being training-free, the mechanism improves responsiveness to unexpected events and suppresses drift in long-horizon tasks. We evaluate FBFM on both a joint-generation WAM (DreamZero) and a stage-wise WAM (LingBot-VA). On selected LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0 tasks, it improves success rates by over 5% in favorable settings, and real-world robot observation-prediction diagnostics show notably better tracking. We argue that FBFM offers a new paradigm for fine-grained online correction, bridging open-loop flow generation with closed-loop real-world dynamics.

</details>


### [159] [MDIR: A Task-Manifold Impedance Retargeting Method for Contact-Rich Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29271)
*Liu Jiahao,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出流形分解阻抗重定向（MDIR）方法，将单次固定笛卡尔阻抗演示重新参数化为可执行的任务通道变阻抗指令，在保持投影任务通道响应的同时降低腕部力峰值、冲量、力变异性和标称控制器功率，并在Franka Panda机器人的平面擦拭、抓放和推搡任务中通过全部15次闭环执行的任务检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定笛卡尔阻抗虽然使接触丰富的遥操作演示变得实用，但用于保证进度和接触支撑的增益同时也决定了冲击力和力的变异性。因此需要对单次演示进行控制器到控制器的阻抗重定向，以在保持任务表现的同时降低激进的力特性。

Method: MDIR方法通过三个核心步骤实现：首先在操作功、施力和支撑通道中表示源响应，并在控制链度量下使用被动残差补集；然后计算可执行的笛卡尔到流形重定向（C2M）基线；最后应用流形约束参数优化（MPO）选择可行的代表性参数，以降低腕部力峰值、冲量、力变异性和标称控制器功率。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上的平面擦拭、抓放和推搡三个任务中，完整的MDIR控制器在所有15次闭环执行中均通过任务检查，并相对固定阻抗演示在所有四个攻击性度量指标上均有降低。

Conclusion: MDIR能够通过保留演示轨迹附近的投影任务通道响应，将固定阻抗演示确定性重定向为更温和的可执行变阻抗指令，在不牺牲任务完成度的前提下有效降低力的攻击性指标。

Abstract: Fixed Cartesian impedance makes contact-rich teleoperation demonstrations practical, but gains that secure progress and contact support also determine impact and force variability. We study single-demonstration controller-to-controller impedance retargeting. Given one fixed Cartesian impedance command sequence {K0, D0, xcmd}, Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) deterministically reparameterizes the recorded controller into an executable task-channel variable-impedance command. MDIR targets this local retargeting problem by preserving projected task-channel responses near the demonstrated trajectory. It represents the source response in operational work, exertion, and support channels with a passive residual complement under a control-chain metric, computes an executable Cartesian-to-Manifold Retargeting (C2M) baseline, and applies Manifold-Constrained Parameter Optimization (MPO) to select a feasible representative with lower wrist-force peaks, impulse, force variability, and nominal controller power. Across planar wiping, pick-and-place, and pushing on a Franka Panda, the full MDIR controller passes Task Check in all 15 closed-loop executions and reduces all four aggressiveness metrics relative to the fixed-impedance demonstrations.

</details>


### [160] [TRACT: Temporally Routed Action Chunks with Chronological Phase Authority for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29285)
*Jiahao Liu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACT通过将动作分块分解为当前阶段和当前到下一阶段的边界，结合任务局部图、累积边界分布和因果响应缺陷积分器，在六种真实机器人变体上实现10/10全序列成功，消除了相位模糊和停顿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块通过预测多个未来动作缩短了决策周期，但传统的阶段条件化只描述当前控制时刻。当预测范围跨越过程边界时，将当前阶段分配给整个分块会产生结构性的时间错配问题。

Method: TRACT将阶段结构的动作分块分解为已接受的当前阶段和未来视野内的单一"当前到下一阶段"边界。任务局部图约束时间顺序的阶段权威，累积边界分布单调地将未来查询路由到特定阶段的查询和动作路径。对于接触执行环节，引入因果响应缺陷积分器，比较策略意图与可确认的后续运动，在方向响应被抑制时累积手臂补偿，并在确认恢复后衰减。

Result: 在六个真实机器人变体各10次试验中，完整TRACT实现10/10全序列成功，99.00%中位数擦拭完成度，零相位模糊和零停顿。与扁平封装相比（6/10对3/10成功；77.08%对8.03%中位数擦拭完成度），路由表示获得更好的任务结果。时间顺序权威将相位模糊从8/10降至0/10，响应积分将停顿从4/10降至0/10。

Conclusion: TRACT的因子化解耦方法有效解决了动作分块跨过程边界时的相位-时间错配问题，实验验证了时间顺序权威和响应积分机制的贡献，但需注意封装比较未单独隔离路由机制与其他生成器封装差异的影响。

Abstract: Action chunking shortens the effective decision horizon of robot imitation learning by predicting multiple future actions, while conventional phase conditioning describes the current control instant. When a predicted horizon crosses a procedural boundary, assigning the current phase to the entire chunk creates a structural temporal mismatch. We present TRACT, which factorizes phase-structured action chunking into an accepted current phase and a single CURRENT-to-NEXT boundary inside the future horizon. A task-local graph constrains chronological phase authority, and a cumulative boundary distribution monotonically routes future queries through phase-specific query and action paths. For contact execution, a causal response-deficit integrator compares policy intent with ACK-eligible subsequent motion, accumulates arm compensation when directional response is suppressed, and decays after confirmed recovery. Across six real-robot variants with ten trials each, full TRACT achieves 10/10 full-sequence success, 99.00 [88.75, 100.00]% median [min, max] wipe completion, zero observed phase ambiguity, and zero stalls. Under the current complete method package and evaluation setting, the routed representation obtains better observed task results than the flat package (6/10 vs. 3/10 success; 77.08% vs. 8.03% median wipe completion). Chronological authority reduces observed phase ambiguity from 8/10 to 0/10, and response integration reduces stalls from 4/10 to 0/10. The package comparison does not isolate routing from other generator-package differences.

</details>


### [161] [BWM: A Low-Cost High-Fidelity World Simulator for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29302)
*BWM Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Boundless World Model (BWM)，一个开源、低成本、高保真的机器人操作世界模拟器。BWM 是动作条件世界模型，结合初始环境引导、动态视觉历史和时间对齐的机器人动作条件，进行有状态的未来观测自回归预测。BWM 可作为数据引擎（增强模仿学习数据）和策略评估器（闭环评估、风险预测、策略排名），在 WorldArena 基准和物理机器人上验证了更高模拟保真度和功能性，并在 WorldArena 挑战赛中整体排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理模拟器需要大量资产构建和标定且存在 sim-to-real gap；视频生成器缺乏对细粒度机器人动作响应的精确控制。可靠机器人学习需要能够在物理硬件执行前预测动作后果（包括危险和易失败结果）的世界模拟器，因此需要一种兼顾高保真度和精确动作控制的解决方案。

Method: BWM 作为一个动作条件世界模型，将初始环境引导、动态视觉历史和时间对齐的机器人动作条件结合，实现有状态的未来观测自回归预测。通过轨迹回放、重叠片段采样和初始观测增强构建动作对齐的训练片段。BWM 同时承担数据引擎（以动作对齐的 rollouts 增强模仿学习数据）和政策评估器（用于闭环评估、风险预测和策略排序）两种角色。

Result: BWM 在 WorldArena 基准和物理机器人上展现了更高的模拟保真度和功能性，在数据引擎和策略评估器两种设置下均表现出色，并在 WorldArena Challenge 的 Track 1 及其两个 Track 2 应用中整体排名第一。

Conclusion: BWM 作为开源生态系统发布（包括模型检查点、训练推理代码、数据生成与策略评估接口），提供了一种低成本、高保真且动作可控的机器人操作世界模拟方案，弥合了物理模拟器与视频生成器之间的缺口。

Abstract: Reliable robot learning requires a world simulator that can predict action consequences before execution on physical hardware, including risky and failure-prone outcomes. Existing physics simulators require substantial asset construction and calibration and still face a sim-to-real gap, while video generators often lack precise control over their responses to fine-grained robot actions. In this paper, we present the Boundless World Model (BWM), an open-source, low-cost, high-fidelity world simulator for robot manipulation. BWM is an action-conditioned world model that combines initial-environment guidance, dynamic visual history, and temporally aligned robot-action conditioning for stateful autoregressive prediction of future observations. We construct action-aligned training clips through trajectory replay, overlapping clip sampling, and initial-observation enhancement. BWM serves as a data engine that augments imitation-learning data with action-aligned rollouts, and as a policy evaluator for closed-loop assessment, risk anticipation, and policy ranking. Experiments on the WorldArena benchmark and physical robots demonstrate improved simulator fidelity and functional utility across the data-engine and policy-evaluator settings. BWM ranks first overall in the WorldArena Challenge across Track 1 and its two Track 2 applications. We release the BWM open-source ecosystem, including model checkpoints, training and inference code, and interfaces for data generation and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [162] [SAGP: Semantic Affordance-Guided Grasp Planning via Coarse-Zone VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29374)
*Muhayy Ud Din,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGP是一种免训练的语义-几何融合抓取规划框架，通过PCA对齐和DBSCAN聚类将点云划分为粗粒度空间区域（顶部、中部、底部、侧面、突起），利用预训练VLM对各区域进行零样本抓取质量评估，并与几何、可达性和任务对齐信号融合来重排序对极抓取候选。实验表明该方法在保持高成功率的同时显著提升抓取的功能合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于几何的抓取规划忽略了物体的功能语义，常产生物理有效但功能不当的抓取（如抓杯沿、刀片、瓶盖附近）。现有VLM方法要么依赖细粒度部件分割，要么直接推断抓取姿态导致空间幻觉，尚缺乏将高层语义推理与几何抓取规划稳健结合的免训练框架。

Method: SAGP采用三层流程：(1) 通过PCA空间对齐和基于距离的DBSCAN聚类将物体点云划分为粗粒度空间区域（顶部、中部、底部、侧面、突起），完全绕过学习式分割；(2) 使用预训练VLM通过结构化零样本查询评估每个区域的抓取质量；(3) 将区域级语义分数与几何、可达性和任务对齐信号融合，对对抗式抓取候选进行重排序。

Result: 在PyBullet中使用Franka Panda机器人对YCB物体进行实验，SAGP在保持与纯几何规划相当的高成功率的同时，显著提高了所选抓取的功能适当性，尤其在不对称、带柄物体上效果突出——这些场景中纯几何方法无法提供有效信息。

Conclusion: SAGP提出的粗粒度区域抽象层为VLM推理与几何抓取规划之间提供了有效的免训练桥梁，无需细粒度部件分割即可实现功能合理的抓取规划。

Abstract: Geometry-based grasp planners ensure physically valid grasps but ignore functional semantics, often generating grasps that are antipodal and collision-free yet practically inappropriate, for example, gripping a mug by its rim, a knife by the blade, or a bottle near its cap. These inconsistencies cause the downstream task to fail even when traditional grasp metrics are met. Existing vision-language model (VLM) approaches either depend on fine-grained, category-specific part segmentation or attempt to directly infer grasp poses, with the latter prone to spatial hallucinations. As a result, no practical, training-free framework has yet been proposed that robustly links high-level semantic reasoning to geometric grasp planning. We introduce Semantic Affordance-Guided Grasp Planning (SAGP), a training-free pipeline built on a coarse-zone abstraction layer. The method first partitions the object point cloud into spatial regions (top, middle, bottom, lateral sides, and protrusions) by applying PCA-based alignment followed by distance-driven DBSCAN clustering, entirely bypassing learned segmentation. A pre-trained VLM then assesses the grasp quality of each region through a structured zero-shot query, and the resulting zone-wise scores are fused with geometric, reachability, and task-alignment signals to re-rank antipodal grasp candidates. Experiments on YCB objects in PyBullet with a Franka Panda robot show that SAGP preserves the high success rate of geometry-only planning while substantially improving the functional appropriateness of selected grasps, particularly on asymmetric, handle-bearing objects where geometry alone is uninformative. The introduced coarse-zone abstraction offers an effective, training-free bridge between VLM-based reasoning and geometric grasp planning, without the need for fine-grained part segmentation.

</details>


### [163] [AquaJEPA: Action-Conditioned Multimodal Predictive Representations for Underwater Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29393)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Gavrilov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AquaJEPA，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构的动作条件水下机器人世界模型，融合RGB相机、前视声呐和本体感知，并显式建模传感器有效性。在Stonefish仿真中，它通过八推力器指令预测未来潜变量，为共享的滚动时域规划器提供速度和声呐剖面预测。在预注册的120环境复制实验中，AquaJEPA达到74个目标（优于state-only和循环世界模型的68个），并获得最低的平均最终误差（0.906 m），相比普通多模态预测、监督动力学和循环世界模型分别实现了0.273 m、0.364 m和0.106 m的成对最终误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的多传感器可靠性会随着水质能见度、视角和载体运动动态变化，且水下环境难以获取精确动力学模型。现有方法缺乏对未来状态显式建模以及传感器有效性的感知能力，难以应对传感器随机失效和动力学漂移的挑战。因此需要一种能够融合异构传感、显式考虑传感器有效性并预测未来潜状态的世界模型，以提升水下机器人闭环导航性能。

Method: AquaJEPA采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），以动作条件方式编码RGB相机、前视声呐和本体感知信息，并通过EMA目标网络预测未来潜空间表征。模型显式引入传感器有效性掩码，输出速度与声呐剖面预测，供共享的滚动时域规划器使用。训练中综合使用EMA目标、动作边距（action margin）、掩码和模态随机失活等手段。在Stonefish仿真环境中，与反应式、仅状态、普通多模态、监督动力学和循环世界模型等基线进行对比，并设计了预注册的120环境复制实验（5个独立复制，跨越3种未见障碍物地图、4种水质能见度系数、标称与漂移动力学，间歇性移除多普勒测速仪观测）。

Result: 在120个全新的配对环境（含计划内DVL丢失）中，AquaJEPA达成74个目标（state-only与循环世界模型均为68个），并取得最低平均最终误差0.906 m。成对最终误差相比普通多模态预测、监督动力学和循环世界模型分别减小0.273 m（95%CI: 0.190-0.356）、0.364 m（95%CI: 0.260-0.468）和0.106 m（95%CI: 0.025-0.187），均具有统计显著性。但对state-only基线的优势在统计上尚未得到明确结论。

Conclusion: AquaJEPA在聚合闭环性能上达到最优，并在成对最终误差上显著优于三个动作条件预测基线（普通多模态预测、监督动力学和循环世界模型），验证了动作条件联合嵌入预测与显式传感器有效性建模对水下机器人规划的实用价值。但其相比仅状态基线的优势仍需进一步实验验证。

Abstract: Underwater robots combine complementary sensors whose reliability changes abruptly with water visibility, viewpoint, and vehicle motion. We introduce AquaJEPA, an action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive model that fuses an RGB camera, forward-looking sonar, and proprioception with explicit sensor validity. It predicts a future latent target conditioned on eight-thruster commands and supplies velocity and sonar-profile predictions to a shared receding-horizon planner. We study the method in Stonefish against reactive, state-only, ordinary multimodal, supervised dynamics, and recurrent world-model baselines. We further isolate the EMA target, action margin, masks, and modality dropout. A preregistered 120-environment replication comprises five independent replicates of a grid crossing three unseen obstacle maps, four water-visibility coefficients, and nominal versus shifted dynamics, while intermittently removing DVL observations. In 120 fresh paired environments with scheduled DVL loss, AquaJEPA reaches 74 goals, versus 68 for both state-only and the recurrent world model, and attains the lowest mean final error (0.906 m). Paired final-error reductions relative to ordinary multimodal prediction, supervised dynamics, and the recurrent world model are 0.273 m (95% CI: 0.190-0.356), 0.364 m (0.260-0.468), and 0.106 m (0.025-0.187), respectively. AquaJEPA therefore achieves the best aggregate closed-loop performance and significantly outperforms three action-conditioned predictive baselines in paired final error; its advantage over state-only remains statistically unresolved.

</details>


### [164] [Automated Straight-line Sewing of Stretchable Fabrics with Different Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29464)
*Bingchen Jin,Akinari Kobayashi,Dipankar Bhattacharya,Akira Seino,Fuyuki Tokuda,Norman Chihnan Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DLRoSS的新型机器人缝纫系统，用于自动化"不同长度对齐缝纫"（DLAS）任务。该系统采用6自由度机械臂搭载滚筒式末端执行器，通过四阶段操作（织物缠绕、缝纫、缝纫完成、织物释放），实现对不同长度弹力织物的实时拉伸和直线缝纫，实验结果验证了其在多种材料和长度上的稳定高质量缝纫效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLAS是一项将较短织物拉伸至与较长织物对齐后进行直线缝纫的挑战性任务，自动化缝纫过程中需要同时满足多项严格要求（如实时拉伸对齐、防止织物滑脱等）。现有机器人系统难以胜任此任务。

Method: DLRoSS系统采用6自由度机械臂搭载滚筒式末端执行器。末端执行器由主动的短织物滚筒、长织物滚筒以及附着于短织物滚筒上的被动压辊组成。系统假设两层织物的一端已置于缝纫机压脚下，通过四个阶段实现自动化缝纫：(P1)织物缠绕——逐层拾取、固定并从另一端缠绕到进料滚筒上；(P2)缝纫——基于缝纫速度和先验已知的长度比，通过滚筒速度控制实时拉伸短织物并与长织物对齐；(P3)缝纫完成——在最终缝纫轮次中接合压辊，防止拉伸织物因内部张力而滑脱；(P4)织物释放——缝纫结束时织物边缘移过压辊，织物从滚筒上释放。

Result: 实验结果表明DLRoSS能够对不同材质和长度的弹力织物实现一致、高质量的缝纫效果。

Conclusion: 该系统成功解决了机器人自动化DLAS中的关键挑战，为不同长度弹性织物的自动化缝纫提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Different Length Alignment Sewing (DLAS), which involves stretching the shorter fabric to match the longer one and sewing them together in a straight line, is a challenging task that needs to satisfy several requirements when automating the sewing process. To address the challenges, this research proposes a novel robotic sewing system, Different Length Robotic Sewing System (DLRoSS), which consists of a roller type end-effector, attached to a 6-DoF manipulator. The end-effector composed of active shorter and longer fabric rollers, and a passive press-roller attached to the shorter-fabric roller. Assuming that one end of the two fabric layers are initially positioned under the sewing machine's presser foot, the system automates DLAS by operating in four distinct phases. (P1) Fabric wrapping: Individual fabric layers are picked, held, and wrapped from the other end onto the feed rollers. (P2) Sewing: During the sewing, the shorter fabric is stretched and aligned with the longer fabric in real-time using roller velocity control based on the sewing speed and apriori known length ratio. (P3) Sewing completion: In the final sewing round on the fabric rollers, the press roller is engaged to prevent the stretched fabric from slipping off due to internal tension. (P4) Sewing fabric release: At the end of sewing, the fabric edge moves past the press roller, and the fabric releases from the rollers. Experimental results demonstrate that DLRoSS achieves consistent, high-quality sewing of stretchable fabrics of different materials and lengths.

</details>


### [165] [Temporal Policy: History-Initialized Action Generation for Robotic Learning from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29482)
*Dylan Miller,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Policy，一种基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）的生成式框架，通过将生成流初始化于机器人最近的历史状态而非独立高斯噪声，实现时间耦合的传输问题，从而大幅降低传输成本并产生直线向量场，支持高频闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型和流匹配模型依赖独立高斯先验的无信息耦合，迫使模型学习复杂高成本的向量场才能到达物理动作空间。在机器人示教学习（LfD）中，生成模型虽能捕捉多模态行为，但推理成本高，难以满足高频闭环控制的需求。

Method: 将动作生成构建为时间耦合的传输问题：生成流的起点不再是独立高斯噪声，而是机器人的近期历史状态。通过这种数据相关的耦合初始化，显式关联过去状态与未来动作序列，从而简化传输几何，使向量场趋于直线，降低传输距离和计算成本。

Result: 在视觉运动仿真基准和Barrett WAM 2x 7DoF遥操作平台上验证：传输成本比噪声初始化基线降低近一个数量级，在单张NVIDIA RTX 4080上实现19.1ms推理延迟，同时保持与最先进基线相当的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过数据依赖的耦合初始化绕过独立高斯先验的计算瓶颈，简化传输几何，使生成式策略能够支撑高频闭环控制，在效率和性能上均不妥协。

Abstract: By relying on independent couplings from uninformative Gaussian priors, standard diffusion and flow matching models are forced to learn complex, high-cost vector fields to reach the physical action space. Generative models excel at capturing multimodal behaviors for robotic Learning from Demonstration (LfD), but often suffer from high inference cost. This paper introduces Temporal Policy, a generative framework based on stochastic interpolants that formulates action generation as a temporally coupled transport problem. By initializing the generative flow at the robot's recent history, we explicitly couple past states to future action sequences. This data-dependent coupling reduces transport cost and produces straight vector fields. We validate Temporal Policy across visuomotor simulation benchmarks and on a physical Barrett WAM 2x 7DoF teleoperation platform. Our approach reduces transport costs by nearly an order of magnitude compared to noise-initialized baselines, achieving a 19.1 ms inference latency on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080. Crucially, these geometric and computational efficiencies are achieved while matching the success rates of state-of-the-art baselines. This simplified transport geometry bypasses the computational bottleneck of independent Gaussian priors, helping enable high-frequency, closed-loop control. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/dmiller12/TemporalPolicy.

</details>


### [166] [Tri-Space Operational Control of Redundant Multilink and Hybrid Cable-Driven Parallel Robots Using an Iterative-Learning based Reactive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29500)
*Dipankar Bhattacharya,Yin Pok Chan,Siqi Shang,Yuen Shan Chan,Ying Tan,Darwin Lau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合反应控制（RC）和迭代学习控制（ILC）的三空间控制框架，用于冗余驱动缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPR）在操作空间执行重复任务时，同时满足执行空间、关节空间和操作空间（三空间）中的各类约束。该框架支持在线轨迹跟踪并保证缆索力可行，同时规避缆索连杆干涉、关节干涉和可操作度丧失等问题，并通过在零空间中对参数进行新颖的参数化寻优，利用ILC提升重复任务性能。仿真和硬件实验表明该框架可便捷高效地应用于多种CDPR架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余驱动的CDPR由于其两级冗余以及执行空间、关节空间和操作空间（三空间）中大量约束的存在，在操作空间跟踪给定轨迹的同时满足三空间约束极具挑战。目前尚不存在一个鲁棒、有效且可直接适用于多种冗余驱动CDPR架构的三空间控制框架。

Method: 提出了一个结合反应控制（RC）和迭代学习控制（ILC）的三空间控制框架。RC用于在线跟踪操作空间轨迹并保持缆索力可行，同时避免缆索连杆干涉、关节干涉和可操作度丧失等不良情况；ILC通过一种新颖的零空间向量参数化方法，在零空间中找到最优参数，在任务重复执行时进一步改善性能。

Result: 在多种多连杆缆索驱动机器人（MCDR）和混合缆索驱动机器人（HCDR）上进行了仿真和硬件实验，结果表明所提出的三空间控制框架能够便捷有效地应用于不同CDPR架构的实时控制。

Conclusion: 该三空间控制框架为冗余驱动CDPR提供了一种鲁棒、有效且通用性强的实时控制方案，可广泛适用于不同架构的缆索驱动并联机器人。

Abstract: Cable-Driven Parallel Robots (CDPRs) are a type of parallel mechanism in which cables are used as actuators. Due to the two levels of redundancy and numerous constraints within the CDPR actuation, joint and operational spaces (together known as the tri-space), tracking a given trajectory in the operational space while satisfying constraints in tri-space simultaneously is challenging. To the best of the authors' knowledge, there does not exist any tri-space control framework, which is robust, effective, and directly applicable to several architectures of redundantly actuated CDPRs. This paper proposes a tri-space control framework that combines Reactive Control (RC) and Iterative-Learning Control (ILC) to perform repetitive tasks in the operational space. The framework allows the tracking of operational space trajectories online with feasible cable forces, while avoiding undesirable situations such as cable-link interference, joint interference, and loss of manipulability. On the other hand, by finding an optimal parameter in the null space using a novel parameterization of a null space vector, the performance can be improved through ILC when the task is repeatedly executed. Simulation and hardware results on various Multilink Cable-Driven Robot (MCDRs) and Hybrid Cable-Driven Robots (HCDRs) show that the proposed tri-space control framework can be conveniently and effectively applied to the real-time control of different CDPRs.

</details>


### [167] [Homotopy-Aware Corridor Generation without Predefined Reference Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29513)
*Haoze Dong,Minghan Li,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需参考路径的走廊生成框架，基于凸集图（GCS）直接从自由空间表示中构建凸集序列形式的走廊；扩展了可见性形变以实现同伦类感知的走廊融合，并开发自适应多尺度GCS结合细粒度采样图与粗粒度可见性图，支持增量更新。仿真与硬件实验验证了高效性、稳定性和优于现有基线的同伦感知轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有走廊生成方法大多依赖预定义参考路径，这会使走廊几何产生偏差，并隐式限制可探索的同伦类。作者希望打破对参考路径的依赖，让走廊结构直接从自由空间表示中涌现，同时解决现有GCS方法基于静态全局分解而适应性不足的问题。

Method: （1）在GCS框架上直接生成凸集序列作为走廊，无需参考路径；（2）将可见性形变从路径扩展到凸集序列，用于度量走廊间的相似性，融合拓扑冗余走廊同时保留不同备选方案；（3）提出自适应多尺度GCS：采样驱动的细尺度图支持局部更新，基于可见性的粗尺度图支持紧凑全局探索，两层保持拓扑一致性，实现无需重建全图的增量更新。

Result: 数值实验表征了GCS构建、走廊生成、同伦感知探索和局部更新性能，结果表明图构建高效、轨迹级性能稳定，且同伦感知轨迹比现有基线耗时更短。地面与空中机器人硬件实验（包括机载定位部署）在平移及未知障碍物场景下验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无需参考路径的GCS走廊生成框架能够从自由空间表示直接获取走廊结构，并通过自适应多尺度设计与拓扑一致的增量更新机制，在环境不确定性下保持高效性与鲁棒性，为同伦感知的机器人运动规划提供了新的途径。
