Abstract: Generating safe corridors is essential for collision-free robotic motion planning, yet most existing methods rely on predefined reference paths, which bias corridor geometry and implicitly limit the homotopy classes that can be explored. We propose a reference-path-free corridor generation framework on graphs of convex sets (GCS) that constructs corridors directly as sequences of convex sets, allowing corridor structure to emerge from the free-space representation rather than from a guiding path. To reason about similarity among corridors, we extend visibility-based deformation from paths to convex-set sequences, enabling the fusion of topologically redundant corridors while preserving distinct alternatives. To overcome the limited adaptability of existing GCS methods based on static global decompositions, we further develop an adaptive multi-scale GCS, in which a sampling-based fine-scale graph supports localized updates and a visibility-based coarse-scale graph enables compact global exploration. The two levels maintain topological consistency, allowing incremental updates without full graph reconstruction under environmental uncertainty. Numerical experiments characterize GCS construction, corridor generation, homotopy-aware exploration, and local updates, showing efficient graph construction, stable trajectory-level performance, and shorter-duration homotopy-aware trajectories than existing baselines. Hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots, including deployment with onboard localization, further validate the framework under translated and previously unknown obstacles.

</details>


### [168] [STAGE: STyle-controllable Action GEneration for personalized autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29517)
*Zihao Liu,Xing Liu,Yizhai Zhang,Panfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STAGE的风格可控动作生成方法，通过模仿学习结合风格值与潜在值动作模态编码进行训练，利用偏好学习识别用户驾驶风格为连续单调的风格值，并设计了规则比较数据对中的驾驶风格以降低人工成本。推理时用户输入风格值即可动态控制生成的动作模式，实验验证了其在风格可控性、连续性、驾驶风格对齐能力和驾驶安全性方面的独特功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户使用自动驾驶系统时，期望系统的驾驶风格贴近自身习惯，但当前工业级自动驾驶系统难以实现个性化的风格模仿。因此需要一种能够生成风格可控动作的方法，使系统能够与用户自身驾驶习惯保持一致。

Method: 基于模仿学习训练，引入风格值与潜在值动作模态编码；采用偏好学习将用户驾驶风格识别为连续单调的风格值；开发规则集以比较数据对中的驾驶风格，降低偏好训练中人工参与的代价；推理阶段通过用户输入风格值直接控制生成动作模式。

Result: 在多个典型道路场景下，风格控制的动作生成结果与人类期望显著对齐；通过与多种其他方法的对比，展示了STAGE在风格可控性、风格连续性、驾驶风格对齐能力和驾驶安全性方面的独特功能。

Conclusion: STAGE方法实现了自动驾驶系统中风格可控的动作生成，能够根据用户输入的连续风格值动态满足个性化驾驶期望，在风格对齐和安全性上均表现出优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: Driving style refers to the behavioral preferences that drivers maintain during driving, shaped by their diverse experiences, habits, and needs, and is typically reflected in varying levels of aggressiveness. If humans choose to use autonomous driving systems, they would expect the driving style of the systems to closely resemble their own habit. However, this is challenging for current industrial autonomous driving systems. To address this, we developed a style controllable action generation method, STAGE, for driving tasks. Its training process is based on imitation learning, incorporating both style value and latent value action modality encoding. Preference learning is then used to identify the user's driving style as a continuous, monotonic style value. And to reduce the cost of human involvement in the preference training process, we also developed a set of rules to compare driving style in data pairs. Then, during inference, the user inputs the style value to control the generated action patterns, dynamically meeting the user's expectations. Using the STAGE method, we verified that the style-controlled action generation results in several typical road scenarios significantly align with human expectations. Furthermore, through comparisons between the STAGE method and various other approaches, we reveal the unique functionalities of STAGE, including its style controllability, style continuity, driving style alignment capability and driving safety. The code for this work is available at: https://github.com/CarlDegio/STAGE

</details>


### [169] [TransGraspNet: Physically and Geometrically Consistent Manipulation of Transparent Labware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29567)
*Hailing Hu,Mingyi Zhu,Yiquan An,Yifei Tian,Tianyou Zuo,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TransGraspNet，一个几何-物理一致的透明玻璃器皿机器人抓取框架，通过边界一致性、表面一致性和物理一致性三个耦合原则，实现从感知到执行的端到端一致性约束，在杂乱场景和高速液体运输中实现高抓取成功率和零溅洒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明玻璃器皿操纵是安全关键的（即使是微小几何误差也会导致抓取不稳定和液体溅洒）。现有系统将透明物体检测、深度重建和抓取规划独立优化，导致跨阶段的输出不一致：不完美的边界引起深度混叠，畸变表面破坏法向估计，任务无关的抓取评分产生倾斜或偏心抓取，在动态运动下失效。

Method: TransGraspNet通过三个耦合原则明确施加从感知到执行的一致性：（1）边界一致性——产生结构可靠的物体轮廓作为下游先验；（2）表面一致性——在深度重建过程中保持几何保真度和表面法向精度；（3）物理一致性——通过质心对齐和力旋量空间稳定性细化抓取选择，实现竖直且动态鲁棒的操作。

Result: 在公开基准、专用透明玻璃器皿数据集和真实机器人平台上评估。结果显示边界质量和表面法向保真度提升，在杂乱透明场景中任务级性能显著；真实世界操作实现了高抓取成功率（杂乱场景）和高速液体运输中零溅洒。

Conclusion: 提出的系统在真实世界可靠运行，验证了几何-物理一致框架在安全关键的透明物体操纵中的有效性，显著优于传统独立优化的方法。

Abstract: Manipulating transparent laboratory glassware that contains liquid is inherently safety-critical: even small geometric errors can cause unstable grasps and hazardous spillage. Although recent progress has been made in transparent object perception and robotic grasping, most existing systems optimize detection, depth reconstruction, and grasp planning independently, which leads to cross-stage inconsistency imperfect boundaries induce depth bleeding, distorted surfaces corrupt normal estimation, and task agnostic grasp scoring yields tilted or off-center grasps that fail under dynamic motion. In this paper, we propose TransGraspNet, a geometry physics consistent framework that explicitly enforces consistency from perception to execution through three coupled principles: boundary consistency to produce structurally reliable object contours as downstream priors, surface consistency to preserve geometric fidelity and surface normal accuracy during depth reconstruction, and physics consistency to refine grasp selection with centroid alignment and wrench-space stability for upright and dynamically robust manipulation. We evaluate TransGraspNet on public benchmarks, a dedicated transparent glassware dataset, and a real robotic platform. The results show improved boundary quality and surface normal fidelity, and demonstrate strong task-level performance in cluttered transparent scenes. Most importantly, the proposed system achieves reliable real-world operation, including high grasp success rates in clutter and zero spillage during high speed liquid transport, highlighting the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [170] [Safe Vision Language Action Models via Barrier Enhanced Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29569)
*Kasra Sinaei,Hung-Chieh Wu,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种模块化推理框架，将Flow Matching生成模型与控制屏障函数（CBF）安全保证相结合，通过在去噪过程中强制执行安全性来生成安全的机器人轨迹。采用平滑的Log-Sum-Exponential聚合屏障函数在动作块级别保证安全，理论上证明2-Wasserstein距离保持有界，无需安全数据集或模型重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将安全过滤作为外部模块应用到模型的最终输出上，这种方式可能改变模型语义意图且在较长的动作块上难以保证安全性。作者旨在从生成过程的内部机制上嵌入安全约束，避免额外数据集和重训练成本，同时不牺牲模型性能。

Method: 1) 修改Flow Matching去噪过程，在模型内部而非外部施加CBF安全约束；2) 采用平滑的Log-Sum-Exponential聚合屏障函数，在完整动作块（action chunks）上强制执行安全；3) 该聚合屏障保证计算开销最小化且不改变模型语义意图；4) 通过理论分析证明生成分布与目标分布之间的2-Wasserstein距离有界。

Result: 在两个机器人操作平台和一个2D导航基准上验证，框架在保证可靠安全性的同时不降低模型成功率。

Conclusion: 该框架为安全生成式轨迹规划提供了一种通用解决方案，无需安全专用数据集或昂贵的模型重训练，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可靠的推理阶段安全保障。

Abstract: This article presents a modular inference framework that integrates Flow Matching generative models with formal Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety guarantees. Unlike existing methods that apply external safety filters to a model's final output, our approach modifies the Flow Matching denoising process within the model to inherently generate safe trajectories. By employing a smooth Log-Sum-Exponential aggregate barrier, we enforce safety over entire action chunks. This aggregate barrier ensures a minimal increase in computational overhead and does not alter the semantic intent of the model. We show that, within the proposed framework, the 2-Wasserstein distance between the generated distribution and the target distribution remains bounded. Our method eliminates the need for safety-specific datasets or costly model retraining, providing a versatile solution for safe inference. We validate the approach on two robotic manipulation platforms and a 2D navigation benchmark, verifying that our framework achieves reliable safety without degrading the success rate of the model.

</details>


### [171] [FibVLA: An Efficient Temporal Vision-Language-Action Model with Fibonacci Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29596)
*Li Lin,Wujun Xu,Weiwei Meng,Kaiwen Xia,Kang Hao Cheong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FibVLA，一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的高效框架，通过对数后验抽样捕获长期时间依赖，并结合流匹配和斐波那契循环推理策略生成动作分布和长期规划步骤，在保持长上下文历史感知的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型主要关注当前数字认知，虽然已有工作通过捕获时间信息增强推理能力，但编码长上下文历史会导致效率下降。本文旨在解决捕获时间信息与维持推理效率之间的冲突。

Method: 1) 对数后验抽样（logarithmic hindsight sampling）：分别应用于本体感觉状态和视觉帧，以最小冗余捕获长期时间依赖。2) 动作专家模块使用流匹配（flow matching）生成动作分布。3) 斐波那契循环推理策略（Fibonacci recurrent inference strategy）基于实时闭环反馈生成长范围规划步骤。

Result: 实验表明FibVLA显著提升了动作平滑度和成功率，且无需重训大规模视觉编码器。效率分析显示在真实世界评估中，相比基于视频的基线方法具有更优的实时响应能力。

Conclusion: FibVLA通过精心设计的时序采样与推理策略，成功兼顾了VLA模型对长期时间依赖的捕获能力和实时推理效率，为具身AI的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs), which leverage the cognition of multimodal information to infer physical-world actions, provide a generalized solution for embodied AI applications. Conventional VLAs usually concentrate on current digital cognition. While some efforts are made to enhance VLAs' reasoning capabilities by capturing temporal information, encoding the long-context history causes an efficiency-decreasing issue. To reconcile the conflict between capturing temporal information and maintaining inference efficiency in VLAs, this paper introduces FibVLA, an efficient framework featuring temporal perception of long-context history. Specifically, we leverage logarithmic hindsight sampling to both proprioceptive states and visual frames to capture long-term temporal dependencies with minimal redundancy. For the action expert, we introduce the flow matching to produce action distributions, and the Fibonacci recurrent inference strategy to generate long-range planning steps based on real-time closed-loop feedback. Experiments demonstrate that FibVLA significantly improves action smoothness and success rates without retraining large-scale visual encoders. Efficiency analysis demonstrates superior real-time responsiveness compared to video-based baselines in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [172] [HAM-VLN: Harnessing Hierarchical Agentic Memory for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29600)
*An Liu,Bingxi Liu,Hongyu Ding,Yixuan Jiang,Yaran Chen,Fulin Tang,Cong Leng,Hong Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HAM-VLN提出了一种无需训练的视觉语言导航方法，通过决策耦合的智能体作者记忆（depth-grounded world graph）在每次动作决策时同时记录语义与反思信息，配合相关性、近期性和显著性检索及一跳拓扑扩展来控制上下文长度，在多个导航基准上显著提升性能并将上下文长度压缩超过65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需训练的VLN方法依赖多模态大语言模型理解观测并规划动作，但长时程导航中无论使用图像流还是稠密地图，都会不可避免地导致记忆与推理瓶颈（不断增长的上下文开销和推理负担）。

Method: HAM-VLN构建一个持久化的、基于深度感知的世界图（depth-grounded world graph）。在每次调用大模型选择下一动作的同一过程中，模型同时记录语义和反思信息（包括房间类型、物体、导航进度和失败笔记）。近期航点以逐字形式保存在有界窗口中，更早的历史仅通过按相关性、近期性和显著性评分检索重新进入上下文，并结合一跳拓扑扩展。该设计不需要额外的大模型调用。

Result: 在VLN-CE R2R上取得61.0%成功率（SR），VLN-CE RxR上取得52.7% SR，HM3D-v2 ObjectNav上取得79.7% SR，均在无训练条件下实现；相比先前方法，上下文长度减少超过65%。

Conclusion: HAM-VLN通过决策耦合的智能体自主记忆机制，以零额外LLM调用实现了高效的长期导航，在提高导航指标的同时大幅压缩上下文长度，验证了结构化持久记忆在无训练VLN范式中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow instructions in previously unseen environments. Recently, a training-free paradigm has emerged: the robot queries a multimodal LLM to understand its observations and plan the next action. However, long-horizon navigation based on either image streams or dense map inevitably introduces a growing memory and reasoning bottleneck. We present HAM-VLN, a decision-coupled, agent-authored memory that equips the robot with a persistent, depth-grounded world graph. In the same model call used to select the next action, HAM-VLN also records semantic and reflective information---including room type, objects, navigation progress, and failure notes. Recent waypoints remain verbatim within a bounded window, while older history re-enters the context only through retrieval scored by relevance, recency, and salience, together with one-hop topological expansion. This design requires no additional LLM calls beyond the per-waypoint decision. Compared to previous methods, HAM-VLN not only improves various navigation metrics but also reduces the context length by more than 65%. Specifically, HAM-VLN achieves 61.0% Success Rate (SR) on VLN-CE R2R, 52.7% SR on VLN-CE RxR, and 79.7% SR on HM3D-v2 ObjectNav without any training.

</details>


### [173] [WCM: A World Critic Model for Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29613)
*Senyu Fei,Xiaopeng Yu,Siyin Wang,Xianzhong Zhao,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出世界评论家模型（WCM），通过在评论家表示中显式引入未来潜状态预测的世界建模目标，解决VLA模型中基于评论家的RL后训练在部分可观测机器人控制场景下的状态近似问题，在149个任务和7个真实世界操作任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评论家的强化学习方法在VLA模型后训练中，价值估计器通常只依赖单帧观测或单帧VLM骨干潜变量，这与机器人控制的部分可观测本质存在根本性不匹配。简单地将观测历史引入评论家会导致视觉高维空间的指数复杂度，且纯标量回报回归无法提供足够的监督来学习跨时间动态。根本原因是缺乏显式的世界建模目标，评论家的表示无法捕捉价值估计所需的时间结构。

Method: 提出WCM，基于轻量级LeJEPA架构，联合预测未来潜状态和估计价值，使评论家表示被显式训练以捕捉时间动态而非仅回归标量回报。WCM可无缝集成到在线和离策略训练流程中，兼容Pi0、Pi0.5和OpenVLA-OFT等前沿VLA骨干模型。

Result: 在四个基准的149个任务上，WCM在分布内和分布外设置中均一致实现最先进性能，特别是在泛化方面表现突出。在7个真实世界操作任务（使用OpenVLA-OFT和Pi0.5搭配离策略RL）上验证了跨多样化设置的稳定部署能力。

Conclusion: WCM通过显式世界建模目标解决评论家状态近似问题，证明在评论家表示中引入未来潜状态预测能显著提升VLA模型RL后训练的效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models has shown strong promise for robotic manipulation. Among RL methods, critic-based approaches rely on a value estimator that predominantly operates on single-frame observations or single-frame VLM backbone latents, which is a fundamental mismatch with the partially observable nature of robot control. A naive approach to incorporate observation history into the critic incurs exponential complexity with high-dimensional visual space, and still fails because pure scalar-return regression provides insufficient supervision for learning cross-temporal dynamics. We identify the root cause as a state approximation problem: without an explicit world modeling objective, the critic's representation cannot capture the temporal structure needed for accurate value estimation. To address this, we propose the World Critic Model (WCM), built on a lightweight LeJEPA architecture; WCM jointly predicts future latent state and estimates values, such that the critic's representation is explicitly trained to capture temporal dynamics rather than merely regress scalar returns. WCM integrates seamlessly into both on-policy and off-policy training pipelines and is compatible with state-of-the-art VLA backbones including Pi0, Pi0.5, and OpenVLA-OFT. Extensive experiments on 149 tasks across four benchmarks demonstrate that WCM consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, with particularly strong generalization gains. We further validate WCM on seven real-world manipulation tasks using OpenVLA-OFT and Pi0.5 with off-policy RL, confirming stable deployment across diverse settings.

</details>


### [174] [RayViT: Ray-Conditioned Visual Representations for Viewpoint-Robust Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29622)
*Qian Wang,Longrui Chen,Peiran Sun,Aleksandar Taranovic,Niklas Freymuth,Ge Li,Weiran Liao,C. F. Maximilian Nagy,Yucheng Tan,Tao Chen,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RayViT（射线条件视觉Transformer编码器），通过将相机几何信息以Plücker射线图形式注入预训练ViT骨干网络，使用门控交叉注意力生成几何感知的类令牌，并辅以余弦相似度损失，显著提升视觉模仿学习策略在相机扰动下的鲁棒性，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均取得大幅优于基线模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿学习允许机器人直接从图像中获取视觉运动技能，但RGB观测缺乏显式几何线索，导致学到的策略对相机扰动（如位姿变化）非常脆弱。现有方法未充分利用相机几何信息来增强策略的几何鲁棒性。

Method: RayViT将相机几何表示为Plücker射线图，将其patch化生成射线特征；通过门控交叉注意力产生射线条件类令牌；射线特征作为密集位置嵌入添加到ViT骨干中，射线条件类令牌替代原始ViT类令牌提供几何感知的摘要表示；同时结合辅助余弦相似度损失以持续提升几何感知令牌的性能与鲁棒性。整个架构轻量，可注入预训练ViT骨干。

Result: 在多任务RoboCasa基准的相机扰动下，鲁棒性相对基线提升约13个百分点；在真实世界多任务成功率方面，平均完成阶段数相比基线提升1.78。

Conclusion: RayViT通过将相机几何显式注入预训练ViT，有效解决了RGB观测缺乏几何线索导致的相机扰动脆弱性问题，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均验证了其有效性和泛化能力，为视觉模仿学习提供了轻量而稳健的编码方案。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning enables robots to acquire visuomotor skills directly from images, yet RGB observations lack explicit geometric cues, making learned policies brittle to camera perturbations. To address this, we propose \textbf{Ray-conditioned Vision Transformer Encoder (RayViT)}, a lightweight architecture that injects camera geometry into pretrained ViT backbones. RayViT represents camera geometry as a Plücker ray map, patchifies it into ray features, and uses gated cross-attention to produce a ray-conditioned class token. These ray features are added as dense positional embeddings, while the ray class token replaces the original ViT class token to provide a geometry-aware summary representation. We combine this approach with an auxiliary cosine similarity loss to consistently improve the performance and robustness for geometry-aware tokens. Experiments on sim- and real-robot tasks demonstrate that RayViT improves robustness by approximately 13 percentage points under camera perturbations in multi-task RoboCasa benchmark and by 1.78 average completed stages in real-world multi-task success rate compared to baselines.

</details>


### [175] [Balancing of Humanoid with Object Mass: Trade-off Analyses and Lifting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29625)
*Hyunjong Song,William Z. Peng,Joo H. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了物体质量对仿人机器人载物操作任务中平衡稳定性的动态影响。通过在全身动力学中引入物体质量参数，构建平衡状态盆地（BSB）以划分质心状态空间，并提出了临界质量和过渡质量两个关键量，最终将BSB作为显式阈值约束集成到全身轨迹优化中，实现了稳定的物体举升控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人载物操作（loco-manipulation）需求日益增长，但现有稳定性控制方法多依赖启发式或机器学习技术，缺乏对物体质量动力学效应的严格分析，导致平衡稳定性难以系统保证。

Method: 1) 在全身动力学中显式建模物体质量参数，结合分布式接触力和接触点处的压力中心；2) 量化物体质量对系统动量和约束的非线性影响；3) 构建平衡状态盆地（BSB）划分双足系统的质心状态空间；4) 定义临界质量（平衡能力最大时的质量）和过渡质量（激活不同限制因素的质量）；5) 将BSB作为显式阈值约束集成到全身轨迹优化中，用于物体举升控制。

Result: 在不同支撑基座、驱动能力和姿态条件下系统比较了BSB，刻画了物体质量对平衡能力的影响，表征了动量调节与平衡限制因素之间的权衡关系。通过仿人机器人和解析可约化的降阶机构验证了BSB在预测和控制中的作用，并在仿真和实验中成功完成了不同质量属性物体的举升-保持和举升-释放任务。

Conclusion: BSB为分析物体质量对双足系统平衡能力的影响提供了系统化框架，临界质量和过渡质量揭示了动量调节与限制因素间的权衡机制，将BSB作为显式约束可有效提升仿人机器人载物操作的稳定性。

Abstract: The demand for humanoid loco-manipulation tasks with an object has recently increased, and most existing control approaches for stability in such tasks rely on heuristics or machine-learning techniques. This study rigorously analyzes and exploits the dynamic effects of the object mass on balance stability. By formulating the object mass parameters in the whole-body dynamics with distributed contact wrenches and centers of pressure at the stance contacts, their nonlinear effects on the system momenta and constraints are quantified. The dynamic models and constraints are incorporated into the construction of the balanced state basin/boundary (BSB), a partition of the center-of-mass state space for a biped system to maintain balance in its desired contacts. The implications of the BSB for prediction and control are highlighted using a humanoid robot and an analytically tractable reduced-order mechanism. The BSBs under different conditions of base of support, actuation capacity, and pose provide systematic analyses of the effects of object mass on the balancing capability of a system. In particular, the trade-off relationships between momentum regulation and limiting factors in balancing are characterized, introducing two key quantities of the object: the critical mass, at which the system's balancing capability is maximum, and the transition mass, which activates different limiting factors. In addition, sufficient conditions for imposing balanced states on a trajectory are established and implemented with BSBs as explicit threshold constraints in the whole-body trajectory optimization for stable object-lifting control of the humanoid, demonstrating the lift-and-hold and lift-and-release tasks with distinct mass properties in simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [176] [Bootstrapping Self-Supervised Learning of Binary Classification Using Error Bounds: A Case Study on a Robotic Insertion Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29640)
*Zebin Duan,Norbert Krüger,Juan Heredia,Thorbjørn Mosekjær Iversen,Frederik Hagelskjær*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据引擎系统，在执行任务的同时收集数据并提升性能，通过"快速模型预测+昂贵验证"的双分类器架构，利用置信度阈值控制误差水平，并在真实机器人插装任务中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性制造要求快速部署和最小化设置时间，但传统部署流程需要大量数据收集、模型训练和参数调优，导致延迟严重、阻碍商业可行性。需要一个能边执行边学习、同时可控制误差水平的系统。

Method: 提出一个由快速模型预测和昂贵验证两个分类器组成的数据引擎。先进行模型预测，根据预测置信度决定是否调用昂贵验证。用户通过调整置信度阈值来控制可容忍错误水平。在真实机器人插装任务中实现，使用力数据作为模型输入，采用UMAP降维，用Wilson-Score计算预测置信区间。

Result: 实验结果表明系统能够随时间学习并减少对昂贵验证的依赖，同时保持在设定的错误率范围内。置信区间在自改进模型中增强了机器人分类任务的可靠性。

Conclusion: 置信度边界在自改进模型中具有潜力，能够提升机器人分类任务的可靠性，实现边执行边学习并控制误差的可商用柔性制造方案。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires rapid deployment of solutions and minimal setup time to remain competitive. An essential attribute is the ability to control error levels, as failures can range from minor performance degradation to severe equipment damage. However, conventional deployment often involves extensive setup, data collection, model training or parameter tuning, and system testing, resulting in significant delays that hinder commercial feasibility. We propose a data engine which gathers data and improves its performance while executing the task. The data engine consists of two classifiers, a fast model prediction and expensive verification. First, a model prediction is performed and based on the confidence level of the prediction, the expensive verification can be used. By adjusting the confidence level, users can control the level of tolerable error.
  Our method is implemented on a real-world robotic insertion task, which uses force data for the model prediction. The system applies UMAP dimensionality reduction and uses Wilson-Score to compute the confidence bounds of the prediction. Results demonstrate the ability to learn and reduce the need for expensive verifications over time, while staying within the set error-rate. The results highlight the potential of confidence bounds in self-improving models to enhance reliability in robotic classification task.

</details>


### [177] [Diagnosing Compositional Generalization in Sequential Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29687)
*Yixiao Wang,Cheng-En Wu,Lingfeng Sun,Pengcheng Wang,Xiang Ji,Boyuan Liang,Guojian Zhan,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过指令空间覆盖的视角研究顺序机器人操作中的组合泛化问题，将泛化差距分解为边际指令偏移、指令组合偏移和上下文-动作偏移三个来源，发现结构化子集（仅占全任务空间四分之一）在覆盖动作相关依赖时即可恢复强力OOD性能，且稀疏训练失败的主要原因是指令引导而非低级技能缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序机器人操作需要策略执行熟悉指令组件的新颖组合，但为所有可能指令元组收集示范的组合成本极高，而稀疏覆盖的数据集在分布外重组下往往失败。需要理解何时稀疏训练覆盖足够，以及训练集需要保留什么结构才能实现可靠的行动预测。

Method: 将泛化差距分解为三个来源：边际指令偏移（marginal instruction shift）、指令组合偏移（instruction-compositional shift）和上下文-动作偏移（context-action shift），并利用该分解诊断稀疏训练覆盖的充分条件及训练集需要保持的结构。

Result: 穷举元组枚举并非必要：仅占全任务空间四分之一的结构化子集在覆盖动作相关依赖时即可恢复强分布外性能；稀疏训练失败常源于指令引导问题而非低级技能缺失，仅对每任务微调一个示范即可将OOD成功率从0.4%提升到54.7%；对于语义依赖任务，有效覆盖需捕获关系结构而非仅因子多样性。

Conclusion: 高效的机器人数据收集应优先关注指令空间中的依赖覆盖，而非穷举式任务扩展。

Abstract: Sequential robot manipulation requires policies to execute novel combinations of familiar instruction components. However, collecting demonstrations for all possible instruction tuples is combinatorially expensive, while sparsely covered datasets often fail under out-of-distribution recombination. This paper studies compositional generalization through the lens of instruction-space coverage. We decompose the generalization gap into three sources: \textit{marginal instruction shift}, \textit{instruction-compositional shift}, and \textit{context--action shift}. This decomposition allows us to diagnose when sparse training coverage is sufficient, and what structure the training set must preserve for reliable action prediction. Our results show that exhaustive tuple enumeration is unnecessary: a structured subset, as small as one quarter of the full task space, can recover strong out-of-distribution performance when it covers action-relevant dependencies. We further find that sparse training often fails due to instruction steering rather than missing low-level skills; finetuning only one demonstration per task improves OOD success from \(0.4\%\) to \(54.7\%\). For semantically dependent tasks, effective coverage must capture relational structure rather than only factor diversity. These findings suggest that efficient robot data collection should prioritize dependency coverage in instruction space over exhaustive task expansion. More results are available in the supplementary material. Project website: https://yixiaowang7.github.io/Diagnosing_Compositional_Generalization_Robot_Page/.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Quaternionic Response Geometry for Proteins: Toward a Noncommutative Theory of Ordered Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29101)
*Xiaoting Chen,Chon-Fai Kam,Yu Li,David Medina-Ortiz,Cedric Damour,Jean Pierre Chabriat,Alain Miranville,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于四元数框架传输的蛋白质变形轨迹描述框架，能够区分具有相似端点构象但变形历史顺序不同的蛋白质运动。该框架通过沿着蛋白质主链的四元数框架传输编码非交换传输代数，并构建谱响应层（Dirac型算子、谱发散、重正规化谱密度和混合响应形式），在理想α-螺旋上验证了顺序记忆信号的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质功能可能同时依赖端点构象及其到达这些构象的有序变形历史，这一区别对变构、构象转换、突变诱导重排和上位效应至关重要。现有状态或端点中心的表征方法无法保留这种顺序敏感信息。

Method: 提出变形优先（deformation-first）几何框架：将局部主链框架提升为四元数变量，通过Ω(ℓ)=2 q(ℓ)⁻¹∂_ℓ q(ℓ)编码无穷小旋转；有序拼接可容许变形路径生成非交换传输代数；在此基础上构建包括全局Dirac型算子、局部谱发散、重正规化谱密度和混合响应形式的谱响应层。

Result: 在理想α-螺旋上的最小实现表明，局部螺距和弯曲扰动可以产生相似的端点描述符，同时保留非零的顺序记忆信号。框架将顺序敏感的传输记忆扇区（在交换阴影下丢失）与仍然可见的谱响应扇区分离开来。

Conclusion: 该框架可为变构切换、突变顺序效应、构象记忆和路径依赖响应的分析提供理论基础，适用于分子动力学轨迹、NMR系综、结构家族和几何生成模型的输出。

Abstract: Protein function may depend on both endpoint conformations and the ordered deformation histories by which they are reached. This distinction is relevant to allostery, conformational switching, mutation-induced rearrangements, and epistatic effects, where different perturbation sequences may produce similar structures while retaining distinct internal transport histories. Current state- or endpoint-centered representations may not preserve this order-sensitive information. We therefore provide a foundation for descriptors of protein deformation trajectories that distinguish ordered histories even when endpoint conformations are similar. Such descriptors could support analyses of allosteric switching, mutation-order effects, conformational memory, and path-dependent response in molecular-dynamics trajectories, NMR ensembles, structural families, and outputs of geometric generative models. We propose a deformation-first geometric framework based on quaternionic frame transport along the protein backbone. Local backbone frames are lifted to quaternionic variables, with infinitesimal rotation encoded by \(Ω(\ell)=2\,q(\ell)^{-1}\partial_\ell q(\ell)\). Ordered concatenation of admissible deformation paths generates a noncommutative transport algebra, recording that deformation A followed by B need not be equivalent to B followed by A. From this ordered transport layer, we construct a spectral-response layer comprising a global Dirac-type operator, local spectral germs, a renormalized spectral density, and a mixed response form. A minimal realization on an idealized \(α\)-helix shows how localized pitch and bending perturbations can yield similar endpoint descriptors while preserving a nonzero order-memory signal. The framework separates an order-sensitive transport-memory sector, lost under a commutative shadow, from a spectral-response sector that remains visible.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层架构的Agentic AI统一框架，将LLM推理、编排和执行为三层，并通过OpenClaw-Ollama全栈原型验证了持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成，且随架构复杂度提升而改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从响应式向持久性、可行动系统过渡的过程中，缺乏统一的全栈Agentic AI架构设计和评估框架，特别是在推理、编排与执行的层分离方面存在架构理解上的关键空白。

Method: 提出分层架构将Agentic AI划分为推理层、编排层和执行层；以Ollama作为LLM推理层、OpenClaw作为Agent运行时编排层的全栈系统，通过原型实验验证系统级集成效应，并分析可扩展性、安全、隐私、治理与评估等挑战。

Result: 实验证明持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非单一模型，性能随架构复杂度提升而一致改善；同时识别出可扩展性和治理等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 建立了Agentic AI的统一架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并给出了构建可扩展、安全、可信的智能体系统的路线图，同时公开全部模型、代码和数据以支持可复现性与基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [180] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个基于自动化同行评审的AI科学家系统基准测试协议，利用前沿大语言模型（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）在原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文质量。对Sakana AI (v1&v2)、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper四个框架及FARS基准论文进行评测，发现FARS论文显著优于其他系统，且Gemini与Claude的评审结果高度一致，验证了多模型LLM评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家系统（能够自主研究的系统）有望大幅加速科学发现，但评估和比较AI生成论文质量仍是一个未解决的开放挑战。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立首个定量基准来评估AI Scientist系统的研究质量。

Method: 提出一个严格的基准测试协议，使用自动化同行评审系统，通过三个独立的LLM评审器（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）在四个核心维度（原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度、重要性）评估论文。具体流程：对4个AI Scientist框架（Sakana AI v1&v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper），每个框架在15个一致的FARS研究提案上运行，生成60篇论文，与15篇FARS基准论文一起由三个LLM评审器独立评估。

Result: FARS基准论文在所有评估中显著优于其他框架，平均得分2.14-2.47（1-5分制），而其他系统仅为1.00-1.87。Gemini和Claude的评估高度一致（ρ=0.907, p<0.001），两者与综合得分极强相关（ρ=0.961）。但GPT-5.4与其他评审器一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其使用不同标准评估。FARS在Gemini和Claude评估中得分是次优系统的2倍以上。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI Scientist系统的定量基准，证明了多模型LLM评审可提供可扩展、一致的自动评估框架。同时指出不同LLM评审者之间可能存在的标准差异（如GPT-5.4），为未来自动化学术评审系统的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [181] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个三阶段流水线用于重大数学猜想发现：区域搜索（从局部证据模块中搜索）、反思性验证（评估基础性、新颖性和潜在意义）、以及Lean 4和Mathlib中的形式化验证。实验在20个候选上显示全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，全部未被exact?直接吸收或aesop自动调度，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，目前缺乏统一的、系统化的方法来生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想，因此需要一种能自动发现高"数学品味"问题的流水线。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索——基于显式局部证据模块搜索候选猜想；(2) 反思性验证——评估猜想的基础性（foundationality）、新颖性（novelty）和潜在重要性（potential significance）；(3) 形式化验证——在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行正式验证。

Result: 在20个候选猜想的实验中：全部（20/20）通过Lean解析和类型检查；全部（20/20）未被exact?直接吸收；全部（20/20）未被aesop自动调度；没有显式重复或近重复。表明从自然语言到形式化检查的稳定转化。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够系统化地发现具有高问题品味（high problem taste）的数学猜想，即那些证明可能重构研究领域语言、为人类数学研究提供持久帮助的问题，并能够稳定地将发现从自然语言推进到形式化验证阶段。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [182] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ThinkReset框架，在受限上下文窗口下通过显式构建可复用中间接口（interface writeback and reset）替代被丢弃的历史轨迹，并直接优化重置后的继续求解成功率，从而在多个长时推理基准上持续提升固定窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理虽能提升复杂问题性能，但引入冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定等问题。现有方法聚焦轨迹压缩或测试时控制，但作者认为在受限上下文窗口下核心瓶颈是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史。此外，结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习存在关键失败模式：窗口接近耗尽时模型未完成任务，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: 提出ThinkReset框架，在文本空间中显式构造可复用的中间接口，通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）机制实现，并直接优化重置后继续求解（post-reset continuation）的成功率。

Result: 在多个长时推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口下一致地提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 作者论证了在受限上下文窗口下，长链推理的核心瓶颈在于缺乏可复用的中间接口；通过ThinkReset的实验验证，该视角能够有效提升长时推理在固定上下文窗口下的表现。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [183] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter），一个通过强化学习（GRPO）训练的提示词改写模型，将用户原始提示词改写为任务优化的提示词，以提升下游LLM的性能。实验表明在问答、摘要和算术推理等任务上均取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常需要精心设计的提示词才能发挥全部潜力，这对非专家用户构成了使用障碍。现有方法缺乏针对特定任务的提示词优化机制，因此需要一种自动化的、任务感知的提示词改写方法来降低提示工程门槛。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)作为强化学习训练策略，以LLM-as-judge的评估结果作为奖励信号，同时评估改写后的提示词质量和对应任务的输出质量。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为TAPR的基础模型进行微调。

Result: 在多种任务（问答、摘要、算术推理）上相对于基础模型在提示词改写能力上取得一致的性能提升，在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准测试上获得更高的准确率，生成的提示词包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言。

Conclusion: 任务感知的提示词改写器通过RL训练能够有效提升提示词质量和下游任务性能，为降低提示工程门槛提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [184] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR是一个面向跨域序列推荐（CDSR）的生成式推荐框架，通过跨域混合分词机制（多塔架构+层次共享/特异码本）联合捕捉跨域共性与领域特性，并提出跨域串并行解码策略利用层次化语义标识结构部分并行化生成，在三个公开数据集上平均准确率提升1.5%，推理延迟降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法在CDSR场景下存在两个关键问题：(1) 分词（tokenization）过程中忽视了跨域协同相关性；(2) 采用波束搜索等低效解码策略，阻碍实时部署。

Method: (1) 设计跨域混合分词机制，采用多塔架构，通过层次化的共享-特异（shared-specific）码本和细粒度码本联合捕获跨域共性与领域差异；(2) 提出跨域串并行解码策略，利用层次化语义标识（SID）结构部分并行化生成过程，在保证生成一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，相比当前最优基线方法，GenCDSR平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%。

Conclusion: GenCDSR验证了通过层次化共享/特异码本分词与串并行解码策略，能够在保持甚至提升推荐精度的前提下大幅提升生成式CDSR的推理效率，为生成式推荐的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [185] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个生产级知识图谱抽取系统，通过本地托管的Qwen3.5-9B模型、本体引导抽取和五阶段精炼流水线，将实时文档流转换为符合形式化本体的验证知识图谱，将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无错误合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时存在一致性问题：类型词汇在不同文档间割裂、同一人物出现多种姓名变体、关系重复、同名不同人可能被错误合并。现有方法缺乏系统性的验证与精炼机制。

Method: 系统采用两阶段抽取：从Kafka消费文档元数据，按PDF、电子表格、Office和图像格式分派处理；使用本地托管的Qwen3.5-9B模型（基于本体微调）进行实体和关系抽取。核心组件是本体引导抽取——通过嵌入相似度从图数据库实时检索本体相关切片并注入抽取提示，将目录开销降低94%。抽取结果经过五阶段精炼流水线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、关系二次抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法、以及具有不可覆盖冲突守卫的嵌入消解子系统。

Result: 在情报语料上的评估表明：搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，无假合并；修正了七类静默质量缺陷，包括截断源文本单字符的bug以及携带标题前缀实体的系统性重复问题。

Conclusion: 该生产系统的核心贡献在于通过本体引导抽取和纯算法层面的多阶段精炼，实现了高准确率的知识图谱构建，既减少了模型推理开销，又通过不可覆盖的冲突守卫确保了消解的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [186] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARE框架，通过CKA对相邻Transformer层间的token隐状态Gram矩阵进行对齐，在单个CoT步骤粒度上量化推理能量，并基于潜在语义状态建模推理轨迹的语义进展。实验表明推理能量在步骤类型间高度非均匀，存在轨迹级指标无法捕捉的相位式转变；错误轨迹在关键推理节点呈现系统性更低能量；SARE特征在多数设置下匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法要么依赖输出层信号，要么将处理深度压缩为单一轨迹级标量，无法揭示思维链推理过程中计算资源在单个步骤间的分配情况，步骤级努力程度仍然是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE)：通过Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)计算相邻Transformer层间token隐状态Gram矩阵的相似度来量化每个CoT步骤的能量；将CoT轨迹建模为潜在语义状态之间的转移以上下文化能量在语义进展中的角色；不需要特征向量对齐或聚类对应。

Result: 在6个推理基准和3个开源LLM上的实验发现：(1)推理能量在各步骤类型间高度非均匀，存在轨迹级指标无法捕捉的相位式转变；(2)错误轨迹在关键推理连接点呈现系统性更低的能量；(3)基于SARE的特征在大多数设置下匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线，表明内部几何动态编码了超越表层信号的预测信息。

Conclusion: 内部几何动态（步骤级推理能量）蕴含超越输出级信号的可预测信息，SARE提供了在细粒度层面理解思维链推理计算分配的可行框架，在推理置信度估计等任务上具有实用价值。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [187] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从Perspective视角探讨了LLM在自主分诊（autonomous triage）场景下的安全缺陷。作者指出，尽管LLM已能通过医学执照考试并在某些情况下媲美医生的诊断推理能力，但将其用于自主分诊的循证安全证据尚不存在。核心问题不在于医学知识储备，而在于临床评估的保真度：LLM被优化为生成最可能的文本，而非在安全答案是需要"不可遗漏诊断"时做出安全行动。这一差距从根本上说是信息收集不确定性下的序贯决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学领域的快速发展已使其被用于症状评估和临床决策支持，而最重大的应用是自主分诊——即在没有或很少有临床医生参与的情况下对自我呈现的未分化患者进行分流。作者写作本文的动机在于指出，这一关键应用缺乏安全性的循证证据，且现有评估方式的缺陷掩盖了LLM可能产生的严重失败模式。

Method: 本文是一篇Perspective（观点性文章），通过逻辑论证和理论分析阐述LLM在自主分诊场景下的安全问题。作者从三个层面展开分析：（1）指出安全分诊的本质是"不对称成本下的序贯决策"，单次灾难性漏诊压过多次误报，关键信号可能是患者未主动提供而模型未被训练去追问的信息；（2）列举LLM在信息不完整历史下可能缺乏的关键行为：扩展鉴别诊断、寻找遗漏的红旗信号、降低升级阈值、在信息不足时延迟判断、在高伤害诊断未被排除时升级关注；（3）揭示现有评估方式的缺陷——往往使用完整、精心策划、置信度门控的模拟，掩盖了LLM的失败，且助手性行为和正向偏差（轻信、顺从、校准不良）会放大这些风险。

Result: 本文的核心结论是：对于LLM自主分诊任务，安全性的证据尚不存在。其关键的不足不在医学知识，而在于临床评估的保真度——模型被优化用于延续最可能的文本，而非在"安全的答案是不可遗漏的罕见诊断"时采取安全行动。此外，由于现有评估多基于完整、精选的仿真数据且受置信度门控，这些失败模式难以被检测到。

Conclusion: LLM自主分诊的核心缺陷是"不确定条件下的信息收集"问题。安全分诊并非选择最可能的诊断，而是在不对称成本下进行的序贯决策，其中灾难性的漏诊成本远高于误报。LLM在信息不完整的病史中可能无法表现出安全分诊所需的行为，且助手式的正向偏差会进一步放大风险。这提示在LLM自主分诊真正应用于临床之前，需要重新设计评估体系并约束临床分诊逻辑。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [188] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态实体身份绑定、双向记忆精炼和多智能体交叉验证，构建自纠正的实体中心记忆，在长时程多模态推理任务中比最强基线准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法在长时程多模态环境中存在两大问题：一是激进压缩和分段处理导致细粒度实体身份线索丢失；二是过度依赖向量相似度检索，可能返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，引发实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉答案。这促使作者设计一个能保持实体身份一致性、支持时间跨度推理的记忆框架。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心组件：(1) 跨模态绑定（cross-modal binding），在长时间范围内锚定实体身份；(2) 双向记忆精炼（bidirectional memory refinement），传播延迟的身份证据，回溯性地统一历史记录并改善未来推理；(3) 多智能体交叉验证（multi-agent cross-verification），在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索证据，当证据缺失时选择弃答而非给出无支撑的答案。

Result: 大量实验表明，ViSAGE在多个基准上持续优于最强基线，准确率平均提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过实体中心的自纠正记忆设计，有效解决了长时程多模态推理中的实体身份混淆问题，验证了双向精炼和弃答机制对减少错误传播和幻觉答案的显著价值。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [189] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体规划问题中满足时空逻辑STL-GO约束的路径规划，提出了两种基于混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码方法，并提供了统一接口，在多元人机搜索救援基准上评估了二者的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题中同时存在时空约束（何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互），这些约束可通过时空逻辑形式化。STL-GO是支持多智能体及其拓扑推理的近期形式化框架，但在该框架下规划满足约束的多智能体路径仍具挑战，因为需要编码多个潜在时变的图结构。

Method: 提出两种编码方案：基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码和基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码，均具有可靠性保证（soundness guarantees）。同时设计了统一接口，用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范，使得两种方法可无缝使用并便于直接对比。

Result: 在多元人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估，针对团队规模和图的复杂度进行了消融实验，展示了所提出编码在动态多图交互场景下的表达能力。

Conclusion: 两种编码方法（MIP和SMT）都能有效解决STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划问题，统一接口便于两种方法的比较和使用，实验验证了编码在动态多图交互下的可扩展性和表达能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [190] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了LSR-Synth基准是否能够区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的传统算子搜索。在当前的固定词表、搜索预算和打分协议下，固定词表已覆盖大多数任务，语言模型生成的候选很少扩展可解实例集合；其边际贡献仅在词表覆盖被选择性破坏时才显著，表明多数当前任务虽能评估表达式拟合与重组，但不足以单独识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准多由公开域已知方程构成，难以判断模型是真正从数据中发现规律还是从训练语料中回忆答案。LSR-Synth通过引入新合成项缓解了这一问题，但一个更窄的测量问题被提出：这些任务能否进一步区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索？

Method: 作者构建了一个使用固定词表且具有公开来源记录的无语义基线，通过语义盲化、词表弱化和匹配算子族剔除等手段，评估候选覆盖的作用。在严格的分布外评估下对所有方法进行了测试。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和打分协议下，固定词表已覆盖大多数任务；语言模型生成的候选很少扩展可解实例集合，仅在词表覆盖被选择性破坏时其边际贡献才变得显著。严格的分布外评估降低了所有方法的绝对成功率，但未改变上述关系。

Conclusion: 这些发现既没有否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的防护措施，也不意味着语言模型先验普遍无用。更有限的结论是：大多数当前任务适合评估未见表达式的拟合与重组，但不足以单独识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [191] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判性地验证了四个流行智能体安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）作为"安全测量工具"的效度。研究发现：二元轨迹判定基准存在"总是正面"策略的评分漏洞（R-Judge上F1可达0.690，超过21个模型中5个真正区分的模型）；不同基准对同一批模型排名不一致，且这种分歧源于小样本伪影（n=7时相关性为-0.64，n=18时降至+0.02）；能力与"任务成功"正相关但与"错位安全"负相关；AgentHarm与越狱安全性呈强关联，但这更多是收敛效度而非通用安全性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同智能体安全基准测量的是不同行为，但其得分却被当作可互换的"智能体安全性"指标引用。作者旨在验证这些基准作为测量工具的有效性，揭示评分方法、样本规模和结果选择对安全结论的深远影响。

Method: 作者将四个安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）视为待验证的测量工具，在官方实现和作者提供的评分器下对多达22个模型进行测试，并统一协议测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力复合指标。分析涵盖了评分度量选择的影响、基准间排序一致性、小样本相关性的伪影效应，以及多种样本面板（n=7、n=18、n=21、n=20、n=41）下的留一组织验证和聚类bootstrap分析。

Result: 核心发现包括：（1）在F1评分下，"总是正面"策略在R-Judge上可达F1=0.690，超越5个真正有判别力的模型；（2）三个宽覆盖基准对相同18个模型的排序互不一致，分歧源于小样本伪影（n=7时ρ=-0.64，n=18时ρ=+0.02）；（3）能力预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但与错位安全性负相关（ρ=-0.44，n=21，成对面板Δ=-1.00，p<0.001），在扩大的41模型面板中该负相关减弱至-0.16，越狱相关性增至+0.34；（4）AgentHarm与三模板越狱安全性有强关联（控制能力后ρ=+0.72），但这只是收敛效度而非通用安全性的证据。

Conclusion: 作者主张任何安全声明都必须至少指明基准、度量、目标行为和模型面板，因为不同选择会产生截然不同甚至相反的结论。安全基准间的分歧并非随机噪声，而是系统性源于评分度量缺陷、小样本伪影和目标行为定义差异，需要更严格的测量效度验证框架。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [192] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个面向开放世界科学工作流的本体感知自进化智能体。它通过演化记忆（技能、经验、本体化工具图）从对比轨迹中蒸馏通用知识，在推理阶段利用LinUCB带状门动态平衡探索与利用，实现新工具的自动获取和本体在线补全。论文还构建了包含900个任务的OpenSciToolBench基准，实验证明其达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在科学研究中依赖预定义工具空间和静态语义，无法适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的问题。需要一种能够自我进化的智能体来应对这种动态环境。

Method: 1) 演化记忆机制：由技能记忆、经验记忆和本体化工具图组成，从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识；2) 推理阶段的主动请求机制与LinUCB-based bandit gate动态平衡探索与利用；3) 新工具获取后在线补全其科学本体，无缝集成到已有工具图中；4) 构建OpenSciToolBench基准，包含900个跨四个难度级别的真实任务。

Result: 在OpenSciToolBench基准上，SciToolAgent-Evo在四个难度级别的900个任务中均取得最先进性能（SOTA），验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SciToolAgent-Evo通过本体感知的自进化机制和探索-利用平衡策略，有效解决了开放世界科学工具获取问题，为LLM智能体在动态科学工作流中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [193] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyDx，一个基于MIMIC-IV中154,834个急诊科就诊记录的大规模开放结束早期诊断基准。该基准将证据限制在入院时间t0可用的记录中，以急诊就诊期间而非出院时的诊断作为监督标签，并通过LLM审核员验证自由文本标签的证据支持程度。评估发现所有系统（包括前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域后训练模型）在可靠综合入院证据方面均表现不佳，零样本模型主要靠抽取信息，仅恢复3-31%需要推理的诊断，后训练将推理相关召回率提升至56%，但仍存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床入院时的诊断必须基于有限且不完整的证据快速做出，但现有诊断预测基准不适合此场景：它们限制在封闭代码集、排除自由文本笔记、且以包含完整住院过程的出院诊断作为监督。这导致现有基准无法真实反映早期诊断的挑战。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV构建EarlyDx基准，包含154,834个急诊科就诊，将每个就诊限制在入院时间t0可用的记录，以急诊科就诊期间记录的诊断而非出院诊断为监督标签。使用LLM审核员将每个自由文本标签验证为完全支持、部分支持或不支持，主要评估仅对完全支持的标签打分。采用语义LLM-as-judge协议进行评估。

Result: 零样本模型主要依靠信息抽取而非推理，仅恢复3-31%需要推理推断的诊断；后训练可将推理依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍存在显著差距；在时间紧迫条件下，没有系统能达到临床医生在敏感性和精确性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 现有评估的所有系统（前沿通用、医学专用或领域后训练）均未能可靠地综合入院时间的证据进行早期诊断，当前方法在推理能力上仍有明显不足，此基准将开源完整构建与评估流程。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [194] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以交互为中心的智能体故障分类法，将41种故障模式定位到智能体系统中两个组件之间的交互边，并指明故障归属侧（模型侧、harness侧、环境/评测器侧），从而解决"修复分配问题"（repair-assignment problem），使故障分类具有可操作性且跨架构通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估将失败归因于系统级结果，无法追溯故障源头，导致"修复分配问题"：同一个可见失败可能源于模型后训练、harness工程、环境重设计或基准修复等不同原因。现有故障分类法大多针对特定基准，缺乏共享结构，难以指导干预改进。

Method: 提出交互中心分类法（interaction-centric taxonomy）：将41种故障模式映射到两个组件之间的有向边，并为每条边分配"故障侧"（fault side）以指明修复责任归属。模型侧失败指向后训练目标，harness侧失败指向脚手架与工具集成修复，环境/评测器失败指向评估条件重设计。该分类法跨智能体架构通用，并通过公共基准、系统卡片、公开报告和日志轨迹中的工作示例进行落地。采用四个前沿模型作为独立推理裁判来评估分类的可复现性。

Result: 在四个前沿模型中，最强裁判与人类类别标签之间达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明该分类框架捕获的是共享结构而非标注者特定偏好，具有较好的可复现性。

Conclusion: 该交互中心分类法提供了一种可操作的、跨架构的故障定位与修复分配框架，弥补了现有评估中系统级归因与干预指导之间的断层，适用于从编码助手到长时程个人助手及多智能体系统等多种智能体架构。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [195] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANCHOR，一种受控合成审计框架，用于分别测量AI伴侣在长时间会话中的人格扮演（persona enactment）和轨迹回忆（trajectory recall），发现所有被测模型和配置都无法可靠维持这两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI伴侣越来越多地介导重复社交互动，用户依赖稳定的角色和共享历史进行交流。然而，局部可接受的回复并不能保证角色一致性和历史连续性得以维持。现有研究缺乏能够分离测量人格崩溃和行为漂移的系统化方法。

Method: ANCHOR是一个受控合成审计框架，包含：（1）Identity Probe：密封的102项问卷配合回合级判断；（2）Trajectory Probe：来自35个对话库的110个校准反事实问题。研究涵盖2,008段对话、27个角色、9种交互调度、3种生成的记忆设置和4个评估模型。

Result: 所有被测模型和配置均无法可靠地保持两个维度：轨迹准确率仅平均44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选项随机水平，没有上下文条件或记忆设置能一致解决这些问题。问卷保留因模型和人格面而异，与回合级行为不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠支持AI伴侣的长时程连续性，审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署上下文，而不能将它们合并为单一的信任或稳定性评分。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [196] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了AI对齐问题的数学模型，分析在理想化对齐训练下，代理的价值函数满足代理条件但可能仍是η-灾难性的，从而在部署时导致人类价值期望被压低。研究发现过度优化是危险根源，并主张采用限制优化压力的设计方案（如quantilizers）而非仅依赖部署前训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担越来越多责任，确保其与人类价值观对齐至关重要。AI安全领域普遍担心人类价值是"脆弱的"——即对不完善的人类价值代理函数过度优化可能导致灾难性后果。本文旨在用形式化模型刻画这一对齐问题，并揭示过度优化的具体危险条件。

Method: 本文构建了一个形式化对齐模型：代理在部署前经历理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数满足某个代理条件，然后才开始优化世界。通过分析人类价值函数的结构特征以及不同代理条件的准确性，推导出在何种条件下会部署一个具有η-灾难性价值函数（即在优化力趋于无穷时，将人类价值期望压缩到η以下的保证）的代理。

Result: 主要结果为：在特定的代理条件准确度和人类价值函数结构条件下，即使代理通过了理想化的对齐训练，其价值函数仍可能被证明是η-灾难性的，从而导致部署后产生灾难性后果。这些结果凸显了过度优化的危险性。

Conclusion: 文章结论认为，仅依赖部署前训练不足以保证AI安全，需要设计能够限制优化压力的AI架构（如quantilizers），从根本上缓解过度优化带来的风险。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [197] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于贝叶斯实验设计（BED）的框架，用于优化认知规划实验的环境设计，从而更有效地推断认知参数。研究通过Mouselab-MDP范式验证，表明摊销BED能匹配精确BED的环境排序但计算成本大幅降低，且不存在对所有认知推断目标都最优的单一环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模旨在从行为中推断潜在的认知机制，但现有方法通常将实验环境视为固定的，忽略了"哪些认知实验环境对参数推断最有效"这一关键问题，这限制了贝叶斯逆规划框架的性能。

Method: 将认知规划实验设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，以实验环境作为设计变量；建立了精确的蒙特卡洛BED基准，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架用于高效的参数后验推断和设计评估。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上的实验表明，摊销BED与精确蒙特卡洛BED在环境排序上高度一致，但计算成本显著降低。此外发现没有单一环境在所有认知推断目标上统一最优，揭示了预期信息增益、后验可恢复性和信息效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计信息丰富的认知实验以进行贝叶斯参数推断提供了原则性框架，有助于提升认知模型参数推断的效率和效果。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [198] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号快慢思考框架（NeSyFS），用于解决大型语言模型代理在部分可观测环境中的决策问题。该框架使用知识图谱表示信念状态，通过快思考模块进行反应式行动、慢思考模块基于扭序序列蒙特卡洛算法进行不确定性感知规划，并引入反思模块缓解目标错位问题。在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个基准上取得了显著优于先前方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地部署为自主代理（如自我反思、检索增强生成、科学发现等），这些场景中代理只能基于有限观测而非完整环境状态行动，导致部分可观测性。这带来了三个关键挑战：信念状态推断、任务目标错位、不确定性下的规划。先前的方案通常基于完整或摘要化的动作-观测历史来决策，但其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM代理的决策。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架，包含三个模块：（1）知识图谱（KG）表示信念状态，为每个模块提供三元组上下文；（2）快思考模块执行反应式行动，模拟人类快速直觉决策；（3）慢思考模块通过遵循扭序序列蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定性感知规划，模拟人类深思熟虑；（4）反思模块对快思考行动进行反思以缓解任务目标错位，并在反应式行动反复失败时切换到慢思考模块。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，NeSyFS框架相比先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS通过结合神经符号方法模拟人类快慢双系统思维，以统一方式应对部分可观测性带来的多项挑战，为LLM代理在部分可观测环境中的可靠决策提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [199] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MerchantBench，一个基于98,843条真实电商商品记录、365天订单级仿真、配备26个工具，用于评估大语言模型智能体长程一致性（Long-Term Coherence）能力的基准。实验显示最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产27.3%，揭示两者间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体评估基准多聚焦于有明确成功标准的有限任务，而真实部署需要智能体在长时间跨度内保持目的性行为并基于累积证据适应决策（即长程一致性）。缺乏一个具有持久环境、可量化累积效果下的评估框架来检验此能力。

Method: 在卖方电商领域构建了365天的订单级仿真环境MerchantBench，包含98,843条真实商品记录、26个交互工具，覆盖商品采购、上架与定价控制、现金流管理以及混合延迟反馈适应四个维度。环境将即时可见的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果耦合，要求智能体追踪单个订单生命周期并重新审视先前决策。在两种智能体框架下评估了8个LLM，共48轮运行。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使是最新的大语言模型与人类参与者之间仍存在显著能力差距，最佳LLM配置所获得的平均最终净资产仅达到人类参与者的27.3%。

Conclusion: MerchantBench成功揭示了当前LLM智能体在长程一致性能力上的不足，验证了该基准作为评估智能体在持久、多反馈延迟环境中的目的性行为维持能力的有效工具，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [200] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco框架，通过构建可过程验证的科学发现环境（SciThèque）、基于DAG的轨迹合成和DiscoPO训练策略，使14B规模的语言模型智能体在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长期科学分析仍受限于缺乏基于真实科学数据的过程监督环境，导致智能体在交互过程中无法获得细粒度的中间反馈。

Method: 1) SciThèque：将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译为任务环境，支持交互过程中的分析进度检查；2) DAG-grounded轨迹合成：利用该环境构建经验证器筛选的多轮演示；3) DiscoPO：以环境为训练信号来源，为产生可验证分析证据的动作分配turn级信任度。
