### [300] [Convergence and Regret of the Policy Gradient for Multi-Armed Bandits in Diffusion Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29593)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散环境下多臂老虎机问题的策略梯度更新算法，在连续时间强化学习框架下，证明了对数线性参数化的随机策略在任意常数学习率下几乎必然收敛到最优臂，并在学习率低于某一时不变阈值时给出O(log T)阶的非渐近遗憾上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过构造新颖的Lyapunov函数改进Lattimore（2026a）对同一SDE的分析，展示使用SDE工具分析策略梯度的透明性，并推广至离散时间策略梯度算法。

Method: 基于Wang et al.（2020）和Jia & Zhou（2022b）提出的连续时间强化学习框架，采用对数线性（logit）参数化随机策略，利用构建的Lyapunov函数对策略梯度更新进行收敛性分析，推导常数学习率下的几乎必然收敛性和非渐近遗憾上界。

Result: 在任意常数学习率下算法几乎必然收敛到最优臂；当常数学习率低于时不变阈值时，得到O(log T)阶的非渐近遗憾上界。

Conclusion: 该Lyapunov函数方法不仅适用于连续时间SDE框架下的策略梯度分析，同样可推广用于离散时间策略梯度算法的分析，方法具有普适性和透明性。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [301] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于大语言模型参数高效微调的优化路径组织框架，通过任务分组和任务排序实现自动多策略PEFT架构。该框架在固定参数预算下，将兼容的优化路径组织为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA），使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保持兼容任务间的正向迁移。在TRACE基准上，所提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到最佳性能44.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PEFT（如LoRA）通常使用单一共享低秩适配器来适配多样的任务序列，但这种共享优化空间在处理异构任务时会产生干扰，导致较差的迁移能力和严重的灾难性遗忘。现有方法主要通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器来提升适配器表达能力，但仍依赖于共享的优化路径，未能从根本上解决异构任务间的优化干扰问题。

Method: 提出一种自动多策略PEFT架构，作为优化路径组织框架。核心方法包括：(1) 在固定参数预算下，通过任务分组和任务排序自动组织优化兼容的适配路径；(2) 将组织好的优化路径实现为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA）；(3) 异构任务在解耦的适配空间中独立优化，同时兼容任务间保持正向迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上的实验表明，从传统单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT的性能持续提升，所提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下取得最佳性能44.78。

Conclusion: 优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量对于异构参数高效微调更为有效，这一发现为PEFT领域提供了新的设计视角。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [302] [A Human-Centered Validation of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29614)
*Christian Oliva,Luis F. Lago-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPC分数（Explainability-Performance Coefficient的扩展），一个模型无关的XAI评估指标，通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持来量化解释质量，并在表格、文本和图像三种模态上验证了其有效性，同时与人类标注对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在高风险领域的广泛应用对可信赖的可解释人工智能提出了迫切需求，但客观评估解释保真度以及使XAI指标与以人为中心的理解对齐仍是关键开放挑战。

Method: 提出EPC分数的模型无关评估指标，通过平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持来量化解释质量；在表格、文本和图像三种模态上进行实证验证，并与独立的人工解释进行对比验证。

Result: EPC分数能有效揭示网络激活、数据维度与解释器性能之间的操作依赖关系；更高的EPC分数与人类词汇情感判断和空间视觉标注强烈对齐。

Conclusion: EPC分数作为一种模型无关的XAI评估指标，不仅在技术上能够有效评估解释质量，而且与人类理解高度一致，为XAI评估提供了更值得信赖的量化标准。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning models in high-risk domains has intensified the need for trustworthy Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). However, objectively evaluating explanation fidelity and aligning XAI metrics with human-centered understanding remain critical open challenges. In this work, we propose a model-agnostic metric, the EPC score, which is an extension of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient (EPC), that quantifies explanation quality by explicitly balancing the trade-off between feature selection sparsity and preserved model performance. Through an empirical validation across tabular, text, and image modalities, we show that the EPC score effectively uncovers operational dependencies among network activations, data dimensionality, and explainer performance. Furthermore, we validate the EPC score against independent human-based explanations, proving that higher EPC scores strongly align with human lexical sentiment judgments and spatial visual annotations.

</details>


### [303] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OVI算法，一种交互式在线模仿学习算法，证明了专家交互可以放宽学习者的表征需求——只需建模专家价值函数而非专家策略本身，并辅以必要的阴性结果验证交互的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆（BC）在无法完美表征专家策略时（如蒸馏场景）会遭受复合误差和性能瓶颈。尽管交互式查询和价值函数估计被广泛认为能提升性能，但其本质原因及两者间的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 提出OVI（Online Value-based Imitation）算法：一种交互式在策略模仿学习算法，结合专家交互查询与价值函数估计，利用线性最大化预言机实现计算效率，仅需学习者能表征专家价值函数即可达到统计效率。

Result: OVI在统计效率上优于离线策略方法（BC）、交互式策略方法（DAgger）和离线基于价值的方法，尤其是当学习者网络表达能力远低于专家时获益最大。同时，阴性结果显示仅依赖专家价值可实现性时，任何离线IL算法都必须面临专家策略类的复杂度。

Conclusion: 专家交互的本质作用在于放宽了学习者的表征需求：从需要精确复制专家策略分布收敛到只需实现专家价值函数，且这种交互是必要的——没有交互，离线算法在缺乏更强假设时无法规避专家策略类的复杂度。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [304] [CENDRe: Concept Extraction with Natural Domain Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29621)
*Antonia Holzapfel,Andres Felipe Posada Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CENDRe，一种面向CNN时间序列分类的概念提取方法，通过两阶段聚类自动确定概念数量，利用梯度定位在时域和频域生成概念掩码，并计算每个概念对各类别的相关性得分，解决了现有方法忽略频域特征、预定义概念数量和定位错位的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN广泛用于时间序列分类，但在关键领域的部署需要理解驱动预测的时域和频谱模式。现有时间序列概念提取方法存在三个局限：仅操作时域而忽略频率特征、预定义概念数量、定位与模型实际使用的区域不对齐。

Method: CENDRe首先通过两阶段聚类发现概念，每个时间步的潜在表征通过轮廓系数引导的聚合自动选择概念数量。然后通过存在分数的梯度（对比潜在表征与其原型）对每个概念进行定位，生成聚焦于驱动概念区域的掩码。这些梯度通过可微的可逆输入映射（如傅里叶变换）传播，得到同一概念在频域的定位。最后每个概念获得一个相关性得分，量化其对每个类别贡献。

Result: 在合成基准上，CENDRe的表征正确性与最先进的概念提取方法相当，重要性正确性显著更高。在真实轴承故障数据上，CENDRe成功提取了驱动模型预测的频带，而这些频带位于故障诊断中常被检查的区域，提供了时域概念提取方法无法提供的模型评估证据。

Conclusion: CENDRe通过同时提供时域和频域的概念定位以及自动确定概念数量，显著提升了时间序列概念提取的能力，特别是在对频域特征关键的真实应用场景中（如轴承故障诊断），为模型可解释性提供了更全面的证据。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for time-series classification, but their deployment in critical domains requires understanding the temporal and spectral patterns that drive their predictions. Concept extraction (CE) methods identify such patterns by analyzing representations within the models' latent space. However, existing time-series CE methods have three limitations: they operate only in the time domain and overlook frequency features, predefine the number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the regions the model uses. We address these limitations by proposing CENDRe, a concept extraction method for CNNs. It first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, where silhouette-guided aggregation selects the number of concepts automatically. Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [305] [Freeze, Then Select: Structured Field Adapters and Stability-Validated Weak Selection for PDE Discovery from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29665)
*Juncheng Zhong,Chenghuang Shen,Jianfeng Liu,Zhengdong Xiao,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Wenjun Xu,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"先冻结再选择"的神经偏微分方程(PDE)发现方法，通过结构化场适配器与稳定性验证弱选择(SVWS)解耦方程选择与神经优化，在稀疏观测下实现了最高的精确支持恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏观测中做PDE发现需要同时重建连续场和选择正确的微分项，但耦合神经PDE发现中的优化路径呈现三种行为：精确支持可持续到训练结束、仅短暂出现或完全无法涌现。这种优化不确定性导致方程选择与神经优化深度耦合，需要解耦二者以提高可解释性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出freeze-then-select方法：(1) 结构化场适配器在无需PDE残差的情况下从观测数据训练，将场分解为学习的空间特征与由三次样条表示的时序系数；(2) 冻结场后，稳定性验证弱选择(SVWS)在独立弱形式系统中识别递归项、重拟合候选支持，并在留出弱形式系统上选择最终方程；(3) 除固定库外，还将该原则应用于遗传编程生成的表达式，以恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Result: 在全部六个稀疏MDBench基准上，该方法达到了最高的精确支持恢复率，在具有挑战性的Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学上较经典与神经基线有最明显的优势；同时能从稀疏、含噪观测中恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Conclusion: 通过将场重建与方程选择解耦、并利用跨独立弱形式系统的项选择一致性，可以有效提升稀疏观测下神经PDE发现的精确支持恢复能力，且该原则可推广到遗传编程生成的表达式空间。

Abstract: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires reconstructing a continuous field and selecting the correct differential terms. Our analysis of optimization paths in coupled neural PDE discovery reveals three behaviors: the exact support can persist to the end of training, appear only transiently, or fail to emerge. To decouple equation selection from neural optimization, we develop a freeze-then-select method combining a structured field adapter with Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS). Trained from observations without a PDE residual, the adapter factorizes the field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field, SVWS identifies recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refits candidate supports, and selects the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. Beyond fixed libraries, we apply the same principle to expressions generated by genetic programming and recover the power-law form of an unknown nonlinear diffusion function from sparse, noisy observations. Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, our method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with its clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [But What Behavior?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28898)
*Robert C. Froemke*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视"自然行为"的概念，认为对于行为泛化物种（如啮齿类、人类等），不存在所谓的"天然栖息地"或"自然行为"，主张以客观结果指标替代对自然性的追求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"自然行为"研究中隐含的前提假设，指出在行为泛化物种中"自然性"是无法定义的概念，需要更科学的方法框架。

Method: 通过理论论证和概念批判的方式，分析"自然行为"定义的局限性，提出以可量化的客观结果指标（如存活与繁殖所需的特定行为序列和适应机制）为研究核心。

Result: 提出研究重点应从"行为是否自然"转向"哪些行为序列和适应机制使动物能在多样化的动态或危险环境中生存与繁殖"。

Conclusion: 自然行为研究应放弃对自然性的定义执念，转而关注跨环境可量化的行为结果指标，以更科学地理解行为适应机制。

Abstract: What is a natural behavior? I argue that the study of natural behaviors is often the study of the spontaneous behaviors of animals placed in quantifiably different environments. For behavioral generalists such as rodents, humans, and many other species, there may be no such definable construct as a native habitat or natural behavior, due to their successful abilities and needs to rapidly adapt to a wide range of different ecosystems. Instead of prioritizing naturalness, it may be more essential to determine objective outcome measures related to specific behaviors; i.e., which sequences of behaviors and adaptive mechanisms allow animals to survive and reproduce, across a range of dynamic or hazardous physical and social environments.

</details>


### [307] [Critical Flicker Fusion Frequency As A Falsifiable Boundary Between Plastic And Non-Plastic Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29068)
*Natalia D. Rydzenska,Pawel J. Winklewski,Michal W. Blaszczyk-Niezgoda,Anna B. Marcinkowska*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个层级框架，将临界闪烁融合频率（CFFF）界定为可塑性神经系统的边界标志，综述表明CFFF源于跨处理层次的收敛约束，尽管其对非特异性认知训练不敏感，却可通过感知学习范式调节，并可作为特质标记用于连接工作记忆精度与元认知准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间能力可通过训练获得高度可塑性，而通过相同皮层通路处理的视觉时间处理能力（如CFFF）表现出异常的个体内稳定性，这一鲜明对比缺乏系统性解释框架，需要阐明CFFF稳定性的神经机制与可塑性边界。

Method: 提出层级框架，将CFFF标记为可塑与不可塑神经系统的边界，并通过明确的证伪标准进行操作化；系统考察CFFF稳定性可能源于的外周约束、丘脑皮层动力学、皮层时间滤波或多层级整合架构，最终判定其来自跨处理层次的收敛约束；辅以三条原则（感知时钟的稳定参考系、代谢约束、速度-准确性权衡）巩固该框架。

Result: 发现CFFF稳定性源于跨处理层次的收敛约束；初级视觉皮层中空间属性表现出稳健的感知学习而时间处理受限，时间关键期早于空间关键期关闭；CFFF对非特异性认知训练无响应，但可通过涉及大细胞-背侧通路的感知学习范式加以调节，这一定义了可塑性边界而非与之矛盾。

Conclusion: CFFF可作为特质标记，其与工作记忆精度、元认知准确性及容量限制的关联仍属可测试假设而非已确立结论；理解CFFF作为可塑性边界有助于阐明刚性感官约束下灵活认知系统的运作机制。

Abstract: Experience-dependent neural plasticity is fundamental to adaptive behaviour, yet certain perceptual abilities resist modification despite extensive training. Critical flicker fusion frequency (CFFF), the threshold at which flickering light appears continuous, is a foundational constraint in visual temporal processing that shows exceptional within-individual stability in adults, contrasting sharply with the highly plastic spatial abilities processed through the same cortical pathways. This review proposes a hierarchical framework in which CFFF marks a boundary between plastic and non-plastic neural systems, operationalised via explicit falsification criteria. We examine whether CFFF stability reflects peripheral constraints, thalamocortical dynamics, cortical temporal filtering, or an integrated multi-level architecture, concluding it emerges from convergent constraints across processing levels. Within primary visual cortex, spatial properties show robust perceptual learning while temporal processing remains constrained, with temporal critical periods closing earlier than spatial ones. Critically, CFFF is unresponsive to non-specific cognitive training yet modifiable by perceptual-learning paradigms engaging the magnocellular-dorsal stream, a pattern defining rather than contradicting the plasticity boundary. Three principles reinforce this stability: a perceptual clock requires a stable reference frame for temporal binding; metabolic constraints render faster processing energetically prohibitive; and speed-accuracy trade-offs suggest selection optimised integration windows. We argue CFFF may serve as a trait marker whose links to working memory precision, metacognitive accuracy, and capacity limits remain testable hypotheses, not established findings. Understanding CFFF as a plasticity boundary illuminates how flexible cognitive systems operate within rigid sensory constraints.

</details>


### [308] [metasignal: A Python Package for Comprehensive Metacognitive Analysis and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29093)
*Saurabh Ranjan,Mukesh Makwana,Konstantina Sokratous,Brian Odegaard*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: metasignal是一个开源的Python包，用于信号检测理论和元认知测量，实现了Rahnev (2025)评估的17种元认知测量指标，提供统一的单函数计算接口，支持二元区分任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元认知研究领域存在多种测量指标，缺乏统一的工具平台来整合这些指标，阻碍了元认知研究的广泛采用和比较。

Method: 开发一个Python包，实现17种元认知测量指标（包括meta-d'家族、非参数Type-2测量及其SDT归一化形式、模型基础测量），从试验级数据（刺激、反应、置信度）通过单一函数计算完整指标集，并提供命令行接口、分组汇总、bootstrap置信区间、置换检验、层级贝叶斯模型和信息论测量。

Result: metasignal支持二元（两选一）区分任务，统一了17种元认知测量指标在单一平台上，为元认知研究和决策研究提供了可用的开源工具。

Conclusion: metasignal通过统一多种元认知测量指标于单一开源平台，有望促进更广泛的元认知研究及其在决策研究中的推广应用。

Abstract: Metasignal is an open-source Python package for signal detection theory (SDT) and metacognitive measurement. It implements the 17 metacognitive measures evaluated by Rahnev (2025), together with the reference variables d' (perceptual sensitivity), response criterion c (response bias), and mean confidence. The 17 measures comprise three meta-d' family estimates, meta-d', M-ratio, and M-difference; four nonparametric Type-2 measures, the Type-2 area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (AUC2), Gamma, Phi, and delta confidence, together with their eight SDT-normalized ratio and difference forms; and two model-based measures, meta-noise and meta-uncertainty. A single function computes the complete set from trial-level stimulus, response, and confidence arrays. `metasignal` currently supports binary (two-alternative) discrimination tasks, in which each trial's stimulus and response are coded with exactly two categories. The package also provides a command-line interface, group summaries, bootstrap confidence intervals, permutation tests, optional hierarchical Bayesian models, and information-theoretic measures. `metasignal` unifies these measures in a single platform to encourage broader metacognition research and adoption in decision-making studies.

</details>


### [309] [Resource depletion accelerates rate learning but not composition learning in patch foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29476)
*Zachary P. Kilpatrick,Ahmed El Hady*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个规范性的贝叶斯框架，研究智能体在开发斑块环境的同时学习其结构的层级推断问题。资源耗竭在不同层级上以不同方式塑造学习：斑块内加速速率学习，斑块间组成学习缓慢且不受耗竭影响。奖励最大化与信息寻求策略因此出现分歧，当斑块间有补给时策略会坍缩到稳定轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数先前的觅食模型假设动物已知其环境中资源的分布，但这一结构实际上必须在探索过程中被学习。觅食因此可被视为一个层级推断问题，理解这一层级结构如何被塑造以及耗竭如何影响学习是核心动机。

Method: 作者构建了规范性的贝叶斯账户，模拟智能体在学习斑块环境的同时进行开发。分析斑块内（速率学习由连续遭遇的下降速率钉定）与斑块间（组成学习由采样斑块数量决定）不同层次的学习动态，并比较奖励最大化与信息寻求策略的差异。

Result: 耗竭加速斑块内速率学习但对于已知速率的组成学习无影响；解决组成不确定性的觅食者会欠收富裕斑块。固定斑块补给下，奖励最大化策略收敛到高产出斑块的稳定轨道。最优离弃规则在猎物计数与时间计时之间切换，当丰富度变异超过约四分之一时发生转换。环境学习比仅从奖励中学到规则带来更高摄入。

Conclusion: 觅食是一个多层次的贝叶斯推断问题，耗竭对各层学习的影响不同，这导致信息寻求与奖励最大化策略的结构性分歧。只要模型对耗竭的假设留有余地，学习环境本身能显著提升摄入效率。

Abstract: Foraging is a universal animal behavior that has increasingly attracted the interest of both experimentalists and theorists. Most prior models assume an animal knows the distribution of resources in its environment, but this structure must be learned as the animal explores its environment. Foraging can thus be regarded as a hierarchical inference problem. We develop a normative Bayesian account of an agent learning a patchy environment while exploiting it, and show that resource depletion shapes the levels of that hierarchy differently. Within a patch, depletion accelerates rate learning, since successive encounters occur at falling rates whose spacing pins down the initial rate. Across patches, composition learning, inferring the fraction of patches that are high yield, is slow, set by the number of patches sampled rather than the time spent in each, and unaffected by depletion once the rates are known. Reward-maximizing and information-seeking strategies therefore diverge, a forager resolving the composition underharvesting rich patches because learning it requires departures, most sharply early in exposure. When a fixed set of patches replenishes between visits, the reward-maximizing policy collapses onto a stable orbit over the high-yield patches, and the replenishment rate sets whether the forager maps the whole environment or locks onto a rich subset. Which departure rule maximizes intake is itself set by how variable the patches are, switching from counting prey to timing the gaps between them once richness varies by more than about a quarter. Learning the environment thus buys significant intake over learning a rule from reward alone, as long as its assumptions about depletion leave room for the truth.

</details>


### [310] [Quantifying the cost of network computations to unpack structure-function relationships in the brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29537)
*Suman S. Kulkarni,Jason Z. Kim,Panagiotis Fotiadis,Fabio Pasqualetti,Dani S. Bassett*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的定量框架，通过网络结构对计算成本的分布（称为"计算可负担性景观"）来衡量神经网络支持特定计算的能力，并在昆虫导航回路、人类大脑网络和训练后的循环神经网络中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑通过底层网络上的协调活动模式支持计算，这些网络的组织结构被认为支持其功能，但目前缺乏统一的定量框架来理解网络结构如何塑造网络可以支持的计算。

Method: 将计算定义为目标导向的状态转换，利用控制理论在给定网络上量化每种转换的代价，定义所有可能转换代价的分布为"计算可负担性景观"，并应用于三类网络系统。

Result: 在昆虫方向感回路中，更新朝向是最低代价的计算；在人类大脑中，感觉网络呈现更异质的景观而联合网络呈现更均质的景观；在训练后的循环神经网络中，学习会逐步增加景观的异质性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了一个定量研究神经回路结构与计算之间关系的通用方法，未来可扩展应用于其他生物和物理网络。

Abstract: The brain supports computations through coordinated patterns of activity on an underlying network. These networks---from microscale navigational circuits in insects to macroscale brain areas in humans---are organized in structured ways that are thought to support their function. We seek a unifying quantitative framework to understand how network structure shapes the computations a network can readily support. To do so, we frame computation as a goal-directed transition of activity and quantify its cost on a given network using control theory. We then define the distribution of costs across all possible transitions as a $\textit{computational affordance landscape}$ that encodes which computations a network structure readily supports. We apply this framework to a circuit model for how insects maintain a sense of direction and show that updating orientation is the least costly computation, with predicted inputs consistent with known circuitry. In the human brain, we find that the affordance landscape varies systematically with the functional role of each network. Sensory networks display more heterogeneous landscapes (reflecting their role in specialized information processing), whereas association networks display more homogeneous landscapes (reflecting their role in generalized information processing). In recurrent neural networks trained on cognitive tasks, we show that learning progressively increases landscape heterogeneity, reshaping the distribution of affordable computations. Generally, we establish a quantitative framework for studying relationships between structure and computation in neural circuits, with future applications extending to other biological and physical networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Bridging the Gap Between PHE and FHE: A Performance and Trade-off Analysis of The Somewhat Homomorphic BGN Cryptosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28700)
*Sefik Serengil,Alper Ozpinar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过将BGN同态加密方案集成到lightphe Python框架中，对其进行了系统性的基准测试和比较分析，揭示了BGN在计算性能上低于PHE和FHE（因双线性配对运算成本高），但公钥尺寸极小（3-6 KB，比FHE小五个数量级），且支持单次乘法后的无限次同态聚合，适用于带宽受限的分布式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密实践中面临PHE（轻量但功能有限）与FHE（功能强大但计算开销大）之间的权衡；BGN作为SWHE方案填补了这一空白，支持无限次加法和一次密文乘法，但缺乏易用的软件实现阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 将BGN集成到lightphe Python框架中，在80位、112位和128位安全级别下，对128维向量加密操作进行基准测试，与Paillier、Damgard-Jurik、Okamoto-Uchiyama（PHE）以及TenSEAL的CKKS（FHE）方案进行对比；并从计算速度、通信开销、公钥大小、聚合能力和解密精度等多个维度进行分析。

Result: BGN在计算速度上因双线性配对而慢于PHE和SIMD优化的FHE，但公钥大小仅3-6 KB，比FHE小至多五个数量级；BGN支持单次乘法后的无限同态聚合，可应用于线性回归推理、余弦相似度和欧氏距离等任务；2位小数精度即可匹配明文排序基线。

Conclusion: 通过开源lightphe中的BGN实现，证明BGN可在带宽受限的分布式架构中作为实用的同态加密引擎，在功能与通信开销之间提供了独特的平衡点。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving data analytics, but practitioners often face a trade-off between lightweight Partially Homomorphic Encryption (PHE) and computationally dominant Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE). The Boneh-Goh-Nissim (BGN) cryptosystem bridges this gap as a Somewhat Homomorphic Encryption (SWHE) scheme supporting unlimited additions and one ciphertext multiplication. Despite its algebraic elegance, practical BGN adoption has been hindered by a lack of accessible software implementations. This paper presents a comparative analysis of BGN against PHE and FHE paradigms through its integration into the lightphe Python framework, allowing deployment in just a few lines of code. We benchmark encrypted 128-dimensional vector operations under 80-bit, 112-bit and 128-bit security levels against Paillier, Damgard-Jurik, Okamoto-Uchiyama, and the FHE CKKS scheme via TenSEAL. Results reveal a computation-communication trade-off: BGN is computationally slower due to bilinear pairings compared to PHE and SIMD-optimized FHE, but retains a microscopic public key size of 3-6 KB, up to five orders of magnitude smaller than FHE. Crucially, BGN enables boundless homomorphic aggregation after a single multiplication, supporting complex tasks such as linear regression inference, Cosine Similarity, and Squared Euclidean Distance. Furthermore, an optimized precision of 2 digits suffices to match plaintext ranking baselines, overcoming the target-group discrete logarithm decryption bottleneck. By open-sourcing this pipeline in lightphe, this work establishes BGN as a practical engine for bandwidth-constrained, decentralized architectures.

</details>


### [312] [Costs of Arbitrary Real Matrix Factorizations for Pure-DP Continual Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28703)
*Awnon Bhowmik,Mahmudul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了下三角前缀和矩阵 T_n 的纯ε-差分隐私拉普拉斯矩阵机制下，均方误差与最大坐标均方误差的分解代价均为 Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，从而两种误差均为 Θ(ε^{-2}log^3(n+1))，并解答了 Arkhipov–Kalinin 提出的任意实因子情形的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纯ε-差分隐私下拉普拉斯矩阵机制的最优误差界是基础问题，此前 Arkhipov 和 Kalinin 仅对 {0,1} 系数因子证明了匹配下界，任意实数因子的扩展被列为开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过 p-核范数障碍法：对低秩范围 1≤k≤n/16 建立前缀链的聚合列宽估计 D_k(T_n)≍n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}，代入 Pietsch 与 Hinrichs–Pietsch 的经典逼近空间转化，在临界指数 p=2/3 处达到调和，再用 Hölder 不等式将其转移到两种分解代价；上界由 Fenwick 区间分解构造匹配。

Result: 证明 cfrob(T_n) 与 ctwo(T_n) 均为 Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，无符号、稀疏或方阵限制且内维任意有限；同时确定了 0<p<1 时 p-核范数 nucpow_p(T_n) 的阶：p<2/3 时为 n，p=2/3 时为 n log n，p>2/3 时为 n^{3p/2}。

Conclusion: 在纯ε-DP拉普拉斯矩阵机制类内，优化后的最大与均方误差均为 Θ(ε^{-2}log^3(n+1))；结果仅限于该机制与两种平方误差准则，不覆盖非矩阵持续机制、近似-DP灵敏度或坐标上的期望最大值情形。

Abstract: Let \(T_n\) be the lower-triangular prefix-sum matrix and let \(\cfrob(T_n)\) and \(\ctwo(T_n)\) be the factorization costs that govern mean and maximum per-coordinate squared error of the Laplace matrix mechanism under pure \(\eps\)-differential privacy, for \(\eps>0\). We prove \(\cfrob(T_n),\ctwo(T_n)=Θ\bigl((\log(n+1))^{3/2}\bigr)\) with no sign, sparsity, or squareness restriction and with arbitrary finite inner dimension. Consequently, within the pure-\(\eps\)-DP matrix-mechanism class the optimized maximum and mean squared errors are both \(Θ(\eps^{-2}\log^{3}(n+1))\). Under the factorization contract of Arkhipov and Kalinin (arXiv:2607.08963v1), who prove the matching lower order for factors with entries in \(\{0,1\}\) and state the arbitrary-factor extension as open, the theorem below establishes the order for arbitrary real factors. The lower bound runs through a \(p\)-nuclear obstruction: an aggregate column-width estimate \(D_k(T_n)\asymp n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}\), valid in the low-rank range \(1\leq k\leq n/16\), for the prefix chain, fed into the classical approximation-space conversion of Pietsch and Hinrichs--Pietsch, becomes harmonic at the critical exponent \(p=2/3\), and Hölder's inequality transfers it to both factorization costs. The same computation determines \(\nucpow_p(T_n)\) for each fixed \(0<p<1\): order \(n\) below \(2/3\), \(n\log n\) at \(2/3\), and \(n^{3p/2}\) above. A Fenwick interval factorization supplies matching upper bounds. The claims are confined to pure-\(\eps\)-DP Laplace matrix mechanisms and the two stated squared-error criteria; they do not cover non-matrix continual mechanisms, approximate-DP sensitivity, or expected maxima across coordinates.

</details>


### [313] [Blockchain Transaction Simulation Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28747)
*Xiaocan Wang,Shixuan Guan,Tong Yang,Xiapu Luo,Yue Duan,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了"交易模拟钓鱼"（transaction simulation phishing）这一新型攻击，利用动态状态依赖的恶意合约欺骗钱包交易模拟功能，使模拟结果呈现良性或盈利而实际执行时重定向用户资金。作者提出了SIMGUARD——一种基于字节码的检测系统，结合静态与动态分析识别钓鱼合约，在四条链上检测到超4000个钓鱼合约、5700+受害者和约348万美元损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流加密货币钱包（如MetaMask）引入的交易模拟功能旨在保护用户免受传统钓鱼攻击，但该防御机制本身存在可被利用的漏洞。作者发现攻击者可以构造依赖动态区块链状态的智能合约，使模拟预览与真实链上执行产生差异，从而绕过交易模拟的防护屏障，需要系统性地研究并检测这类新型威胁。

Method: 首先对可利用于交易模拟钓鱼的合约进行分类（taxonomy），然后提出SIMGUARD检测系统，该方案在字节码层面结合静态分析和动态程序分析来识别钓鱼合约，并在Ethereum、Binance Smart Chain、Avalanche和Polygon四条区块链上进行大规模应用检测。

Result: SIMGUARD在2024年8月至2025年6月期间检测到超4000个钓鱼合约，识别出超过5700名受害者和约348万美元损失，其中91.5%发生在Ethereum上。聚类分析显示最大的钓鱼合约集群单独占总损失的约83%。

Conclusion: 研究结果暴露了当前钱包防御机制的关键弱点，交易模拟功能可被新型动态状态依赖的钓鱼合约所欺骗，亟需开发更稳健的交易模拟机制以应对此类攻击。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency users have increasingly become targets of phishing and scam attacks. To mitigate these threats, leading crypto wallets (e.g., MetaMask) have introduced transaction simulation, which previews a transaction's balance changes before on-chain execution. While effective against traditional fund-draining attacks, we show that this defense can itself be exploited by a new phishing technique, which we term transaction simulation phishing. This attack uses carefully crafted smart contracts whose execution depends on dynamic blockchain state, causing simulations to display benign or profitable outcomes while the actual on-chain execution redirects users' funds to attacker-controlled addresses.
  We present the first comprehensive study of transaction simulation phishing. We first develop a taxonomy of phishing contracts that can be utilized to facilitate this attack. Then, we propose SIMGUARD, a bytecode-level detection system that combines static and dynamic program analysis to identify phishing contracts. Applying SIMGUARD to Ethereum, Binance Smart Chain, Avalanche, and Polygon, we detect over 4,000 phishing contracts deployed between August 2024 and June 2025. Our analysis identifies more than 5,700 victims and approximately $3.48 million USD in losses, 91.5% of which occurred on Ethereum. Moreover, our clustering result reveals that the largest phishing contract cluster alone accounts for about 83% of the total losses. These results expose a critical weakness in current wallet defenses and highlight the urgent need for more robust transaction simulation mechanisms.

</details>


### [314] [Partial Derandomization for Leakage-Resilient Shamir's Secret Sharing over Composite Order Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28757)
*S. Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对复合阶域上防泄漏Shamir秘密共享方案的显式求值位置构造取得进展。基于Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025)的二分法，作者通过固定有理函数Φ的迭代来部分去随机化求值位置，将随机性从nd log p比特降至d log p比特，在n = O(d/log_p d)且重建阈值k≥2的参数范围内实现完全安全的单块泄漏防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maji等人(EUROCRYPT 2024)证明随机求值位置在复合阶域上对物理比特泄漏具有统计安全性，但其需要nd log p比特的随机性。Nguyen(EUROCRYPT 2025)证明了线性码秘密共享方案在此类泄漏下要么统计安全要么完全不安全。基于这一二分法，作者希望减少求值位置所需的随机性，实现部分去随机化。

Method: 作者将n个独立随机求值位置替换为固定有理函数Φ的迭代序列x_j = Φ^j(x_0)，仅需随机选择初始点x_0 ∈ F_{p^d}^*。核心技术是部分分式非退化论证，利用有理迭代的不同极点来证明方案的完全安全性（统计距离恰好为零）对抗单块泄漏。

Result: 在参数范围n = O(d/log_p d)和任何重建阈值k ≥ 2下，该构造成立，并将求值位置的随机性从nd log p比特降至d log p比特。方案对单块泄漏达到完全安全（统计距离精确为零）。

Conclusion: 本文通过有理函数迭代实现了对防泄漏Shamir秘密共享求值位置的部分去随机化，在受限参数范围内改进Maji等人的结果，为后续完全去随机化或进一步的随机性压缩提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We make progress on the question of constructing explicit evaluation places for leakage-resilient Shamir's secret sharing, over composite order fields. Previously, Maji et al. (EUROCRYPT 2024) showed that random evaluation places yield Shamir's secret sharing over the composite order field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ that is statistically secure against physical-bit leakage. Later, Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025) established a dichotomy that linear code-based secret-sharing scheme over the field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ is either statistically secure or completely insecure against such leakage.
  Building upon Nguyen's dichotomy, we present a partial derandomization of evaluation places, improving upon the Maji et al. result for a restricted regime of parameters. We replace the random choice of $n$ independent evaluation places by the iterates $x_j = Φ^j(x_0)$ of a simple fixed rational function $Φ$, where the initial point $x_0 \in \mathbb{F}_{p^d}^*$ is randomly chosen. The randomness in the evaluation places thus drops from $nd \log p$ bits to $d\log p$ bits. Our construction is valid for the regime $n = O(d/\log_p d)$, and any reconstruction threshold $k \ge 2$; in fact, the scheme attains perfect security (statistical distance exactly zero) against single-block leakage. Our technique is a partial fraction nondegeneracy argument that exploits the distinct poles of the rational iterates.

</details>


### [315] [YazSes: An Offline, Privacy-First, Cross-Platform Hold-to-Talk Voice-Dictation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28878)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: YazSes是一个完全在设备端运行的、开源（Apache-2.0）的按住说话语音听写守护进程，跨Linux/macOS/Windows平台，使用faster-whisper在CPU上进行本地转录（int8量化），并通过正则命令语法（可选小型语言模型路由）映射语音到编辑器/终端操作。系统无遥测、无数据泄露，支持可选加密的本地个性化循环。在200条LibriSpeech测试话语上，词错误率范围为4.82%（tiny.en）到2.59%（small.en），实时因子0.520，纯CPU解码快于实时；命令语法达到100%动作准确率，误报率为0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端语音听写服务虽然准确率高，但需要将用户语音流式传输到远程提供商，这在隐私敏感的职业场景以及离线/气隙环境中不可接受。现有的设备端替代方案要么受平台锁定，要么面向专家脚本编写而非即插即用的听写。作者旨在提供一个完全离线、跨平台、开源且隐私保护良好的语音听写解决方案。

Method: YazSes采用协议抽象层实现单一代码库跨平台（Linux/macOS/Windows）。架构为分阶段流水线，后端为JSON-RPC控制面。转录完全本地化，使用faster-whisper（CPU, int8量化）；音频采集采用按住说话（push-to-talk）而非持续监听模式。命令识别采用快速正则表达式语法，可选小型语言模型作为路由器，将话语映射到编辑器/终端操作。个性化循环为可选功能，语料库加密存储于设备本地，仅提出配置更改建议而非外传数据。

Result: 在单台商品Linux笔记本上评估Python实现：200条LibriSpeech测试-clean话语（40位说话人）上，词错误率从4.82%（tiny.en）到2.59%（small.en），小模型实时因子为0.520（纯CPU无GPU，解码快于实时）。命令语法达到100%动作准确率，普通听写在0.021ms/次调用时误报率为0.0%；非解码流水线仅增加0.289ms开销。macOS和Windows后端已实现并通过单元测试，但未进行端到端评估。

Conclusion: YazSes证明了完全在设备端、无GPU的CPU环境下，语音听写仍能达到可用的准确率和实时性能，同时通过按住说话机制、零遥测、加密本地语料等设计提供严格的隐私保障。系统及可复现的基准测试工具已全部开源。

Abstract: Cloud voice-dictation services deliver strong accuracy but require streaming a user's speech to a remote provider, an unacceptable trade-off in privacy-sensitive professions and offline or air-gapped settings; the leading on-device alternatives are either platform-locked or aimed at expert scripting rather than plug-and-play dictation. We present YazSes, an open-source (Apache-2.0) hold-to-talk voice dictation daemon that runs entirely on-device, with a single codebase targeting Linux, macOS, and Windows through a protocol-based platform abstraction. YazSes transcribes speech locally with faster-whisper (CPU, int8) and injects the result into the focused application; a fast regex command grammar, backed by an optional small-language-model router, maps utterances to editor and terminal actions. Nothing leaves the machine: recording is push-to-talk rather than always-listening, there is no telemetry, and an opt-in personalization loop keeps its corpus encrypted on-device and proposes configuration changes instead of shipping data out. We describe the system architecture -- a staged pipeline behind a protocol-based platform abstraction with a JSON-RPC control plane -- and its privacy and threat model. We evaluate the shipping Python implementation on a single commodity Linux laptop; the macOS and Windows backends are implemented and unit-tested but not end-to-end evaluated here. On 200 LibriSpeech test-clean utterances spanning 40 speakers, word error rate ranges from 4.82% (tiny.en) to 2.59% (small.en) at a real-time factor of 0.520 for small.en, decoding faster than real time on CPU with no GPU. The command grammar reaches 100% action accuracy with a 0.0% false-positive rate on plain dictation at 0.021 ms per call, and the non-decode pipeline adds 0.289 ms of overhead. The system and the reproducible benchmark harness behind every number in this paper are public.

</details>


### [316] [Hollow-LLM Attack: Computationally Trivial Weights in Zero-Knowledge Verification of LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28884)
*Chen Gong,Beijie Liu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零知识（ZK）LLM推理验证中的一个关键漏洞：现有的ZK证明只能验证输出与公开架构和私有权重的一致性（等量正确性），但无法约束证明者实际执行的计算量。作者形式化了这一"努力差距"（effort gap），并提出Hollow-LLM攻击——不诚实的服务提供者保留声明的架构和参数数量，但嵌入"幽灵权重"（ghost weights），其代数结构使有效计算坍缩为远小于声明规模的模型。这些幽灵权重能通过验证电路并产生有效证明，使得提供者以小型模型的计算成本提供服务，同时声称大型模型的规模，且零质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模增长并主要部署在远程平台上，验证推理执行的忠实性变得至关重要。ZK证明虽然能保证输出的等量正确性，但作者发现它无法绑定产生输出所付出的计算努力，即存在未被重视的"努力差距"。这使得不诚实的提供者可以利用该漏洞以低成本获利的空间。

Method: 作者形式化了努力差距的概念，提出了Hollow-LLM攻击框架：设计保留了声明架构和参数数量的"幽灵权重"嵌入标准Transformer块中，使这些权重的代数结构在数学上坍缩有效计算（如让部分矩阵运算变为低秩或零运算），同时仍然满足ZK验证电路的所有约束条件。作者刻画了与标准Transformer块兼容的幽灵权重族，并分析了其成本降低与质量保持的平衡。

Result: 作者表明幽灵权重能够成功通过ZK验证电路并产生有效证明，支持拒绝服务的提供者以远小于声明模型的计算成本运行（大幅降低推理成本），同时在相同验证电路下实现零质量损失，形成一个有利可图的失衡均衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明"正确推理的证明"并不等同于"大规模模型执行的证明"。现有的ZK LLM推理验证存在根本性漏洞，需要额外的保护机制将正确性与可验证的计算工作量绑定在一起，未来的ZK推理系统需要超越等量正确性验证，增加对实际计算努力的可证约束。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in scale and are predominantly served from remote platforms, verifying faithful inference execution becomes critical (i.e., ensuring that a provider actually executes the advertised model and computational workload rather than a tampered or downsized variant). Zero-knowledge (ZK) LLM inference offers an appealing approach. It promises public verifiability and delivers per-instance guarantees of equational correctness by proving that an output is consistent with executing a public architecture under committed, private weights. Though, we show that it does not bind the effort expended to produce the output. In this paper, we formalize this overlooked effort gap and introduce the Hollow-LLM Attack, in which a dishonest provider retains the declared architecture and parameter count but embeds ghost weights whose algebraic structure collapses effective computation. These witnesses satisfy the verification circuit and yield valid proofs, even though the dishonest model owner, who serves as the prover, performs computation commensurate with a much smaller model than the declared public architecture. This creates a profitable equilibrium in which providers deliver provably correct outputs at small-model cost while overclaiming model size. Accordingly, we characterize concrete families of ghost weights that compose with standard transformer blocks and show that such hollow deployments substantially reduce serving cost with zero quality loss under the same verification circuit. These findings underscore that proof of correct inference is not proof of large-model execution and necessitate additional protections to bind correctness to verifiable computational work.

</details>


### [317] [A Biometric Sensor Network to Enable Real-Time Measurement of Individual Student Engagement in STEM Lecture Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28944)
*Ahmed Elsayed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"生物传感器网络"(BSN)的系统，用于在STEM课堂中实时、非侵入式地测量和持续追踪学生个体参与度。系统由分布式"学生处理单元"(SPU)组成，支持数据集采集和实时分析两种模式，所有面部检测、注视估计和情感分析均在设备端完成，确保可识别视频数据不离开设备，同时满足非侵入、非侵入性、非污名化、实时和自动五项设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生参与度是STEM教育中学术表现和保留率的关键预测因子，但现有测量方法存在侵入性强、人工密集、不适合实时课堂使用等问题。因此需要一种能够在真实课堂环境中实时、持续、无干扰地测量学生个体参与度的新型系统。

Method: 提出BSN架构，由多个SPU构成分布式感知节点。每个SPU支持两种模式：(i)数据集采集模式，临时录制原始学生视频以构建私有SE数据集；(ii)分析模式，对10秒视频片段进行实时推理，全程在设备端完成包括人脸检测、注视估计和情感分析的处理，不存储不传输原始帧。配套安全后端基础设施负责设备认证、会话编排和加密数据摄取。系统整合了硬件设计、计算机视觉管线、无线网络、安全协议和会话级数据管理。

Result: 系统从架构层面满足了五项设计要求：非侵入、非侵入性、非污名化、实时和自动，并通过设备端处理和加密数据传输严格保障学生数据安全与隐私。

Conclusion: BSN提供了一种在真实STEM课堂中兼顾实时测量能力与隐私保护的完整解决方案，通过硬件、视觉、网络、安全和数据管理的多层面集成，为持续追踪学生个体参与度提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Student engagement (SE) is a critical predictor of academic performance and retention in STEM education, yet existing measurement approaches are often intrusive, manually intensive, or unsuitable for real-time classroom use. This thesis proposes a novel $\textit{Biometric Sensor Network}$ (BSN) designed to enable real-time measurement and continuous tracking of individual student engagement in STEM classroom environments. The system enables capturing of behavioral, emotional, and cognitive indicators through camera-based sensing while preserving ethical and privacy constraints.
  To measure these indicators unobtrusively and ethically, we propose a BSN composed of $\textit{Student Processing Units}$ (SPUs) that function as distributed sensing nodes. The network is explicitly designed to satisfy five objectives: it must be $\textbf{non-intrusive}, \textbf{non-invasive}, \textbf{non-stigmatizing}, \textbf{real-time}$, and $\textbf{automatic}$, while ensuring rigorous protection of student data security and privacy.
  Each SPU supports two operational modes: (i) a $\textit{dataset-collection mode}$, in which raw student video is temporarily recorded to construct a private SE dataset for model training and validation, and (ii) an $\textit{analysis mode}$, in which the SPU performs real-time inference on 10-second video segments without storing or transmitting raw frames. In this analysis role, each SPU enables fully on-device processing---including face detection, gaze estimation, and affective analysis---ensuring that no identifiable video data leaves the device. A secure backend infrastructure manages device authentication, session orchestration, and encrypted data ingestion. The full system integrates hardware design, computer-vision pipelines, wireless networking, security protocols, and session-level data management.

</details>


### [318] [MESS: Fast and Private Semantic Search on Multi-Graph HNSW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28999)
*Haoyu Cui,Zengpeng Li,Tien Tuan Anh Dinh,Mei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MESS是一个兼顾隐私、准确性和效率的语义搜索系统。它将原始向量映射为二进制码，结合局部敏感哈希和随机响应技术，在多图HNSW索引上进行搜索。通过两层查询扰动机制，MESS同时保护数据、查询、访问模式和搜索模式的隐私，且因无需同态加密或ORAM而具有高效性。实验表明其延迟比最先进基线低15.08倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私保护语义搜索系统分别采用同态加密（HE）、不透明随机访问存储器（ORAM）或差分隐私（DP）方案，但均无法同时实现隐私、准确性和效率三者的统一。因此需要设计一种全新系统来同时满足这三个关键属性。

Method: MESS将原始向量映射为二进制码，应用局部敏感哈希（LSH）和随机响应（randomized response）进行扰动，并在扰动码上构建多图分层可导航小世界（HNSW）索引。系统通过两层查询扰动机制确保搜索模式隐私，多图索引缓解扰动对结果质量的影响以保证准确性，搜索直接在扰动码上进行以避免同态加密或ORAM的开销从而保证效率。

Result: MESS在保证数据、查询、访问模式和搜索模式隐私的前提下，实现了高准确性，且延迟比最先进的基线方法低15.08倍。

Conclusion: MESS通过将向量二进制化并引入LSH与随机响应结合的多图HNSW索引，成功地同时实现了隐私、准确性和效率三个目标，为隐私保护的语义搜索提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic search systems map data to a high-dimensional vector space and support retrieval of similar data via approximate nearest neighbor search. When the system is hosted by a trusted cloud provider, there is no privacy for the data or the query. Our goal is to design a system with three properties: privacy, accuracy, and efficiency. Existing works adopt either homomorphic encryption (HE), oblivious RAM (ORAM), or a differential privacy (DP) approach. They fall short of achieving all three properties.
  In this paper, we present MESS, a system that realizes our goal. It maps the original vectors into binary codes, applies locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) and randomized response, and constructs a multi-graph Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) index over the perturbed codes. MESS ensures data, query, and access pattern privacy. It also ensures search pattern privacy via a two-phase query perturbation mechanism. The multi-graph index mitigates the impact of perturbation on result quality, thereby achieving accuracy. MESS is efficient because search is performed directly over perturbed codes, without the overhead of homomorphic encryption or ORAM. We give formal analysis of the system's privacy and extensive evaluation of its performance. The results show that MESS achieves up to 15.08\times lower latency than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [319] [GoldenRetriever: Non-Interactive Homomorphic Encrypted Retrieval for Privacy-Preserving RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29019)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Dongdong Wang,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于阈值选择（threshold selection）的非交互式加密检索框架用于RAG系统，利用CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度评估和文档选择，无需暴露查询内容、中间分数或所选文档索引，将计算复杂度从二次降为线性，并通过精度稳定的掩码极化方法实现加密计算与离散token重建之间的衔接。实验表明该方法在保持竞争力的检索效果的同时显著降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG管线通常基于明文数据运作，存在严重的隐私泄露风险。已有的隐私保护检索方法依赖同态加密或私有信息检索，但往往需要交互式协议或基于排序（top-k）的选择机制，导致高延迟和潜在的信息泄露。因此需要一个实用的、非交互式的、能够在加密域完成文档检索的框架。

Method: 本文提出基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架。核心设计包括：(1) 采用阈值选择替代昂贵的top-k排序，相似度超过预设阈值的文档即被选中，使计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)；(2) 基于CKKS同态加密实现全加密的相似度计算与文档选择，不泄露查询内容、中间分数或所选索引；(3) 引入精度稳定的掩码极化方法来弥合近似加密计算与离散token重建之间的差距，确保所选文档能被准确恢复。

Result: 在标准检索基准上的实验表明，该方法在检索有效性方面具有竞争力，同时相比基于排序的加密方法显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 阈值选择机制可作为规模化、安全的RAG系统的实用基础，在隐私保护和检索效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but existing pipelines typically operate on plaintext data, raising significant privacy concerns. Prior work on privacy-preserving retrieval leverages cryptographic techniques such as homomorphic encryption (HE) and private information retrieval (PIR), but often relies on interactive protocols or ranking-based selection mechanisms that incur high latency and potential information leakage. In this paper, we propose a practical non-interactive encrypted retrieval framework for RAG based on threshold selection. Instead of performing expensive top-$k$ ranking under encryption, our approach selects documents whose similarity scores exceed a predefined threshold, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to linear in the corpus size. We implement this design using CKKS-based homomorphic computation, enabling fully encrypted similarity evaluation and document selection without revealing query content, intermediate scores, or selected indices. To bridge the gap between approximate encrypted computation and discrete token reconstruction, we introduce a precision-stable mask polarization method that ensures accurate recovery of selected documents. Experiments on standard retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive retrieval effectiveness while significantly reducing latency compared to ranking-based encrypted methods. These results highlight threshold-based selection as a practical foundation for scalable and secure RAG systems.

</details>


### [320] [Memory Provenance Laundering in LLM Agents: A Non-Amplification Firewall for Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29167)
*Jinghan Xu,Yiyong Xiao,Wanru Shao,Hankai Liu,Xinjin Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型（LLM）代理在长期记忆整合过程中存在"记忆来源洗白"（memory provenance laundering）漏洞：外部不可信观测可能被重写成看似用户历史或工作流支持的内容，从而清除低信任来源对动作触发器的权威限制。作者提出了来源保持记忆防火墙（PPMF），一种轻量级记忆中间件，通过保留平台维护的来源信息并将动作风险与相关记忆的权威度匹配来授权工具调用。实验表明，在固定风险策略下，易受攻击的整合记忆攻击成功率可达100%；而PPMF可阻止所有未授权的高风险动作通过，同时保持已验证的良性动作和低风险记忆的使用可执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的长期记忆在复用先前偏好和工作流的同时，会将不可信的观测转化为持久化的动作上下文，产生安全隐患。作者识别出"记忆来源洗白"这一漏洞：在基于LLM的记忆整合过程中，外部观测可能被改写成看似良性的用户历史，保留动作触发条件的同时抹去其低信任来源，而现有的提示过滤器、内容清理器和工具防护都无法在损失性记忆整合后强制实施来源权威不放大原则。

Method: 作者形式化了来源权威不放大边界，并实现为PPMF（来源保持记忆防火墙）。PPMF作为轻量级记忆中间件，保留平台维护的来源信息，并根据动作风险与动作相关记忆的权威度进行匹配来授权工具调用。评估采用schema引导的方式，配合固定风险策略进行验证。

Result: 在schema引导的评估中，易受攻击的整合记忆其攻击成功率（ASR）可达100%；而在平台维护的来源信息、确认和风险标签完好保留的情况下，所有未经授权的高风险动作均无法通过PPMF网关，同时已验证的良性动作和针对性的低风险记忆使用仍可正常执行。

Conclusion: PPMF能够有效抵御记忆来源洗白攻击，通过保留来源元数据并在动作授权时匹配动作风险与记忆权威度，在不牺牲良性功能的前提下堵住了长期记忆整合带来的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets large language model(LLM) agents reuse prior preferences and work flows, but it also turns untrusted observations into persistent action context. We identify memory provenance laundering: during LLM-based memory consolidation, an external observation may be rewritten as apparent user history or workflow support, preserving an action trigger while erasing the low-trust source that should limit its authority. Existing prompt filters, content sanitizers, and tool guards do not enforce source-authority non-amplification after lossy memory consolidation. We formalize this boundary and instantiate it as Provenance-Preserving Memory Fire wall (PPMF), a lightweight memory middleware that preserves platform-maintained provenance and authorizes tool calls by matching action risk to the authority of action-relevant memories. In our schema-grounded evaluation with fixed risk policies, vulnerable consolidated memories reach up to 1.000 attack success rate(ASR); with intact platform-maintained provenance, confirmation, and risk labels, no evaluated unauthorized high-risk action passes the PPMF gate while confirmed benign actions and targeted low-risk memory use remain executable.

</details>


### [321] [Alignment Is Local: A Paired Diagnostic for GUI Agents under User-Side Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29199)
*Haoxin An,Yunpeng Song,Zihao Bai,Zhongmin Cai,Guojun Xiong,Chenhao Lin,Wentao Chen,Feng Wei,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究揭示移动GUI智能体的提示级对齐（guardrail）是一种局部防御现象：它仅在单回合、明确口头化意图的评估切片中表现可靠，在多回合升级链和隐蔽请求等真实交互场景下系统性失效，静态单回合ASR指标会系统性高估部署鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体在普适计算环境中的可信部署需要能在动态交互和精确威胁条件下持续有效的对齐，而当前主流的轻量级防御（提示级对齐）仅在狭窄的评估切片中测量，作者旨在论证其作为"局部现象"的本质，并量化其在真实用户可遍历的两个轴向上的系统性退化。

Method: 作者采用配对诊断方法，在三个前沿GUI智能体上进行屏幕接地、用户侧说服实验（无环境注入），对比无守门员与有单行守门员两种条件，沿两个轴向测量攻击成功率（ASR）和过度拒绝率：一是从独立探测到四回合升级链，二是明确与隐蔽（concealed）意图的对比。

Result: 单行守门员在单回合设置下可将ASR大幅降低约40个百分点且过度拒绝成本接近于零；但从独立探测转向四回合升级链后，受防御的ASR在每款模型上回升约20个百分点。相对中性基线，Qwen的守门员明显侵蚀，而Claude和GPT主要表现为防御正交的动态风险。守门员的引入使显著性差距方向翻转：无守门员时隐蔽请求并不系统性地更成功，有守门员时隐蔽请求反而更成功，表明防御仅在意图被明确命名时激活。

Conclusion: 静态单回合ASR会以系统化且可预测的方式高估实际部署中的鲁棒性；提示级对齐作为轻量级防御存在本质局限，需要在多回合动态交互和隐蔽意图条件下进行更全面的评估与设计。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of GUI agents in ubiquitous computing settings requires alignment that survives dynamic interaction and precise threat conditions, not just single-turn refusal of explicit harmful requests. We argue that prompt-level alignment, the dominant lightweight defense in current mobile agents, is a local phenomenon: it works reliably only in the narrow evaluation slice where it is typically measured, namely single-turn, explicitly-verbalized intent, and degrades systematically along two axes that any real user can traverse. Using a paired diagnostic on three frontier GUI agents, screen-grounded, user-side persuasion, with no environment injection, we show that a one-line guardrail achieves large single-shot ASR reductions, up to roughly 40 points, at near-zero over-refusal cost. Nevertheless, moving from independent probes to four-turn escalation chains raises guarded ASR by approximately 20 points on every model. Relative to the neutral baselines, this increase reflects substantial guardrail erosion for Qwen but a largely defense-orthogonal dynamic risk for Claude and GPT. The sign of the salience gap flips under the guardrail: concealed requests are not systematically more successful than explicit ones without a guardrail, but are more successful with one, indicating that the defense engages primarily when intent is named. Static single-turn ASR therefore overstates deployed robustness by a systematic and predictable margin.

</details>


### [322] [MOSAIC: Masked Outsourcing of Secure AI Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29221)
*James Hsin-yu Chiang,Sheila Zingg,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种新颖的矩阵乘法掩膜协议，通过引入少量噪声来放松正确性要求，使得计算能力弱但受信任的客户端可以将大型AI计算（如70B transformer推理）安全外包给不受信任但强大的服务器，同时保证服务器学不到输入和模型信息。其安全性基于决策性LWE和LPN假设，并利用随机Hadamard旋转控制误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在客户端持有输入和模型、服务器必须两者都不学习的场景下，安全高效地将AI计算外包给不受信任的强大服务器是一个挑战。现有工作无法扩展到大型矩阵，难以支撑如大型transformer推理等现代工作负载，因此需要一种能大幅超越先前工作规模的解决方案。

Method: MOSAIC的核心是一种新颖的矩阵乘法掩膜协议，通过向乘法结果引入少量噪声来放松正确性，从而以最优的渐近客户端开销实现计算外包。安全性归约到决策性LWE（Learning With Errors）和LPN（Learning Parity with Noise）假设。针对transformer多层结构中噪声累积的问题，提出基于随机Hadamard旋转的误差缩放机制来控制误差增长。同时设计了端到端实现，利用RDMA类网络在节点间传输激活值、KV缓存和权重，将TCB保持在较小范围。

Result: MOSAIC在后端实现上比先前工作快数个数量级，可扩展到比先前工作大得多的矩阵。在70B transformer模型上，其困惑度与主流量化方法相当，在HumanEval基准上甚至匹配全精度BF16推理效果。端到端实现展示了在分布式现代数据中心中支持大规模机密AI计算的路径。

Conclusion: 通过放松正确性要求引入受控噪声，MOSAIC在保持可靠安全性的同时实现了极高性能，为大型transformer推理等现代工作负载的安全外包提供了可行方案，并证明了规模化机密计算在现代数据中心中的潜在可行性。

Abstract: We address the challenge of securely and efficiently outsourcing AI computations from a trusted but computationally weak client to an untrusted but powerful server, in the setting where the client holds both the input and the model, and the server must learn neither. We present MOSAIC, whose core is a novel matrix-multiplication masking protocol that scales to far larger matrices than prior work, enabling the safe outsourcing of modern workloads such as large transformer inference. By introducing small amounts of noise to the multiplication result and thereby relaxing correctness, MOSAIC achieves optimal asymptotic client overhead and concrete runtimes orders of magnitude faster than prior work. Its security reduces to the decisional LWE and LPN assumptions.
  Because this noise accumulates across the many layers of a transformer, a key technical challenge is bounding error growth; MOSAIC addresses this with an error-scaling mechanism based on random Hadamard rotations. On large 70B transformer models, MOSAIC's perplexity is comparable to popular quantization approaches and even matches full-precision BF16 inference on HumanEval.
  Finally, we present an end-to-end implementation showing how ideas like MOSAIC can promise a path towards large-scale confidential AI in modern data centers. Non-confidential inference is already distributed across phase (prefill/decode), layer, and time to maximize utilization of heterogeneous hardware, using RDMA-like networking to move activations, cached KV values, and weights across nodes. MOSAIC enables scaling of confidential compute by keeping the trusted computing base (TCB) small and outsourcing the bulk of the AI computation to untrusted accelerators.

</details>


### [323] [JUNO: Aggregated Vector Consensus for Optimal Asynchronous Common Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29244)
*Liangrong Zhao,Qin Wang,Joseph K. Liu,Jiangshan Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus）这一新的异步网络向量共识原语，并基于其实现了Juno——一个异步公共子集（ACS）协议，达到最优O(n²)消息复杂度。实验表明，Juno相比HoneyBadgerBFT吞吐量提升93%，相比Dory提升47%，填补了向量共识协议在全异步网络中的应用空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量共识协议此前主要针对同步或部分同步网络设计，在全异步网络中的应用存在明显缺口。作者旨在提出一个适用于异步网络的向量共识原语，并通过高效的ACS协议实现来验证其可行性和性能优势。

Method: 论文提出聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus）原语，该原语通过聚合独立进程输入来输出一个值向量以实现一致性。基于此原语，作者进一步设计了Juno协议，完整实现了聚合向量共识并作为异步公共子集（ACS）协议使用，达到了最优的O(n²)消息复杂度。

Result: 通过实现与评估，Juno相比传统HoneyBadgerBFT实现了93%的平均吞吐量提升，相比当前最先进的Dory协议实现了47%的吞吐量提升，验证了协议的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 本文在向量共识协议的全异步网络应用上取得了显著进展，所提出的聚合向量共识原语和Juno协议在消息复杂度和实际吞吐性能上均表现出优秀的特性，为异步分布式系统中的共识协议设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose \textit{aggregated vector consensus}, a new vector consensus primitive designed for asynchronous networks. The primitive achieves agreement by outputting a vector of values aggregated from independent process inputs. We then introduce \textsc{Juno}, an asynchronous common subset (ACS) protocol that fully implements our aggregated vector consensus to attain optimal $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ message complexity.
  We further implement and evaluate \textsc{Juno} in comparison with the legacy HoneyBadgerBFT and the state-of-the-art Dory. Experiment results demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our protocol demonstrates an average throughput performance improvement of 93\% compared with HoneyBadgerBFT and a 47\% improvement compared with Dory. Notably, our study makes significant progress in addressing the gap in applying vector consensus protocol in fully asynchronous networks.

</details>


### [324] [On the Resilience of 5G NR Against Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29384)
*Sotiris Michaelides,Fidelius Lula,Daniel Eguiguren Chavez,Michael Rademacher,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在ns-3模拟器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰器，用于系统性地比较不同物理层配置（如技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽）下5G网络对蜂窝干扰的韧性，发现信道带宽和频率范围是关键因素，而技术和子载波间隔影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络越来越多地应用于工业网络和关键基础设施等可用性关键系统中，理解其对蜂窝干扰的韧性至关重要。然而现有研究仅针对固定5G物理层配置进行孤立评估，难以进行可靠的横向比较，例如识别不同频段或信道带宽在抗干扰韧性上的差异。

Method: 作者在ns-3模拟器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰器，利用仿真的可控确定性，系统性地对比了不同维度下的蜂窝干扰影响，包括蜂窝技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽。

Result: 结果表明，蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性影响较小，而信道带宽和频率范围在提升干扰韧性方面起关键作用。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了5G抗干扰韧性缺乏系统性对比评估的空白，通过开源模拟工具为后续研究提供可复现的基础，并明确指出信道带宽和频率范围是提升抗干扰韧性的主要设计考量维度。

Abstract: With the increasing use of 5G networks in availability-critical systems, including industrial networks and critical infrastructure, a comprehensive understanding of their resilience to cellular jamming has become imperative. However, research so far has focused on isolated evaluations under fixed 5G physical-layer configurations, making it difficult to perform sound comparisons, for example, to identify differences between frequency bands or channel bandwidths w.r.t. jamming resilience. To fill this gap, we implement and release an open-source 5G jammer within the ns-3 simulator. Leveraging the controllable determinism of simulation, we compare the impact of cellular jamming across different dimensions such as cellular technologies, frequencies, subcarrier spacing, and channel bandwidth. Our results show that while cellular technology and subcarrier spacing have little impact, channel bandwidth and frequency range play a critical role in improving jamming resilience.

</details>


### [325] [On fair and realistic performance evaluations for graph-based lateral movement detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29390)
*Corentin Larroche*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调研了横向移动检测研究中两个流行数据集在预处理和标注方法上的差异，展示了这些差异对下游评估公平性和真实性的影响，提出了有依据的预处理与标注策略，并在新策略下重新评估了三个广泛引用的检测方法，发现结果与原论文有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向移动检测研究虽已取得显著进展，但文献中对基准数据集的使用方式（预处理和事件标注）在不同论文间存在实质性差异，这可能导致评估结果不可比较、失真或不公平，缺乏统一规范。

Method: 作者调研了两个流行数据集在文献中的预处理和标注方法学，分析其对下游评估的影响；提出有充分依据的标准化预处理和标注策略；并在新策略下重新评估三个广泛引用的横向移动检测方法，与原报告结果进行对比。

Result: 在新提出的预处理和标注策略下，三个广泛引用的横向移动检测方法的评估结果与原论文中报告的结果存在显著差异，进一步证实了数据集预处理和标注实践对检测器评估的关键影响。

Conclusion: 数据集预处理和标注方法的差异对横向移动检测器的公平性和真实性评估具有重要影响，研究社区需要采用标准化的预处理和标注策略以确保评估结果的可比性和可靠性。

Abstract: Research on lateral movement detection has made significant progress in recent years, spurred by the widespread availability of benchmark datasets that make evaluating detectors practical. However, the exact way in which these benchmark datasets are used varies across the literature: both the preprocessing applied before feeding the data to the detector and the labeling of lateral movement-related events change substantially from one paper to another. We survey preprocessing and labeling methodologies for two popular datasets and demonstrate their impact on the fairness and realism of downstream evaluations. We also propose well-grounded preprocessing and labeling policies for these datasets. Finally, we re-evaluate three widely cited lateral movement detection methods under these new policies; our results differ significantly from those reported in the original papers, further highlighting the critical importance of dataset preprocessing and labeling practices in evaluating lateral movement detectors.

</details>


### [326] [Enforcing Cryptographic Distributed-VCS Access Control with No Trust on Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29417)
*Xin Xu,Zhen Yang,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Liangqin Ren,Bo Chen,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DVAC，一种基于属性加密（ABE）和属性签名（ABS）的分布式版本控制系统访问控制方案。DVAC通过密码学手段在不依赖中心服务器的情况下实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制，并利用以太坊智能合约维护访问控制策略，同时通过版本控制适配协议保证DVCS核心功能的完整性。原型已集成于Git，性能评估显示开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有版本控制系统（VCS）的访问控制方案都需要中心服务器的参与，无法在完全分布式场景下部署；此外，已有方案通常无法对写权限实施细粒度的访问控制。这限制了协作工作在分布式环境中的安全性和灵活性。

Method: DVAC采用以下技术手段：(1) 基于属性加密（ABE）和属性签名（ABS）在分布式用户节点上实施密码学访问控制，实现文件粒度的读写分离控制；(2) 设计版本控制适配协议，在保障数据安全的同时维持DVCS核心版本控制功能的完整性；(3) 利用以太坊智能合约维护访问控制策略，实现策略的分布式存储和可信管理；(4) 架构设计追求与现有成熟DVCS（如Git）的最小改动集成。

Result: 实现了DVAC原型并成功集成到Git中，通过全面的性能评估验证了DVAC引入的开销是适中的。

Conclusion: DVAC解决了分布式版本控制系统中缺乏无中心服务器支持的细粒度访问控制问题，通过ABE和ABS实现读写分离控制，并借助智能合约实现策略的可信管理，且能以适度开销集成到现有DVCS中。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage changes to software code and various types of documents. Unlike CVCS, where entities obtain data from a central server, each entity in DVCS stores the entire repository and shares it independently. In VCS, existing access control schemes require the participation of a central server and cannot be deployed in a completely distributed scenario. Additionally, these schemes often fail to enforce fine-grained access control for write permissions, which is crucial for collaborative work in a distributed environment. In this paper, we propose a distributed version control system access control scheme (named DVAC), which enforces cryptographic access control on distributed user nodes based on attribute-based encryption (ABE) and attribute-based signature (ABS). DVAC is designed to enforce a cryptographic access control protocol for DVCS, which enables file granularity read and write separation access control without the support of a central server. To ensure the integrity of the core version control functions in DVCS while protecting data security, DVAC incorporates a version control adaptation protocol. Additionally, DVAC leverages Ethereum smart contracts to maintain access control policies, ensuring distributed storage and trusted management of access policies. The architecture of DVAC is designed to seamlessly integrate with existing mature DVCS, such as Git, with minimal modifications. We have implemented a prototype of DVAC and integrated it with Git. A comprehensive performance evaluation was conducted to assess the overhead introduced by DVAC, and it was demonstrated that the overhead is modest.

</details>


### [327] [Bending the Curve: Operational Cyber Epidemiology for Ransomware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29444)
*Stephen V Flowerday,Nikolay Lipskiy,Steven Furnell,Callum E Flowerday,John Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个运营性流行病学框架，将SEIR（易感-暴露-感染-移除）模型适配到勒索软件事件管理领域，以将技术遥测与遏制决策联系起来，并将传播状态与观测状态分离，提供近实时的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件通常被视为检测问题，但最具破坏性的事件实际上更像疫情暴发——单一立足点可通过身份、管理工具和共享服务传播扩散，而响应者在信息不完整的情况下做着时间紧迫的决策。现有方法缺乏将技术遥测与遏制决策连接起来的共享语言和量化工具。

Method: 1) 将SEIR流行病学模型适配到勒索软件事件管理中，其中Exposed表示潜伏性妥协和预置（包括停留期），Infectious表示主动横向传播；2) 参考ISO 5477:2023公共卫生应急准备和响应信息管理指南以及2025 UNDRR-ISC危害信息档案，定义可互操作的勒索软件案例定义和必备信息要素（Essential Elements of Information）；3) 使用基本再生数R0和有效再生数Re作为安全运营中心的定向近实时决策辅助指标；4) 将传播状态与观测状态分离，避免混淆扩散动态与检测能力；5) 基于公开报告事件（WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars）验证框架，并推导保护阈值启发式规则和工具无关的剧本卡片。

Result: 通过WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars等公开事件案例验证，爆发行指标能够支持更早隔离、凭据遏制和恢复排序。论文推导出将Re降低至1以下的实际保护阈值启发式规则，并提供了将运营信息与明确行动触发器关联的工具无关剧本卡片。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是建立了一种共享语言，在资源约束条件下将技术遥测与遏制决策连接起来，为安全运营中心提供流行病学视角下的勒索软件事件管理方法。

Abstract: Ransomware is often treated as a detection problem, but the most disruptive incidents behave more like outbreaks. A single foothold can spread through identities, administrative tools, and shared services while responders make time-critical decisions with incomplete visibility. This paper presents an operational cyber epidemiology framework that adapts the Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Removed (SEIR) model to ransomware incident management. In this ontology, Exposed denotes latent compromise and staging, including the dwell period before confirmed secondary compromise, while Infectious denotes active lateral propagation. Drawing on ISO 5477:2023 guidance for public health emergency preparedness and response information management and the 2025 UNDRR-ISC Hazard Information Profiles, the framework defines interoperable ransomware case definitions and Essential Elements of Information for cross-incident comparison. Basic and effective reproduction numbers, R0 and Re, are used as directional, near-real-time decision aids for security operations centers. Propagation state is separated from observation status to avoid confusing spread dynamics with detection capability. Publicly reported incidents, including WannaCry, NotPetya, SolarWinds, and MGM and Caesars, illustrate how outbreak-style measures can support earlier isolation, credential containment, and restoration sequencing. The paper also derives practical protection-threshold heuristics aimed at reducing Re below 1 and provides a tool-agnostic playbook card linking operational information to explicit action triggers. The primary contribution is a shared language that connects technical telemetry to containment decisions under resource constraints.

</details>


### [328] [Beyond Resilience: Antifragility in Critical Infrastructure Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29550)
*Stephen Flowerday,Mauricio Papa,Ethan Flowerday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向关键基础设施网络安全的反脆弱性理论（AFT），基于五状态韧性系统模型和Jensen增益的数学定义，通过两层级实证设计（CISSM事件数据库与HAI工业控制数据集）检验了三个确认性假设和一个探索性命题，建立了未来反脆弱性测试的两个前提条件：差异化脆弱性负担和过程级扰动可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施网络安全亟需超越恢复导向、迈向在中断条件下有界改进的框架，但针对运营技术（OT）领域的经验支撑理论仍然有限。现有研究缺乏将反脆弱性概念操作化并加以实证检验的理论基础。

Method: 本文构建了反脆弱性理论（AFT），基于五状态韧性系统模型和Jensen增益与中断后增益的有界数学定义。实证部分采用两层级设计：（1）使用CISSM网络事件数据库中与CI相关的子集；（2）结合HAI硬件在环工业控制数据集。共检验三个确认性假设和一个探索性命题。

Result: OT相邻部门中破坏性或混合事件占比显著高于对照组（65.3%对比46.8%，p<0.001），物理攻击和数据攻击子类型更为集中。HAI数据中，攻击标注观测比正常观测超出基线偏差第95百分位的可能性高7.43倍（p<0.001）。在连续攻击窗口间，平均过程状态偏差显著下降（Spearman rho=-0.688, p=0.007），表明存在可测量的响应变化而非适应性增益的直接证据。

Conclusion: 研究发现确立了未来反脆弱性测试的两个前提条件：差异化脆弱性负担和过程级扰动可观测性，为关键基础设施网络安全领域反脆弱性的后续实证研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure cybersecurity increasingly requires frameworks that move beyond recovery toward bounded improvement under disruption, yet empirically grounded theories for operational technology remain limited. This paper develops a Theory of Antifragility (AFT) for critical infrastructure (CI) cybersecurity, anchored in a five-state Resilient System Model and a bounded mathematical definition based on Jensen gain and post-disruption gain. A two-layer empirical design pairs a CI-relevant subset of the CISSM Cyber Events Database with the HAI hardware-in-the-loop industrial control dataset and tests three confirmatory hypotheses and one exploratory proposition. OT-adjacent sectors show significantly higher shares of disruptive or mixed events than comparison sectors (65.3 percent versus 46.8 percent, p less than 0.001), together with a greater concentration of physical-attack and data-attack subtypes. In HAI, attack-labeled observations were 7.43 times more likely than normal observations to exceed the 95th percentile of baseline deviation (p less than 0.001). Across successive attack windows, mean process-state deviation declined significantly (Spearman rho = -0.688, p = 0.007), indicating measurable response variation rather than proof of adaptive gain. Together, the findings establish two prerequisites for future antifragility testing: differentiated fragility burden and process-level perturbation observability.

</details>


### [329] [CWEEP: A Lexical Static Analysis Framework for CWE Early Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29604)
*Bryan Kwan,Benjamin Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CWEEP是一个用于检测寄存器传输级（RTL）硬件代码安全弱点的静态分析框架，无需详细安全规格说明即可在早期开发阶段使用，能定位漏洞确切位置并支持自动代码修复建议。在文献数据集上，CWEEP的正确警告率高达60.8%，远超先前工作的17.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件层日益成为攻击者的关注焦点，但现有硬件安全验证技术需要大量具备安全专业知识的人工投入，且缺乏标准方法在RTL代码中定位故障位置，亟需自动化且易于使用的安全验证方法。

Method: CWEEP是一种静态分析框架，不依赖详细的安全规格说明即可检测RTL中的安全弱点。它能够在早期RTL开发阶段（属性仍在构建中）使用，并能精确定位潜在漏洞在RTL代码中的位置，同时在适用时提供自动代码修复建议。

Result: 在两个带有手动插入漏洞的SoC设计以及一个由大型语言模型生成、包含3874个缺陷模块的数据集上评估，CWEEP的正确警告率高达60.8%，而先前工作的工具在同一数据集上的正确警告率仅为17.5%。

Conclusion: CWEEP显著优于先前的工作，在准确率上有大幅提升（60.8%对比17.5%），证明了其作为早期RTL开发阶段硬件安全验证工具的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: As the hardware layer becomes a focus point for attackers, the need for improved hardware security verification techniques is more important than ever. State-of-the-art security verification techniques require significant manual effort from individuals with security expertise. Furthermore, there is no standard method to locate where the fault lies within the register transfer level (RTL) code. This paper presents CWEEP, a static analysis framework for detecting security weaknesses in RTL. CWEEP does not require a detailed security specification, so it can be used in the early stages of RTL development while properties are still under construction. Furthermore, CWEEP can identify the exact location in the RTL where the potential vulnerability resides and supports automatic code repair suggestions when applicable. Using datasets from the literature, we evaluate the performance of CWEEP on a set of two SoC designs with manually inserted bugs and on a large language model generated dataset, consisting of 3874 buggy modules. We find that CWEEP issues a correct warning up to 60.8% of the time. In contrast, the tool from a previous work issued a correct warning 17.5% of the time for the same dataset.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Improving scDiffusion with Sparsity-Biased Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29043)
*Yu Song,Hao Sun,Ikuko Nishikawa,Yen-Wei Chen*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏偏置的无分类器引导（SB-CFG）策略，用于单细胞RNA测序数据的扩散模型生成。与标准CFG依赖近似真实边缘分布的无条件分支不同，SB-CFG引入一个故意欠信息量的稀疏参考作为无条件分支，去除基因身份而仅保留粗略稀疏统计，从而扩大条件与无条件预测之间的对比，实现更强的引导效果。在五个公开数据集上，SB-CFG作为无需训练的采样修改方案，在标记基因表达保真度、细胞类型一致性和稀疏性保持方面均优于标准CFG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在条件scRNA-seq生成中的引导策略（包括分类器引导和CFG）依赖于训练一个近似真实边缘分布的无条件分支，但该分支可能保留大量基因特异性结构，限制了引导的有效性。

Method: 提出SB-CFG策略：不再近似"中性"边缘分布，而是用故意欠信息量的稀疏参考作为无条件分支，去除基因身份信息仅保留粗略稀疏统计，通过"坏"参考扩大条件与无条件预测的对比，增强采样时的引导强度。该方法作为训练免费的采样修改，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在五个公开scRNA-seq数据集上的实验表明，SB-CFG相比标准CFG采样在标记基因表达保真度、细胞类型一致性和稀疏性保持方面均取得一致性改进，表明其能更好地捕捉生物学上有意义的基因表达模式。

Conclusion: SB-CFG通过引入引导性退化的稀疏参考而非假定的中性边缘分布，有效增强了扩散模型在scRNA-seq条件生成中的引导性能，是一种简单而有效的训练免费改进方案。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) has become an essential tool in modern cellular biology, and generating accurate synthetic scRNA-seq data is becoming increasingly important. Although diffusion models have achieved promising results in conditional scRNA-seq generation, existing guidance strategies, including classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance (CFG), rely on an unconditional branch trained to approximate the true marginal distribution, which may retain substantial gene-specific structure and limit guidance effectiveness. Inspired by recent work showing that diffusion models can be effectively guided using intentionally degraded references, we propose a sparsity-biased classifier-free guidance (SB-CFG) strategy for scRNA-seq generation. Rather than approximating the assumed "neutral" marginal distribution, SB-CFG introduces a deliberately under-informative sparse reference for the unconditional branch, removing gene identity while preserving only coarse sparsity statistics. This "bad" reference amplifies the contrast between conditional and unconditional predictions, leading to stronger and more effective guidance during sampling. We evaluated SB-CFG as a training-free sampling modification on five publicly available scRNA-seq datasets. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements over standard CFG-based sampling in terms of marker gene expression fidelity, cell-type consistency, and sparsity preservation, indicating that SB-CFG better captures biologically meaningful gene expression patterns.

</details>
